## 560912
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なんだって？<line-break>それじゃ親父、ギルドのくせに、<line-break>冒険者に仕事を譲れねえってのか？<end>"
EN: "What? You run a Guild, old man, and you won't give an adventurer the job?<end>"
KO: "뭐라고? 그럼 아저씨, 길드면서 모험자에게 일을 양보 못 하겠다는 거야?<end>"

## 560913
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そんなにはっきり言ってほしいか？<line-break>お前さんごときの力じゃ、<line-break>この仕事には力不足だってんだよ。<end>"
EN: "You really want me to spell it out? Someone of your ability isn't up to this job.<end>"
KO: "그렇게까지 확실히 말해 줘야겠어? 너 정도 실력으론 이 일에 역부족이라고.<end>"

## 560914
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "な、な、なんだと！<line-break>詳しい話を聞かせもしねえで…<line-break>冒険者を値踏みしようってのか！<end>"
EN: "W-What did you say? You haven't even told me what the job is, and you think you can judge me as an adventurer?<end>"
KO: "뭐, 뭐라고! 자세한 얘기도 들려주지 않고… 모험가를 평가하겠다는 거냐!<end>"

## 560915
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いや、そうではないのだ…。<line-break>これは、あなたのために<line-break>言っておるのですぞ、お若いの。<line-break>その命、無駄に散らしてしまうのは<line-break>もったいない。<end>"
EN: "No, that is not it. I say this for your own sake, young one. It would be a pity to see you throw your life away.<end>"
KO: "아니, 그런 게 아닙니다…. 이건 당신을 위해 하는 말이에요, 젊은이. 그 목숨을 허무하게 버리기엔 아깝습니다.<end>"

## 560916
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうだぜ…っと。<end>"
EN: "That's right... Well, now.<end>"
KO: "그렇다니까… 이거지.<end>"

## 560917
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おお！<line-break>こいつはいいところに。<line-break>あんた、<value:$28>じゃねえか！<end>"
EN: "Oh! Talk about perfect timing. You're <value:$28>, aren't you?<end>"
KO: "오! 마침 잘 왔군. 당신, <value:$28> 아니야!<end>"

## 560918
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "追加メッセージここから<end>"
EN: ""
KO: "추가 메시지 여기부터<end>"

## 560919
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "神官さん、こいつはめぐり<line-break>合わせかもしれねえ。<line-break> <line-break><value:$28>…あんたに、<line-break>ぜひお願いしたい仕事があるんだ。<end>"
EN: "Priest, this might be fate at work. <value:$28>, there's a job I'd very much like you to take.<end>"
KO: "신관님, 이건 인연일지도 모르겠군. <value:$28>… 자네에게 꼭 부탁하고 싶은 일이 있어.<end>"

## 560920
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<if><value:$2A><greater-equal>50<value:$28>って、<line-break>あの<value:$2B>の、<line-break><value:$28>かよ！<end>な、なんだって？<line-break>まさかあんた…<line-break>あの<value:$28>かよ！<end>"
EN: "<if><value:$2A><greater-equal>50<value:$28>? You mean <value:$28>, the <value:$2B>?<end>W-What? Don't tell me you're <value:$28>!<end>"
KO: "<if><value:$2A><greater-equal>50<value:$28>라니, 그 <value:$2B>의,<value:$28>라고!?<end>뭐, 뭐라고? 설마 당신…그 <value:$28>라고!?<end>"

## 560921
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "こ、こいつは俺の出番じゃねえ。<line-break>親父、じゃあな！<end>"
EN: "Th-This is out of my league. See you around, old man!<end>"
KO: "이, 이건 내 차례가 아니네. 아버지, 그럼 안녕!<end>"

## 560922
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ま、聞いてのとおりだ。<line-break> <line-break> <line-break>生半可な冒険者には頼めない仕事が<line-break>あるんだが…<line-break>聞いてはくれないかね？<end>"
EN: "Well, you heard all that. We've got a job we can't entrust to just any adventurer. Will you at least hear us out?<end>"
KO: "뭐, 들은 그대로다. 어설픈 모험자에겐 맡길 수 없는 일이 있는데… 들어 주겠나?<end>"

## 560923
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "やめておく<end>"
EN: "Decline<end>"
KO: "그만둔다<end>"

## 560924
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part98.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "話を聞く<end>"
EN: "Hear about the job<end>"
KO: "이야기를 듣는다<end>"

## 560925
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうですか。<line-break>あなたのように力量のある冒険者に<line-break>頼みたかったのですが…。<end>"
EN: "I see. I had hoped to entrust it to an adventurer of your caliber.<end>"
KO: "그렇습니까. 당신처럼 실력 있는 모험자에게 부탁하고 싶었습니다만….<end>"

## 560926
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "仕方ねえさ、神官さん。<line-break>依頼を受けるかどうか決めるのは、<line-break>命をかける冒険者だからな。<line-break><value:$28>、この話、<line-break>聞かなかったことにしてくれ。<end>"
EN: "That's how it goes, priest. Adventurers are the ones risking their lives, so the choice is theirs. <value:$28>, forget you ever heard about this.<end>"
KO: "어쩔 수 없지, 신관님. 의뢰를 받을지 말지 결정하는 건 목숨을 거는 모험자니까. <value:$28>, 이 얘긴 못 들은 걸로 해줘.<end>"

## 560927
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ま、気長に待ちましょうや。<line-break>次に、見込みがあるのが来たら、<line-break>こっちからお知らせしますから。<end>"
EN: "We'll just have to be patient. If another promising prospect comes along, I'll let you know.<end>"
KO: "뭐, 느긋하게 기다리자고. 다음에 가능성 있는 녀석이 오면 이쪽에서 알려 줄 테니까.<end>"

## 560928
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "よし来た！<line-break>あんたならきっと<line-break>そう言ってくれると思ってたよ。<line-break>よかったな、神官さん。<end>"
EN: "That's the spirit! I knew you'd say that. Good news, priest.<end>"
KO: "좋아! 당신이라면 분명 그렇게 말해 줄 줄 알았어. 잘됐네요, 신관님.<end>"

## 560929
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "紹介するぜ。<line-break> <line-break> <line-break>こちら、アルノートゥンの神殿に<line-break>仕えてらっしゃる神官さんだ。<end>"
EN: "Let me introduce you. This priest serves at the temple in Alnortun.<end>"
KO: "소개하지. 이쪽은 알노툰 신전에서 일하는 신관님이야.<end>"

## 560930
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "よろしくお願いします。<line-break>ご主人がそこまで推される方でしたら、<line-break>安心してお話ができます。<end>"
EN: "It is a pleasure to meet you. If the guildmaster recommends you so highly, I can speak to you without reservation.<end>"
KO: "잘 부탁드립니다. 주인께서 그렇게까지 추천하신 분이라면 안심하고 말씀드릴 수 있겠군요.<end>"

## 560931
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "依頼ですが…<line-break> <line-break> <line-break>アルノートゥンの北東に眠る、<line-break>聖光石の坑道を<line-break>調べていただきたいというものです。<end>"
EN: "As for the request, we would like you to investigate the Holy Light Stone Mine, which lies dormant northeast of Alnortun.<end>"
KO: "의뢰 내용은… 알노툰 북동쪽에 잠든 성광석 갱도를 조사해 달라는 거다.<end>"

## 560932
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "確かに、山の管理を神殿で<line-break>行っているなど、疑問に思われるのも<line-break>無理からぬことです。<line-break>実は、あの山には、<line-break>易々と語ってはならぬ<line-break>秘密があるのです。<end>"
EN: "You would be quite justified in wondering why the temple administers the mountain. The truth is, that mountain holds a secret not to be spoken of lightly.<end>"
KO: "확실히 산의 관리를 신전에서 하고 있다는 점을 의문스럽게 여기는 것도 무리는 아닙니다. 실은 그 산에는 쉽게 말해서는 안 될 비밀이 있습니다.<end>"

## 560933
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それが、ディンガル軍が攻め入ってから<line-break>坑道で不気味な地鳴りが<line-break>聞かれるようになった…。<end>"
EN: "Ever since the Dyneskal Army invaded, an ominous rumbling has been heard in the mine.<end>"
KO: "그게, 딘갈군이 쳐들어온 뒤부터 갱도에서 불길한 땅울림이 들리기 시작했습니다….<end>"

## 560934
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "私も視察に行ったのですが、<line-break>鉱夫ではない身ながら、<line-break> <line-break>あれはどうも<line-break>ただの山鳴りではないように<line-break>思われました。<end>"
EN: "I went to inspect it myself. I am no miner, but it did not sound like an ordinary mountain rumble.<end>"
KO: "저도 시찰하러 갔는데 광부는 아니지만, 아무래도 그건 단순한 산울림은 아닌 것 같았습니다.<end>"

## 560935
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そこで、よくない結果を招くより早く、<line-break>屈強な方に真相を確かめてきて欲しいと<line-break>考えたのです。<line-break>行って何もなければ、それでよい。<line-break>それでも報酬は支払いましょう。<line-break>なにとぞ、よろしくお願いします。<end>"
EN: "Before this can lead to disaster, I want someone capable to uncover the truth. If you find nothing, so much the better. You will still be paid. Please, I ask that you undertake this task.<end>"
KO: "그래서 좋지 않은 결과가 생기기 전에 강한 분께 진상을 확인해 달라고 부탁드리려는 겁니다. 가서 아무 일도 없다면 그걸로 좋습니다. 그래도 보상은 지급하겠습니다. 부디 부탁드립니다.<end>"

## 560936
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "聖光石の坑道は今では完全に封鎖し、<line-break>誰をも入れぬようになっています。<line-break> <line-break>機密を厳守するため、<line-break>機会は一度きりとさせていただきたい。<end>"
EN: "The Holy Light Stone Mine is now completely sealed, and no one is permitted to enter. To ensure absolute secrecy, we can allow you only one attempt.<end>"
KO: "성광석 갱도는 지금 완전히 봉쇄해 누구도 들어갈 수 없습니다. 기밀을 엄수하기 위해 기회는 한 번뿐으로 하겠습니다.<end>"

## 560937
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "この条件をのんでいただけないのなら、<line-break>今でも遅くはありません。<line-break>ご辞退願いたい。<line-break>お覚悟は、よろしいですか？<end>"
EN: "If you cannot accept that condition, it is not too late to withdraw. I ask that you do so. Are you prepared to proceed?<end>"
KO: "이 조건을 받아들이지 못하시겠다면 지금이라도 늦지 않았습니다. 사양해 주십시오. 각오는 되셨습니까?<end>"

## 560938
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part98.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "まかせて<end>"
EN: "Leave it to me<end>"
KO: "맡겨<end>"

## 560939
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "やっぱりやめる<end>"
EN: "I've changed my mind<end>"
KO: "역시 그만둔다<end>"

## 560940
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "追加メッセージここから<end>"
EN: ""
KO: "추가 메시지 여기부터<end>"

## 560941
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ありがたいです、<value:$28>殿。   <line-break>では、坑道の入り口までは<line-break>私がお送りしましょう。<line-break>そこから、頼りはあなた方のお力のみ。<line-break>どうかくれぐれもお気をつけ<line-break>なさるよう……。<end>"
EN: "You have my gratitude, <value:$28>. I shall escort you to the mine entrance. From there on, you will have only your own strength to rely upon. Please, take the utmost care.<end>"
KO: "감사합니다, <value:$28> 님. 그럼 갱도 입구까지는 제가 안내하겠습니다. 그 뒤로는 여러분의 힘만이 의지할 전부입니다. 부디 각별히 조심하시길….<end>"

## 560942
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それじゃ、頼んだぜ。<end>"
EN: "All right, we're counting on you.<end>"
KO: "그럼 부탁한다.<end>"

## 560943
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "左様ですか…。<line-break>仕方がない、<line-break>他に頼れる方を探すとします。<end>"
EN: "Is that your decision? Very well. I shall seek someone else who can help us.<end>"
KO: "그렇습니까…. 어쩔 수 없군요. 다른 부탁할 분을 찾아보겠습니다.<end>"

## 560944
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "仕方ねえさ、神官さん。<line-break>依頼を受けるかどうか決めるのは、<line-break>命をかける冒険者だからな。<line-break><value:$28>、この話、<line-break>聞かなかったことにしてくれ。<end>"
EN: "That's how it goes, priest. Adventurers are the ones risking their lives, so the choice is theirs. <value:$28>, forget you ever heard about this.<end>"
KO: "어쩔 수 없지, 신관님. 의뢰를 받을지 말지 결정하는 건 목숨을 거는 모험자니까. <value:$28>, 이 얘긴 못 들은 걸로 해줘.<end>"

## 560945
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ま、気長に待ちましょうや。<line-break>次、見込みがあるのが来たら、<line-break>こっちからお知らせしますから。<end>"
EN: "We'll just have to be patient. If another promising prospect comes along, I'll let you know.<end>"
KO: "뭐, 느긋하게 기다리죠. 다음에 가망 있어 보이는 사람이 오면 이쪽에서 알려 드릴 테니까.<end>"

## 560946
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フ…私自身の話など<line-break>面白いとも思わないが、<line-break>君が聞きたいと言うなら、少し話そう。<end>"
EN: "Hmph. I doubt there is anything interesting about my life, but if you wish to hear it, I will tell you a little.<end>"
KO: "후… 내 이야기가 재미있다고는 생각하지 않지만, 네가 듣고 싶다면 조금 이야기해 주지.<end>"

## 560947
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "私を生んだ両親は、<line-break>ディンガル家の一族。しかし、<line-break>その地位は低く、生活は貧しかった。<end>"
EN: "My birth parents belonged to the Dyneskal family, but their standing was low and they lived in poverty.<end>"
KO: "나를 낳은 부모는 딩갈 가문의 일족이었다. 하지만 지위는 낮았고 생활은 가난했다.<end>"

## 560948
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "話はつけてきたのか？<line-break>早く頼むよ。<end>"
EN: "Did you get it sorted out? Hurry up, will you?<end>"
KO: "이야기는 끝냈나? 빨리 부탁해.<end>"

## 560949
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フゴーと面識があるとは、<line-break>大した冒険者だ。<end>"
EN: "You know Hugo personally? You must be quite the adventurer.<end>"
KO: "휴고와 아는 사이라니, 대단한 모험자군.<end>"

## 560950
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "カルラ様もさすがに<line-break>フゴーには手を出せんよ。<line-break>金の力はすごいな。<end>"
EN: "Even Lady Karula wouldn't dare touch Hugo. Money is a powerful thing.<end>"
KO: "칼라 님도 아무리 그래도 휴고에게는 손대지 못해. 돈의 힘은 대단하네.<end>"

## 560951
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "クリュセイス様は、<line-break>今はいらっしゃらない。<line-break>日をあらためて、また来てくれ。<end>"
EN: "Lady Khryuseis isn't here at present. Come back another day.<end>"
KO: "크류세이스 님은 지금 계시지 않는다. 날을 바꿔 다시 와라.<end>"

## 560952
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ノエル・ボリューム１その２終端<end>"
EN: ""
KO: "노엘 볼륨1 그 2 끝<end>"

## 570000
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec057-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ルルアンタ・ボリューム１その２開始<end>"
EN: ""
KO: "루루안타 볼륨1 그 2 시작<end>"

## 570001
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec057-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ここは…。<line-break>いや、ここがそうなのか…。<end>"
EN: "This is... No, so this is the place...<end>"
KO: "여긴…. 아니, 여기가 그곳인가….<end>"

## 570002
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec057-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ええ、お察しのとおりです。<line-break>ようこそ、猫屋敷へ。<end>"
EN: "Yes, just as you suspected. Welcome to Cat Manor.<end>"
KO: "네, 짐작하신 대로입니다. 고양이 저택에 오신 걸 환영합니다.<end>"

## 570003
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec057-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "……？　あなたは…。<line-break>なるほど。どうりでこの屋敷の結界を<line-break>通り抜けられるわけですね。<end>"
EN: "...? You... Ah, of course. That explains how you passed through the barrier around the manor.<end>"
KO: "……? 당신은…. 그렇군요. 그래서 이 저택의 결계를 통과할 수 있었군요.<end>"

## 570004
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec057-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "はじめまして。<line-break>私はオルファウス。<line-break>この屋敷の主です。<end>"
EN: "A pleasure to meet you. I am Orphaus, master of this manor.<end>"
KO: "처음 뵙겠습니다. 저는 오르파우스. 이 저택의 주인입니다.<end>"

## 570005
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec057-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "俺はゼネテス。<line-break>そしてこっちは<value:$28>。<line-break>　　　　　　　　　　　　　　　　　<line-break>いきなりで悪いが<line-break>俺たちは人を探している。<line-break>ルルアンタというリルビーの少女だ。<end>"
EN: "I'm Xenetes, and this is <value:$28>. Sorry to spring this on you, but we're looking for someone―a young Lilubee named Lulluantha.<end>"
KO: "나는 제네테스. 그리고 이쪽은 <value:$28>. 갑자기 미안하지만 우리는 사람을 찾고 있어. 루루안타라는 릴비 소녀야.<end>"

## 570006
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec057-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ルルアンタさん…ですか？<end>"
EN: "Lulluantha...?<end>"
KO: "루루안타 씨…인가요?<end>"

## 570007
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec057-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ああ、盗賊にさらわれちまってな。<line-break>この森にいるらしいんだが<line-break>見なかったかい？<end>"
EN: "Yeah. Bandits carried her off. We heard she was somewhere in this forest. Have you seen her?<end>"
KO: "그래, 도적에게 납치됐어. 이 숲에 있는 모양인데 못 봤어?<end>"

## 570008
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec057-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ああ、あの子ですか。<line-break>ええ、お会いしましたよ。　<line-break>ですが、今どこにいるのかは<line-break>私にもわかりません。<end>"
EN: "Ah, that girl. Yes, I met her. But I'm afraid I don't know where she is now.<end>"
KO: "아, 그 아이 말인가요. 네, 만났습니다. 하지만 지금 어디 있는지는 저도 모릅니다.<end>"

## 570009
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec057-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そんなにあせらないでください。<line-break>どうです？　ここで立ち話もなんですし<line-break>中でお話ししませんか？<end>"
EN: "Please, don't be so hasty. Rather than stand here talking, why don't we go inside?<end>"
KO: "그렇게 초조해하지 마세요. 어때요? 여기서 서서 이야기하기도 그러니 안에서 이야기할까요?<end>"

## 570010
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec057-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ん、ああ。お前さんの気持ちはわかる。<line-break>だが、今あせることが<line-break>いい結果を生むとは限らない。<line-break>ここが猫屋敷ということは<line-break>あいつは猫屋敷の賢者様だ。<line-break>きっと何か、道を示してくれるさ。<end>"
EN: "Yeah, I know how you feel. But rushing off now won't necessarily help. This is Cat Manor, so that must be the Sage of Cat Manor. I'm sure the sage can point us in the right direction.<end>"
KO: "응, 그래. 네 마음은 이해해. 하지만 지금 서두른다고 좋은 결과가 나온다는 보장은 없어. 여기가 고양이 저택이라면 저 사람은 고양이 저택의 현자님이겠지. 분명 뭔가 길을 보여 주실 거야.<end>"

## 570011
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec057-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "というわけで、盗賊からは助けました。<line-break>ですが、彼女は私に短く礼を言ったあと<line-break>またどこかへと去っていきました。<line-break>とてもさびしそうな顔をしていました。<line-break>……すいません、私が知っているのは<line-break>これくらいのものです。<end>"
EN: "So, I rescued her from the bandits. But after briefly thanking me, she went off somewhere again. She looked terribly lonely. I'm sorry, but that is all I know.<end>"
KO: "그래서 도적에게서는 구해 냈습니다. 하지만 그녀는 짧게 감사 인사를 한 뒤 다시 어디론가 떠났습니다. 아주 쓸쓸한 표정이었어요. …죄송합니다. 제가 아는 건 이 정도뿐입니다.<end>"

## 570012
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec057-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…そうか。<line-break>いや、無事ならいいんだ。<line-break>助けてくれてありがとう。<end>"
EN: "I see... No, as long as she's safe, that's what matters. Thanks for helping her.<end>"
KO: "…그런가. 아니, 무사하면 됐어. 구해 줘서 고마워.<end>"

## 570013
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec057-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いえいえ。<end>"
EN: "Think nothing of it.<end>"
KO: "아니에요.<end>"

## 570014
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec057-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そういえばさっき、<line-break>何か話そうとしてたな。<line-break>　<line-break>なんだい？<line-break>俺たちにできる話なら<line-break>相談に乗るぜ。<end>"
EN: "Come to think of it, you were about to say something earlier. What is it? If there's anything we can do, let's hear it.<end>"
KO: "그러고 보니 아까 뭔가 말하려 했지. 무슨 일이지? 우리가 도울 수 있는 이야기라면 상담해 줄게.<end>"

## 570015
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec057-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ええ。<line-break>実はお願いしたい事があります。<end>"
EN: "Yes. There is something I would like to ask of you.<end>"
KO: "네. 사실 부탁드릴 일이 있습니다.<end>"

## 570016
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec057-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "例のミイス村が焼き払われたことと<line-break>関係があるのかい。<end>"
EN: "Does it have something to do with Miis Village being burned to the ground?<end>"
KO: "그 미이스 마을이 불타 버린 일과 관계가 있나?<end>"

## 570017
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec057-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さすがは、剣狼のゼネテスですね。<line-break>噂通りの冒険者だ。耳が早い。<line-break>　<line-break>そうです。<line-break>ことはミイスを焼き払った魔人<line-break>アーギルシャイアに関わります。<end>"
EN: "Just what I'd expect from Xenetes the Sword Wolf. You're every bit the adventurer they say you are, and you have a keen ear for news. Yes. This concerns Argyleshire, the demon who burned Miis.<end>"
KO: "과연 검랑 제네테스군요. 소문대로의 모험자입니다. 귀가 빠르군요. 그렇습니다. 미이스를 불태운 마인 아르길샤이어와 관련된 일입니다.<end>"

## 570018
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec057-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "アーギルシャイア…？<end>"
EN: "Argyleshire...?<end>"
KO: "아르길샤이어…?<end>"

## 570019
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec057-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "破壊神ウルグの円卓騎士と呼ばれる　<line-break>強力な魔人の１人です。<line-break>　<line-break>妖艶な美女で、残酷で気まぐれです。<line-break>闇の神器をひとつ手に入れるために<line-break>ミイスを焼き払ってしまいました。<end>"
EN: "She is one of the powerful demons known as the Knights of the Round Table of Ulugh, the God of Destruction. A bewitching beauty, she is cruel and capricious. She burned Miis to the ground merely to obtain one of the Dark Artifacts.<end>"
KO: "파괴신 울그의 원탁기사라 불리는 강력한 마인 중 한 명입니다. 요염한 미녀이며 잔혹하고 변덕스럽습니다. 어둠의 신기 하나를 얻기 위해 미이스를 불태웠습니다.<end>"

## 570020
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec057-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "闇の神器。強大で凶々しい力を持つ<line-break>１２の魔道器だ。かつて破壊神ウルグの<line-break>復活に使われたことがあるという。<end>"
EN: "The Dark Artifacts. Twelve magical relics of immense and baleful power. They are said to have once been used to resurrect Ulugh, the God of Destruction.<end>"
KO: "어둠의 신기. 강대하고 불길한 힘을 지닌 열두 개의 마도기다. 과거 파괴신 울그의 부활에 사용된 적이 있다고 한다.<end>"

## 570021
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec057-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "正解です。よく、ご存じですね。<line-break>ただ、彼女はその本来の使い方<line-break>以外のことをしようとしています。<line-break>彼女は究極の合成怪物を作り<line-break>その力でおもしろ半分に世界を<line-break>破壊しようと考えているのです。<end>"
EN: "Correct. You know a great deal about them. But she intends to use one for something other than its original purpose. She means to create the ultimate chimeric monster, then use its power to destroy the world for her own amusement.<end>"
KO: "정답입니다. 잘 알고 계시는군요. 다만 그녀는 본래의 용도 외의 일을 하려 합니다. 궁극의 합성 괴물을 만들어 그 힘으로 재미 삼아 세계를 파괴하려는 겁니다.<end>"

## 570022
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec057-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "その合成怪物を作るための知識を<line-break>闇の神器のひとつ、禁断の聖杯から<line-break>得ようとしているのです。<end>"
EN: "She hopes to obtain the knowledge needed to create that monster from the Forbidden Grail, one of the Dark Artifacts.<end>"
KO: "그 합성괴물을 만들 지식을 어둠의 신기 중 하나인 금단의 성배에서 얻으려 하고 있습니다.<end>"

## 570023
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec057-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なるほど、ね。その禁断の聖杯が<line-break>アーギルシャイアの手に落ちる前に<line-break>俺たちに取ってきてほしいってわけか。<line-break>だが、聖杯って名前の魔道器が<line-break>盗み出される事件がロストールで<line-break>最近あったと思ったがな。<end>"
EN: "I get it. You want us to retrieve the Forbidden Grail before Argyleshire gets hold of it. But wasn't a magical relic called a Grail stolen in Rostorl recently?<end>"
KO: "그렇군. 그 금단의 성배가 아르길샤이어 손에 들어가기 전에 우리가 가져와 달라는 거군. 그런데 성배라는 이름의 마도기가 도난당한 사건이 최근 로스톨에서 있었던 것 같은데.<end>"

## 570024
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec057-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "もし、それが禁断の聖杯だったら、<line-break>もう、聖杯はアーギルシャイアの<line-break>手に渡ってるんじゃないか。<end>"
EN: "If that was the Forbidden Grail, wouldn't it already be in Argyleshire's hands?<end>"
KO: "만약 그게 금단의 성배였다면 이미 성배는 아르길샤이어 손에 넘어간 거 아닐까.<end>"

## 570025
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec057-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さすがに鋭いですね。その通り。<line-break>その聖杯こそ禁断の聖杯です。<line-break>　<line-break>ですが、それを盗み出させたのは<line-break>魔人アーギルシャイアではない<line-break>別の魔人なのです。<end>"
EN: "Very perceptive. That's right: that Grail is the Forbidden Grail. But it wasn't Argyleshire who had it stolen. It was another demon.<end>"
KO: "역시 날카롭군요. 맞습니다. 그 성배가 바로 금단의 성배입니다. 하지만 그걸 훔치게 한 건 마인 아르길샤이어가 아니라 다른 마인입니다.<end>"

## 570026
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec057-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それは？<end>"
EN: "Who?<end>"
KO: "그건?<end>"

## 570027
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec057-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "こいつですよ。<end>"
EN: "This fellow.<end>"
KO: "이 녀석이에요.<end>"

## 570028
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec057-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "やめろ、こら！<line-break>俺をつまむんじゃねぇ！<end>"
EN: "Hey, stop that! Don't pick me up!<end>"
KO: "그만둬, 이 자식아! 날 집어 들지 마!<end>"

## 570029
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec057-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なんだ、そいつ？<end>"
EN: "What's that thing?<end>"
KO: "뭐야, 저 녀석?<end>"

## 570030
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec057-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なんだとはごあいさつだな。<line-break>俺の名は…<end>"
EN: "“That thing”? That's a fine way to greet someone. My name is...<end>"
KO: "뭐냐니 너무하네. 내 이름은…<end>"

## 570031
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec057-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "破壊神の円卓騎士ネモです。<line-break>勇者ネメアに倒されてからは<line-break>猫の姿に封印されているのです。　　<line-break>もともと聖杯は彼のものなのです。<line-break>アーギルシャイアの企みを知った彼は<line-break>自分の神器を奪われたくないと考え、<end>"
EN: "This is Nemo, a Knight of the Round Table of the God of Destruction. Ever since the Hero Nemea defeated him, he has been sealed in the form of a cat. The Grail originally belonged to him. When he learned of Argyleshire's scheme, he decided he could not allow his Artifact to be taken, so...<end>"
KO: "파괴신의 원탁기사 네모입니다. 용사 네메아에게 쓰러진 뒤 고양이 모습으로 봉인되어 있죠. 원래 성배는 그의 것입니다. 아르길샤이어의 계략을 안 그는 자신의 신기를 빼앗기고 싶지 않아 했고….<end>"

## 570032
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec057-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "この際、神器の力でもとの姿に戻ろうと<line-break>３匹のゴブリンたちを使って神器を<line-break>ロストールから盗み出させたのです。<end>"
EN: "He had three Goblins steal the Artifact from Rostorl, intending to use its power to restore his true form while he had the chance.<end>"
KO: "이참에 신기의 힘으로 원래 모습으로 돌아가려고 세 고블린을 이용해 신기를 로스톨에서 훔쳐 오게 한 겁니다.<end>"

## 570033
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec057-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "だが、こいつは猫のままだが？<end>"
EN: "But he's still a cat, isn't he?<end>"
KO: "하지만 이 녀석은 여전히 고양이인데?<end>"

## 570034
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec057-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "禁断の聖杯は持ち主に英知を与えます。<line-break>知恵を身につけたゴブリンたちは<line-break>ネモを見限ってしまったのです。<end>"
EN: "The Forbidden Grail grants wisdom to its bearer. Once the Goblins gained that wisdom, they abandoned Nemo.<end>"
KO: "금단의 성배는 소유자에게 지혜를 줍니다. 지혜를 얻은 고블린들은 네모를 저버리고 말았습니다.<end>"

## 570035
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec057-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ま、おかげで、ロストールは<line-break>魔人に焼き払われずにすんだわけだ。<line-break>にしても、間抜けな魔人だな。<end>"
EN: "Well, at least Rostorl was spared being burned down by a demon. Still, what a half-witted demon.<end>"
KO: "덕분에 로스톨은 마인에게 불타지 않고 끝났군. 그나저나 얼빠진 마인이야.<end>"

## 570036
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec057-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なんだと、テメー！<line-break>上等だ、勝負して…<end>"
EN: "What did you call me, you bastard? Fine, let's settle this...<end>"
KO: "뭐라고, 이 자식아! 좋아, 승부해…<end>"

## 570037
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec057-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "しかし、アーギルシャイアは、<line-break>今も聖杯を手に入れようと<line-break>ねらっているはずです。<line-break>ミイスの時のように<line-break>聖杯を持ってるゴブリンがいる町ごと<line-break>焼き払ってしまうかもしれないのです。<end>"
EN: "But Argyleshire must still be after the Grail. As she did at Miis, she may burn down the entire town where the Goblins carrying it are staying.<end>"
KO: "하지만 아르길샤이어는 지금도 성배를 노리고 있을 겁니다. 미이스 때처럼 성배를 가진 고블린이 있는 마을째로 불태워 버릴지도 모릅니다.<end>"

## 570038
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec057-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "急がないとな。<line-break><value:$28>。<end>"
EN: "We'd better hurry, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "서둘러야겠군. <value:$28>.<end>"

## 570039
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec057-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ゴブリンは聖杯の力で言葉を話し、<line-break>ゴブゴブ団を名乗っています。<line-break>　<line-break>あと、ネモの話だと<line-break>エンシャントにいるという話でしたが<line-break>急がないと、移動してしまうでしょう。<end>"
EN: "The Grail's power has enabled the Goblins to speak, and they call themselves the Gob-Gob Gang. According to Nemo, they're in Ancient, but if you don't hurry, they may move on.<end>"
KO: "고블린은 성배의 힘으로 말을 하며 고브고브단이라고 자칭하고 있습니다. 그리고 네모의 말로는 엔샨트에 있다고 했지만, 서두르지 않으면 이동해 버릴 겁니다.<end>"

## 570040
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec057-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "んじゃ、行くか、<line-break><value:$28>。<end>"
EN: "Then let's go, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "그럼 갈까, <value:$28>.<end>"

## 570041
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec057-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あ、その前に<line-break>お見せしたいものがあります。<line-break>こちらに来てください。<end>"
EN: "Ah, before you leave, there is something I would like to show you. This way, please.<end>"
KO: "아, 그 전에 보여드리고 싶은 것이 있습니다. 이쪽으로 와주세요.<end>"

## 570042
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec057-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "これは？<end>"
EN: "What's this?<end>"
KO: "이건?<end>"

## 570043
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec057-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "これは転送機というんです。<line-break>使ってみましょうか。<end>"
EN: "It is called a teleportation device. Shall we try it?<end>"
KO: "이건 전송기라고 해요. 한번 써 볼까요?<end>"

## 570044
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec057-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "何しやがる！<line-break>俺を実験台にするな！<end>"
EN: "What the hell are you doing? Don't use me as a test subject!<end>"
KO: "뭐 하는 거야! 날 실험대로 쓰지 마!<end>"

## 570045
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec057-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ほう。<line-break>こりゃあ、すげぇな。<end>"
EN: "Now that's impressive.<end>"
KO: "호오. 이거 대단한데.<end>"

## 570046
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec057-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ま、こんな感じで、<line-break>これは遠くの人を呼び寄せたり…。<line-break>さて、それでは、もう一度。<end>"
EN: "As you see, it can summon someone from far away... Now, let us try that once more.<end>"
KO: "뭐, 이런 식으로 멀리 있는 사람을 불러오기도 하고…. 자, 그럼 다시 한번.<end>"

## 570047
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec057-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "こら！<line-break>だから、俺を実験台に…<end>"
EN: "Hey! I said, stop using me as a test subject...<end>"
KO: "이봐! 그러니까 날 실험대로 삼지 말라고…<end>"

## 570048
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec057-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "こんな感じで、ここにいる人を<line-break>遠くに送り込んだりできます。<end>"
EN: "Like this, it can also send someone from here to somewhere far away.<end>"
KO: "이런 식으로 여기 있는 사람을 먼 곳으로 보내기도 할 수 있습니다.<end>"

## 570049
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec057-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なるほどな。<end>"
EN: "Got it.<end>"
KO: "그렇군.<end>"

## 570050
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec057-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あなたが将来、旅で人と出会い<line-break>仲間を増やしたとき、<line-break>　<line-break>一緒に旅する仲間を<line-break>変更をしたいと思ったとき<line-break>ここに来てください。<end>"
EN: "As you meet people on your travels and gain more companions, come here whenever you wish to change who travels with you.<end>"
KO: "앞으로 여행 중 사람들을 만나 동료가 늘고, 함께 여행할 동료를 바꾸고 싶을 때 여기로 오세요.<end>"

## 570051
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec057-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あ、今後は、パーティーメンバーの<line-break>了解を取ってから呼び出しますから<line-break>そのあたりのことはご心配なく。<end>"
EN: "Oh, and from now on, I will obtain your party members' permission before summoning them, so there is no need to worry about that.<end>"
KO: "아, 앞으로는 파티 멤버의 허락을 받은 뒤 불러낼 테니 그 부분은 걱정하지 않으셔도 됩니다.<end>"

## 570052
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec057-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あんたには<line-break>色々世話になりそうだな。<end>"
EN: "Looks like we'll be relying on you for quite a few things.<end>"
KO: "너한테는 여러모로 신세를 지게 될 것 같군.<end>"

## 570053
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec057-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いえいえ。私もあなたたちに一方的に<line-break>お願いしてしまっていますからね。<line-break>これくらいは当然です。<line-break>他に何か、聞きたいことがあれば、<line-break>私にできる範囲でお答えしますよ。<end>"
EN: "Not at all. I have made a rather one-sided request of you, after all. This is the least I can do. If there is anything else you would like to know, I will answer as best I can.<end>"
KO: "아니에요. 저도 일방적으로 부탁드리고 있으니까요. 이 정도는 당연합니다. 다른 궁금한 게 있으면 제가 답할 수 있는 범위에서 알려 드릴게요.<end>"

## 570054
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec057-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "何か質問ですか？<end>"
EN: "Do you have a question?<end>"
KO: "뭔가 질문 있으신가요?<end>"

## 570055
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec057-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "無限のソウルとは<end>"
EN: "What is the Infinite Soul?<end>"

jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "闇の神器について<end>"
EN: "About the Dark Artifact<end>"
KO: "어둠의 신기에 대하여<end>"

jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ネモについて<end>"
EN: "About Nemo<end>"
KO: "네모에 대해서<end>"

## 570059
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec057-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "アーギルシャイアについて<end>"
EN: "About Argyleshire<end>"

## 570060
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec057-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

EN: "I have no questions<end>"
KO: "질문은 없다<end>"

## 570061
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec057-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おや、なかなか耳ざといですね。<line-break>フフ、今はまだ秘密です。<line-break>　<line-break>ま、おいおいわかりますよ。<line-break>とりあえずはギルドで<line-break>仕事をしていってください。<end>"
EN: "Oh, you have sharp ears. Heh heh, for now, it remains a secret. You'll understand in time. For the moment, take on some work at the Guild.<end>"
KO: "오, 꽤 귀가 밝으시군요. 후후, 지금은 아직 비밀입니다. 뭐, 차차 알게 될 겁니다. 일단 길드에서 일을 해 주세요.<end>"

## 570062
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec057-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "他人のために働くことで、<line-break>魂を成長させていけば<line-break>いずれ、わかってきますよ。<end>"
EN: "As you work for the sake of others and nurture your soul, the answer will come to you.<end>"
KO: "다른 사람을 위해 일하며 영혼을 성장시켜 가면 언젠가 알게 될 겁니다.<end>"

## 570063
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec057-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "将来、旅の途中で出会った仲間を<line-break>呼び出すための装置です。<line-break>　<line-break>一緒に旅する仲間を<line-break>変更したくなったら<line-break>この猫屋敷に来てください。<end>"
EN: "This device will allow you to summon companions you meet during your travels. If you ever want to change who travels with you, come to Cat Manor.<end>"
KO: "앞으로 여행 중 만난 동료를 불러내기 위한 장치입니다. 함께 여행할 동료를 바꾸고 싶어지면 이 고양이 저택으로 와 주세요.<end>"

## 570064
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec057-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "一緒に旅をしたい仲間を呼び出したり、<line-break>別行動を取ることを決めた仲間を<line-break>送り出したりできますよ。<end>"
EN: "You can summon companions you wish to travel with, or send off those who have decided to go their separate ways.<end>"
KO: "함께 여행하고 싶은 동료를 불러오거나 따로 행동하기로 한 동료를 돌려보낼 수 있어요.<end>"

## 570065
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec057-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "破壊神ウルグの復活に<line-break>用いられた強力な魔道器です。<line-break>　<line-break>１２種類あるといいます。<line-break>禁断の聖杯は<line-break>その中のひとつですね。<end>"
EN: "It is a powerful magical artifact that was used to resurrect Ulugh, the God of Destruction. Such artifacts are said to come in twelve types, one of which is the Forbidden Grail.<end>"
KO: "파괴신 울그의 부활에 쓰인 강력한 마도기입니다. 12종류가 있다고 하지요. 금단의 성배도 그중 하나입니다.<end>"

## 570066
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec057-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "禁断の聖杯については<line-break>今、ゴブゴブ団と名乗る<line-break>３匹のゴブリンが持っています。<line-break>とりあえずは、<line-break>エンシャントを探してみると<line-break>彼らに会えると思いますよ。<end>"
EN: "The Forbidden Grail is currently held by three goblins who call themselves the Gob-Gob Gang. For now, I think you will meet them if you try looking for Ancient.<end>"
KO: "금단의 성배는 지금 고브고브단이라는 고블린 셋이 가지고 있습니다. 우선 엔샨트를 찾아보면 그들을 만날 수 있을 거예요.<end>"

## 570067
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec057-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ここにいるブサイクな猫です。　　<line-break>口は悪いですけど、無害ですよ。<line-break>　<line-break>もともとは老人星の精霊で<line-break>天空の賢者とも呼ばれた者でしたが<line-break>地上に堕ちて、魔人になりました。<end>"
EN: "It is the ugly cat here. It may have a foul mouth, but it is harmless. Originally a spirit of Old Man Star and also known as the Sage of the Heavens, it fell to earth and became a demon.<end>"
KO: "여기 있는 못생긴 고양이입니다. 입은 험하지만 무해해요. 원래는 노인성의 정령으로 천공의 현자라고도 불렸지만 지상으로 떨어져 마인이 되었습니다.<end>"

## 570068
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec057-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "破壊神の強力な魔人、<line-break>１２人の円卓騎士のひとりで、<line-break>名前を知るもの、の異名を持ちます。<line-break>そのあだ名の通り<line-break>金を出せば名前を付けてくれますが<line-break>ま、あんまりおすすめはしません。<end>"
EN: "This is one of the God of Destruction's powerful demons, one of the twelve Knights of the Round Table, and bears the epithet “the One Who Knows Names.” True to that epithet, a name will be assigned for a price, though I cannot really recommend it.<end>"
KO: "파괴신의 강력한 마인, 12인의 원탁기사 중 하나로 '이름을 아는 자'라는 이명이 있습니다. 별명대로 돈을 내면 이름을 지어 주지만, 별로 추천하진 않습니다.<end>"

## 570069
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec057-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "円卓騎士には他に<line-break>アーギルシャイアなどがいますね。　　<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "The other Knights of the Round Table include Argyleshire.<end>"
KO: "원탁기사에는 그 밖에도 아르길샤이어 등이 있습니다.<end>"

## 570070
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec057-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "破壊神ウルグの円卓騎士の１人です。<line-break>妖艶な美女の姿をしています。<line-break>　<line-break>きわめて、気まぐれで残酷です。<line-break>すでに神器を手に入れるため<line-break>ミイスの村を焼き払っています。<end>"
EN: "She is one of Ulugh the God of Destruction's Knights of the Round Table and takes the form of a bewitching beauty. She is extraordinarily capricious and cruel. She has already burned Miis Village to the ground in pursuit of a divine artifact.<end>"
KO: "파괴신 울그의 원탁기사 중 한 명입니다. 요염한 미녀의 모습을 하고 있습니다. 극도로 변덕스럽고 잔혹합니다. 이미 신기를 얻기 위해 미이스 마을을 불태웠습니다.<end>"

## 570071
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec057-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "究極生物を作ろうとして、<line-break>その製法を神器、禁断の聖杯から<line-break>得ようと、聖杯をねらっています。<line-break>円卓騎士には他に<line-break>うちのネモなどがいますね。<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "She is after the Forbidden Grail, a divine artifact, hoping to learn from it how to create an Ultimate Life-Form. The other Knights of the Round Table include our own Nemo.<end>"
KO: "궁극 생물을 만들려고, 그 제조법을 신기, 금단의 성배에서 얻기 위해 성배를 노리고 있습니다. 원탁기사에는 그 밖에도 저희 네모 같은 이들이 있죠.<end>"

## 570072
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec057-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうですか。<end>"
EN: "I see.<end>"
KO: "그렇습니까.<end>"

## 570073
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec057-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ま、こんな感じですかね。<line-break>わかっていただけましたか？<end>"
EN: "Well, that about covers it. Does that make sense?<end>"
KO: "뭐, 이런 느낌입니다. 이해하셨나요?<end>"

## 570074
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec057-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "俺も冒険者だ。<line-break>それこそ、世界中をまわった。　　　<line-break>　<line-break>賢者の森にも何度も来た。だが、<line-break>ここに入ったことは一度もなかった。<line-break>運命に選ばれていなかったんだな。<end>"
EN: "I'm an adventurer too. I've traveled all over the world. I've been to Sage's Forest many times, but I'd never once set foot in this place. Fate hadn't chosen me, I suppose.<end>"
KO: "나도 모험자야. 정말 세계 곳곳을 돌았지. 현자의 숲에도 몇 번이나 왔어. 하지만 여기에 들어온 적은 한 번도 없었지. 운명에 선택받지 못했던 거야.<end>"

## 570075
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec057-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "その俺がここにいる。<line-break>これはどういうことだって思う？　　<end>"
EN: "And yet here I am. What do you think that means?<end>"
KO: "그런 내가 여기 있어. 이게 무슨 뜻이라고 생각해?<end>"

## 570076
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec057-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お前さんが<line-break>運命に選ばれた人間だってことさ。　　<end>"
EN: "It means fate chose you.<end>"
KO: "자네가 운명에 선택받은 인간이라는 뜻이지.<end>"

## 570077
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec057-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "別に、お前さんが特別な人間で、<line-break>他人より上等だ下等だってんじゃない。<line-break>安心してくれ。<line-break>俺が言いたいのは<line-break>お前さんと旅をしてると面白いことに<line-break>たくさん出会えそうだってことだ。<end>"
EN: "That doesn't mean you're special, or any better or worse than anyone else. Don't worry. All I'm saying is, traveling with you, I have a feeling we'll come across all sorts of interesting things.<end>"
KO: "네가 특별한 인간이라서 남보다 우월하다거나 열등하다는 이야기가 아니야. 안심해. 내가 말하고 싶은 건 너와 여행하면 재미있는 일을 많이 만날 것 같다는 거지.<end>"

## 570078
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec057-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…ルルアンタのこともある。<line-break>これからはそういう出会いを<line-break>大切にしていかないとな。<end>"
EN: "Then there's Lulluantha. From now on, I'd better treasure encounters like that.<end>"
KO: "…루루안타 일도 있고. 앞으로는 그런 만남을 소중히 해야겠어.<end>"

## 570079
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec057-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "じゃ、行きますか。<end>"
EN: "Well then, shall we get going?<end>"
KO: "그럼, 갈까요.<end>"

## 570080
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec057-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "まったく手間をとらせやがって！<end>"
EN: "Damn you! All the trouble you've caused!<end>"
KO: "정말 손이 많이 가게 하는군!<end>"

## 570081
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec057-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "誘拐の現場を見られちまった以上、<line-break>生かしておくわけにはいかねぇんでな。<line-break>森の方で始末しちまおう。<end>"
EN: "Now that you've seen us kidnapping someone, we can't let you live. We'll take you into the woods and finish you off.<end>"
KO: "유괴 현장을 들킨 이상 살려 둘 수는 없어. 숲 쪽에서 처리하자.<end>"

## 570082
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec057-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "何者だ、貴様！<end>"
EN: "Who the hell are you?!<end>"
KO: "네놈, 정체가 뭐냐!<end>"

## 570083
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec057-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "見られちまった以上、<line-break>こいつも始末するしかねぇってか！<end>"
EN: "So now we've got no choice but to finish off another witness?!<end>"
KO: "들켜 버린 이상 이 녀석도 처리할 수밖에 없다는 건가!<end>"

## 570084
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec057-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "うんん…。<end>"
EN: "Nnngh...<end>"
KO: "으음….<end>"

## 570085
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec057-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あ！<end>"
EN: "Ah!<end>"
KO: "아!<end>"

## 570086
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec057-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ルルアンタ、見たんだから！<line-break>みなさ～ん！　この人…<end>"
EN: "Lulluantha saw it! Everybody! This person...<end>"
KO: "루루안타, 봤거든! 여러분~! 이 사람….<end>"

## 570087
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec057-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "むが、むが、むがが…。<end>"
EN: "Mmph, mmph, mmmph...<end>"
KO: "우가, 우가, 우가가….<end>"

## 570088
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec057-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<if><value:$29><equal>%0それじゃあ、<line-break>お兄ちゃんが助けてくれたの？<end>それじゃあ、<line-break>お姉ちゃんが助けてくれたの？<end>"
EN: "<if><value:$29><equal>%0So you were the one who saved me, mister?<end>So you were the one who saved me, miss?<end>"
KO: "<if><value:$29><equal>%0그럼, 오빠가 도와준 거야?<end>그럼, 언니가 도와준 거야?<end>"

## 570089
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec057-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうだったんだ。<line-break>ありがとう。<line-break>それに、ごめんなさい。<end>"
EN: "Oh, that's what happened. Thank you. And... I'm sorry.<end>"
KO: "그랬구나. 고마워. 그리고 미안해.<end>"

## 570090
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec057-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "う～ん。<line-break>おうちにかえりなさいって言われても、<line-break>…ルルアンタ、困っちゃうな。<line-break>あのね、ルルアンタ、<line-break>お父さんとお母さんいないの。<line-break>ずっとはやく、死んじゃった。<end>"
EN: "Hmm. Even if you tell Lulluantha to go home... Lulluantha doesn't know what to do. You see, Lulluantha doesn't have a mother or father. They died a long, long time ago.<end>"
KO: "으음. 집에 돌아가라고 해도… 루루안타는 곤란한걸. 있잖아, 루루안타는 아빠랑 엄마가 없어. 아주 오래전에 돌아가셨어.<end>"

## 570091
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec057-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お世話になってたフリントさんも、<line-break>旅の途中で盗賊に襲われて…、<line-break>…死んじゃった。<end>"
EN: "Mr. Flint was looking after me, but bandits attacked him on the road... and he died.<end>"
KO: "신세를 졌던 플린트 씨도 여행 도중 도적에게 습격당해서… 죽어 버렸어.<end>"

## 570092
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec057-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<if><value:$29><equal>%0それで、ルルアンタも<line-break>捕まっちゃってたところを<line-break>お兄ちゃんに助けてもらったの。<end>それで、ルルアンタも<line-break>捕まっちゃってたところを<line-break>お姉ちゃんに助けてもらったの。<end>"
EN: "<if><value:$29><equal>%0Then Lulluantha got captured too, but you saved me, mister.<end>Then Lulluantha got captured too, but you saved me, miss.<end>"
KO: "<if><value:$29><equal>%0그래서 루루안타도 붙잡혀 있던 걸 오빠한테 도움받았어.<end>그래서 루루안타도 붙잡혀 있던 걸 언니한테 도움받았어.<end>"

## 570093
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec057-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "うん、ルルアンタ、元気だよ。　　　<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>あのね、<line-break>フリントさんが言ってくれたんだ。<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "Mm-hm! Lulluantha's doing fine. You know, Mr. Flint once told me something.<end>"
KO: "응, 루루안타는 잘 지내. 있잖아, 플린트 씨가 말해 줬어.<end>"

## 570094
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec057-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ルルアンタは元気だね、<line-break>ルルアンタが元気だと私も<line-break>うれしくなって元気が出るよって。<line-break>だから、ルルアンタ、<line-break>天国のフリントさんが<line-break>喜んでくれるように元気なんだ。<end>"
EN: "He said, “Lulluantha, you're always so cheerful. When you're cheerful, it makes me happy and cheers me up too.” So Lulluantha stays cheerful to make Mr. Flint happy up in heaven.<end>"
KO: "루루안타는 건강하네, 루루안타가 건강하면 나도 기뻐서 힘이 난다고 했어. 그래서 루루안타는 천국의 플린트 씨가 기뻐하도록 늘 건강하게 지내는 거야.<end>"

## 570095
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec057-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<if><value:$29><equal>%0ねえ、ルルアンタ、<line-break>お兄ちゃんと一緒に行っていい？<end>ねえ、ルルアンタ、<line-break>お姉ちゃんと一緒に行っていい？<end>"
EN: "<if><value:$29><equal>%0Hey, can Lulluantha come with you, mister?<end>Hey, can Lulluantha come with you, miss?<end>"
KO: "<if><value:$29><equal>%0저기, 루루안타, 오빠랑 같이 가도 돼?<end>저기, 루루안타, 언니랑 같이 가도 돼?<end>"

## 570096
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec057-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いいよ<end>"
EN: "Sure.<end>"
KO: "좋아<end>"

## 570097
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec057-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それはできない<end>"
EN: "That's not possible.<end>"
KO: "그건 못 해<end>"

## 570098
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec057-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<if><value:$29><equal>%0わーい、じゃあ、ルルアンタ、<line-break>これからはお兄ちゃんと一緒だぁ！<line-break>　<line-break>お兄ちゃん、お名前は？<end>わーい、じゃあ、ルルアンタ、<line-break>これからはお姉ちゃんと一緒だぁ！<line-break>　<line-break>お姉ちゃん、お名前は？<end>"
EN: "<if><value:$29><equal>%0Hooray! Then Lulluantha gets to be with you from now on! What's your name, mister?<end>Hooray! Then Lulluantha gets to be with you from now on! What's your name, miss?<end>"
KO: "<if><value:$29><equal>%0와아, 그럼 루루안타는 이제부터 오빠랑 함께다! 오빠, 이름이 뭐야?<end>와아, 그럼 루루안타는 이제부터 언니랑 함께다! 언니, 이름이 뭐야?<end>"

## 570099
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec057-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "じゃ、あらためてぇ…。　　　　<end>"
EN: "Okay, let's start over properly...<end>"
KO: "그럼 다시 한번….<end>"

## 570100
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec057-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あたしは、ルルアンタ。<line-break>よろしくね、<value:$28>。<end>"
EN: "I'm Lulluantha. Nice to meet you, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "나는 루루안타야. 잘 부탁해, <value:$28>.<end>"

## 570101
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec057-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "じゃあ、町を出るときに！<line-break>またねー。<end>"
EN: "Then I'll see you when you leave town! Bye-bye!<end>"
KO: "그럼 마을을 나갈 때 보자! 또 봐ー.<end>"

## 570102
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec057-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "じゃあ、近いうちに、<line-break>ねこやしきっていう所で<line-break>呼び出してくれるんだ。<line-break>はやく、一緒に旅がしたいな。<line-break>じゃ、またねー。<end>"
EN: "So you'll call for me at a place called Cat Manor soon. I can't wait to travel with you. Bye-bye for now!<end>"
KO: "그럼 조만간 고양이 저택이라는 곳에서 불러 주는 거구나. 빨리 같이 여행하고 싶다. 그럼 또 봐~.<end>"

## 570103
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec057-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そう…。<line-break>ちょっと、がっかり。<end>"
EN: "Oh... That's a little disappointing.<end>"
KO: "그래…. 조금 실망이네.<end>"

## 570104
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec057-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "じゃあ、またねえ！<line-break>助けてくれてありがとう！<end>"
EN: "Well, bye-bye! Thank you for saving me!<end>"
KO: "그럼 또 봐! 도와줘서 고마워!<end>"

## 570105
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec057-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フリントさん、<line-break>ルルアンタ、今日も元気だったよ。　　<line-break>　<line-break>今はね、<value:$28>と一緒に<line-break>旅してるんだ。とっても楽しいよ。<line-break>だからフリントさん、心配しないでね。<end>"
EN: "Flint, Lulluantha's doing fine today, too. Lulluantha's traveling with <value:$28> now. It's so much fun. So don't worry, Flint.<end>"
KO: "플린트 씨, 루루안타 오늘도 잘 지냈어. 지금은 <value:$28>와 함께 여행하고 있어. 정말 즐거워. 그러니까 플린트 씨, 걱정하지 마.<end>"

## 570106
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec057-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…あのとき、わたしたち、　　　<line-break>変な盗賊の人たちに襲われて…。<line-break>　<line-break>手紙を出せって言われて<line-break>フリントさんたちは殺されちゃって…。<line-break>ルルアンタ、ひとりぼっちになって…。<end>"
EN: "Back then... we were attacked by some strange bandits. They told us to send a letter, and then you and the others were killed... Lulluantha was left all alone...<end>"
KO: "…그때 우리, 이상한 도적들에게 습격당해서…. 편지를 쓰라고 강요받고 플린트 씨 일행은 죽어 버리고…. 루루안타, 혼자가 돼서….<end>"

## 570107
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec057-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あれから、心は毎日雨降りだったけど、<line-break><value:$28>のおかげで、今は<line-break>ルルアンタにもおひさまが見えるよ。<line-break>ルルアンタ、元気だよ。<line-break>心配しないでね、フリントさん…。<end>"
EN: "After that, it rained in Lulluantha's heart every day. But thanks to <value:$28>, Lulluantha can see the sunshine now, too. Lulluantha's doing fine. Don't worry, Flint...<end>"
KO: "그 뒤로 마음엔 매일 비가 왔지만 <value:$28> 덕분에 지금은 루루안타에게도 해님이 보여. 루루안타, 잘 지내. 걱정하지 마, 플린트 씨….<end>"

## 570108
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec057-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>か？<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>?<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>인가?<end>"

## 570109
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec057-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…なあ、<value:$28>。<line-break>冒険をしてて時々思うんだが、<line-break>強さとか弱さって、何だと思う？　　<line-break>ま、俺も答が見えたわけじゃないが、<line-break>少しづつ、わかってきたことはある。<line-break>人ってのは、みんな弱いって事さ。<end>"
EN: "Hey, <value:$28>. Sometimes, when I'm out adventuring, I find myself wondering: what do you think it means to be strong or weak? Not that I've found the answer myself, but there are a few things I'm starting to understand. We're all weak. Every one of us.<end>"
KO: "…있잖아, <value:$28>. 모험을 하다 보면 가끔 생각하는데, 강함이나 약함이란 대체 뭘까? 뭐, 나도 답을 찾은 건 아니지만 조금씩 알게 된 건 있어. 사람은 누구나 약하다는 거야.<end>"

## 570110
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec057-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "で、その当たり前に持ってる弱さと　<line-break>共に生きていける奴ってのが<line-break>強い奴なんじゃないかってな。<line-break>弱さに対して、拒まず、甘えず…、<line-break>抱えた弱さを正面から見つめられる。<line-break>自分の弱さも、他人の弱さもだ。<end>"
EN: "And maybe the strong ones are the ones who can live with that weakness we all have. They don't reject it, but they don't lean on it either. They face weakness squarely, both their own and other people's.<end>"
KO: "그래서 누구나 갖고 있는 그 약함과 함께 살아갈 수 있는 녀석이 강한 사람이 아닐까 싶더라. 약함을 거부하지도, 기대지도 않고… 품고 있는 약함을 정면으로 바라볼 수 있는 것. 자기 약함도, 남의 약함도 말이야.<end>"

## 570111
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec057-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…って、悪りい。妙に語っちまったな。<line-break>よし、もう寝ることにするわ。<line-break>じゃあな、お休み。<end>"
EN: "Sorry. Didn't mean to go on like that. Right, I'm going to sleep. See you. Night.<end>"
KO: "…아, 미안. 이상하게 말이 길어졌네. 좋아, 이제 자야겠다. 그럼 잘 자.<end>"

## 570112
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec057-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…ま、よく考えてな。<line-break>人間ってのは、弱くてもろい<line-break>生き物だって事を。<end>"
EN: "Well, give it some thought. Human beings are weak, fragile creatures.<end>"
KO: "…뭐, 잘 생각해 봐. 인간은 약하고 쉽게 부서지는 생물이라는 걸.<end>"

## 570113
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec057-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お客さん。飲み過ぎだよ。<line-break>店の酒、みーんな、飲んじまって…。<end>"
EN: "You've had too much to drink. You've gone and drunk every drop in the place...<end>"
KO: "손님, 너무 마셨어. 가게 술을 전부 마셔버리고…<end>"

## 570114
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec057-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…どうせ、どうせ、俺なんか、<line-break>世界で一番さ、フヒャヒャヒャヒャ…。<end>"
EN: "...Anyway... anyway, a guy like me is the greatest in the whole world! Fuh-hyah-hyah-hyah...<end>"
KO: "…어차피, 어차피 나 같은 건 세상에서 제일이지, 흐햐햐햐햐….<end>"

## 570115
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec057-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ダメだこりゃ。<line-break>何がなんだかわかってないよ。お客さん。<end>"
EN: "This is hopeless. You don't even know what's going on, do you?<end>"
KO: "이거 안 되겠네. 뭐가 뭔지 전혀 모르고 있잖아, 손님.<end>"

## 570117
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec057-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お客さん。<end>"
EN: "Sir?<end>"
KO: "손님.<end>"

## 570118
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec057-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "……勇者…様。<end>"
EN: "...Hero... sir.<end>"
KO: "……용사… 님.<end>"

## 570119
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec057-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "どうした！　酒場のオヤジ！<end>"
EN: "What is it, barkeep?!<end>"
KO: "왜 그래! 술집 아저씨!<end>"

## 570120
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec057-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "勇者様、魔王がでました。<end>"
EN: "Lord Hero, the Demon King has appeared.<end>"
KO: "용사님, 마왕이 나타났습니다.<end>"

## 570121
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec057-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "何！　魔王？<line-break>ぬぅ！　人として許せん！<line-break>勘定はここに置くぞ！　でやっ！<end>"
EN: "What?! The Demon King? Nngh! As a human, I cannot let this stand! Here's the money for my tab! Hyaa!<end>"
KO: "뭐! 마왕이라고? 으음! 인간으로서 용서 못 해! 계산은 여기 두고 간다! 이야앗!<end>"

## 570122
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec057-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "オヤジ、今のは、なしだ。<line-break>でやっ！！<end>"
EN: "Barkeep, forget what just happened. Hyaa!!<end>"
KO: "아저씨, 방금 건 없던 걸로 해. 이야앗!!<end>"

## 570123
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec057-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あれあれ、忘れ物だ。<line-break>弓なんか置いて行かれても困るなあ。<end>"
EN: "Well, well, something got left behind. What am I supposed to do with a bow?<end>"
KO: "어라, 두고 간 물건이네. 활 같은 걸 두고 가도 곤란한데.<end>"

## 570124
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec057-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あー、<value:$28>だ。<line-break>お酒なんか飲んじゃダメだよお。<line-break>　<line-break>ゼネテスさんみたいな<line-break>飲兵衛になっちゃうから。<end>"
EN: "Oh, it's <value:$28>. You shouldn't drink, you know. You'll end up a drunkard like Xenetes.<end>"
KO: "아, <value:$28>이다. 술 같은 거 마시면 안 돼. 제네테스 씨 같은 술꾼이 돼 버리니까.<end>"

## 570125
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec057-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ガルドランが、いたの…？<end>"
EN: "Girldlan was here...?<end>"
KO: "갈드란이 있었어…?<end>"

## 570126
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec057-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いえね、今の変なお客さんが<line-break>弓矢を忘れて行かれたんですよ。<end>"
EN: "Well, that strange customer who was just here left a bow and arrows behind.<end>"
KO: "그게 말이죠, 방금 그 이상한 손님이 활과 화살을 두고 갔어요.<end>"

## 570127
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec057-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "矢…？　ガルドランの…。<end>"
EN: "An arrow...? Girldlan's...<end>"
KO: "화살…? 갈드란의…?<end>"

## 570128
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec057-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…やっぱり、<value:$28>、<line-break>まだフリントさんのことで…。<end>"
EN: "...I thought so, <value:$28>. This is still about Flint...<end>"
KO: "…역시 <value:$28>, 아직 플린트 씨 일 때문에….<end>"

## 570129
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec057-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ホントだ。<line-break>毒が塗ってあった形跡がない。<end>"
EN: "You're right. There's no sign it was ever coated with poison.<end>"
KO: "정말이네. 독을 발랐던 흔적이 없어.<end>"

## 570130
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec057-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ううん。毒をふき取るも何も、<end>"
EN: "No. It's not even a question of wiping off the poison...<end>"
KO: "아니. 독을 닦아 내고 말고 할 것도 없이,<end>"

## 570131
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec057-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "これ、たぶん買ってから一度も<line-break>手入れしてないよぉ。<end>"
EN: "This probably hasn't been looked after at all since it was bought.<end>"
KO: "이거 아마 산 뒤로 한 번도 손질 안 했지?<end>"

## 570132
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec057-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "これじゃ、さびてて使いものに　<line-break>ならないよ。<line-break>　<line-break>それに見て。<line-break>この矢、フリントさんに<line-break>刺さってたのとぜんぜん違う…。<end>"
EN: "It's so rusty it's useless. And look. This arrow is nothing like the one that was lodged in Flint...<end>"
KO: "이래선 녹슬어서 쓸 수가 없어. 그리고 봐. 이 화살, 플린트 씨에게 박혀 있던 것과 전혀 달라….<end>"

## 570133
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec057-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "まったく。<line-break>港に来ないと思ったら、案の定これだ。<line-break>何やってんだ、<value:$28>。<end>今は急ごうっていう状況だろ？<line-break>こっからエンシャントなら絶対に船だ。<line-break>さあ、港に急ごうぜ。<end>"
EN: "Honestly. When you didn't show up at the harbor, I knew I'd find you doing something like this. What are you doing, <value:$28>?<end>We're in a hurry, remember? If we're going to Ancient from here, we have to take a ship. Come on, let's get to the harbor.<end>"
KO: "정말. 항구에 안 오길래 역시 이거였군. 뭐 하는 거야, <value:$28>.<end>지금은 서둘러야 하는 상황이잖아? 여기서 엔샨트라면 무조건 배야. 자, 항구로 서두르자.<end>"

## 570134
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec057-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "よお、そろそろ出港だろ？<line-break>そんじゃ、よろしく頼むぜ。<end>"
EN: "Hey, you're setting sail soon, right? We're counting on you, then.<end>"
KO: "여어, 슬슬 출항할 때지? 그럼 잘 부탁한다.<end>"

## 570135
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec057-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そいつが迷子になってた連れか…。<line-break>ち、さっさと乗んな。<end>"
EN: "So that's the companion who got lost... Tch. Hurry up and get aboard.<end>"
KO: "저 녀석이 길 잃었던 일행인가…. 쳇, 얼른 타.<end>"

## 570136
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec057-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あん？<line-break>船賃はいいのかって？<end>いいんだ。<line-break>さっきこの男前と軽く勝負してな。<line-break>タダで乗っけてもらう事になったんだ。<end>"
EN: "Huh? You're asking about the fare?<end>It's taken care of. This handsome fellow and I had a little contest earlier, and now we get to ride for free.<end>"
KO: "응? 뱃삯은 괜찮냐고?<end>괜찮아. 아까 이 미남하고 가볍게 승부해서 공짜로 태워 주기로 했거든.<end>"

## 570137
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec057-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "くっ。<line-break>俺はあんな勝負認ないぞ。<line-break>このイカサマ野郎。<end>"
EN: "Grr. I don't accept the result of that contest, you cheating bastard.<end>"
KO: "큭. 난 그런 승부 인정 못 해. 이 사기꾼 자식.<end>"

## 570138
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec057-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "まあまあ、そう怒りなさんな。<line-break>さあ、エンシャントへ出発だ！<end>"
EN: "Now, now, don't get so worked up. All right, off to Ancient!<end>"
KO: "자자, 그렇게 화내지 마. 자, 엔샨트로 출발이다!<end>"

## 570139
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec057-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なんだかなあ…。<line-break>ルルアンタを探すつもりが<line-break>変な方向に話がふくらんできたな…。<line-break>まあ、今は話を聞いておこうや。<line-break>さ、行ってみようぜ。<end>"
EN: "This is getting strange... We set out to find Lulluantha, and now the whole thing's gone off in some odd direction. Still, let's hear the story for now. Come on, let's go see.<end>"
KO: "참…. 루루안타를 찾으려던 게 이상한 방향으로 이야기가 커졌군…. 뭐, 지금은 이야기를 들어 보자. 자, 가 보자고.<end>"

## 570140
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec057-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうだな、とりあえずは動くしかない。<line-break>お前さんの言う通りだ。<line-break>　<line-break>んじゃ、行きますか？<end>"
EN: "Yeah. For now, we've just got to get moving. You're right. Shall we go, then?<end>"
KO: "그래, 일단 움직이는 수밖에 없겠지. 네 말이 맞아. 그럼 가 볼까?<end>"

## 580000
