## 580001
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec058-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おお、<value:$28>か！<line-break>よく来た。まあ、飲もうぞ！<end>"
EN: "Ah, <value:$28>! Good of you to come. Now, have a drink!<end>"
KO: "오오, <value:$28>인가! 잘 왔다. 자, 한잔하자!<end>"

## 580002
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec058-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あちゃー、さっそくアンギルダンに<line-break>捕まってるよ。しょうがないねえ。<end>"
EN: "Oh, dear. Angeerdan's got hold of you already. Well, what can you do?<end>"
KO: "아차, 벌써 앙길단에게 붙잡혔네. 어쩔 수 없지.<end>"

## 580003
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec058-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "どうした、初めて会うような顔をして。<line-break>わしじゃ、アンギルダンじゃ。<end>"
EN: "What's wrong? You look as though we've never met. It's me, Angeerdan!<end>"
KO: "왜 그러느냐, 처음 보는 사람 같은 얼굴을 하고. 나다, 앙길단이야.<end>"

## 580004
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec058-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おお、フィーユ！　なんじゃ！<line-break><value:$28>と知り合いか！<end>"
EN: "Oh, Fille! What's this? You know <value:$28>?<end>"
KO: "오, 피유! 뭐냐! <value:$28>과 아는 사이였나!<end>"

## 580005
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec058-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "んー、アハ…アハハ…<line-break><value:$28>自体のことは<line-break>そんなに詳しくはないんだけどさ。　<line-break>ま、いちおう、今はパーティー組んで<line-break>一緒に冒険してるよ。うん。<line-break>ハハ…ハハハ…。<end>"
EN: "Uh... Ah... Ahaha... I don't really know all that much about <value:$28>, actually. But, well, we're in a party now, adventuring together. Yeah. Haha... Hahaha...<end>"
KO: "음, 아하… 하하…. <value:$28>에 대해서는 그렇게 자세히 아는 건 아니지만 말이야. 뭐, 지금은 일단 파티를 맺고 같이 모험 중이야. 응. 하하… 하하하….<end>"

## 580006
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec058-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>の感じが<line-break>どうも、変なんじゃ。<line-break>どうしたのじゃ、フィーユ？<end>"
EN: "There's something decidedly odd about <value:$28>. What is it, Fille?<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>의 느낌이 아무래도 이상하구나. 무슨 일이냐, 피유?<end>"

## 580007
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec058-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…ちっ、カンのいい爺さんだね。<line-break>いや、その、記憶喪失なんだ。うん！<end>"
EN: "Tch... Sharp old man, aren't you? No, um, it's amnesia. Yes!<end>"
KO: "…칫, 눈치 빠른 영감이네. 아니, 그게, 기억상실이야. 응!<end>"

## 580008
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec058-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "記憶喪失？　ハハハハ！<line-break>そりゃ、いいわい！　ハハハ！　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>じゃが、わしには、<value:$28>が<line-break>記憶喪失なぞではなく、<line-break>別人になったように見える。<end>"
EN: "Amnesia? Hahahaha! That's a good one! Hahaha! To me, though, <value:$28> seems not to have amnesia, but to have become a different person.<end>"
KO: "기억상실? 하하하하! 그거 좋군! 하하하! 하지만 내 눈엔 <value:$28>가 기억상실이라기보다 다른 사람이 된 것처럼 보인다.<end>"

## 580009
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec058-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…ハハ…ハハ！　おかしいねえ！<line-break>そんなバカなこと！　ハハ！<end>"
EN: "Ha... Haha! That's absurd! What a ridiculous idea! Haha!<end>"
KO: "…하하… 하하! 웃기네! 그런 바보 같은 일이! 하하!<end>"

## 580010
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec058-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "傭兵として世界を渡り歩いた<line-break>わしのカンがそう言うておる。　　　<line-break>　<line-break>そうバカにしたもんでもないと<line-break>自分では思うておるがな。往々にして<line-break>常識よりカンが正しいものじゃ。<end>"
EN: "I roamed the world as a mercenary, and that's what my instincts tell me. I happen to think they're not to be scoffed at. Often enough, instinct is sounder than common sense.<end>"
KO: "용병으로 세계를 떠돌아다닌 내 감이 그렇게 말하고 있어. 그리 얕볼 만한 감은 아니라고 생각한다네. 종종 상식보다 감이 맞는 법이지.<end>"

## 580011
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec058-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ハ…ハハ…ハハハ！<line-break>じ、冗談だよ。<line-break>記憶喪失ってのは、さ。　　　　　　　<line-break><value:$28>は○○○○○○○○。<line-break>それ以上でも以下でもない。<line-break>それだけさ。そう、それだけのことさ。<end>"
EN: "Ha... Haha... Hahaha! I-it was a joke. The amnesia, I mean. <value:$28> is ○○○○○○○○. Nothing more and nothing less. That's all. Yes, that's all there is to it.<end>"
KO: "하… 하하… 하하하! 노, 농담이야. 기억상실이라는 건 말이지. <value:$28>는 ○○○○○○○○. 그 이상도 이하도 아니야. 그뿐이야. 그래, 그게 다야.<end>"

## 580012
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec058-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "別に詮索はせんさ。<line-break>それも、冒険者のおきてじゃからな。<line-break>　<line-break>さてと、今日もよう飲んだ。<line-break>ちと、外へ出て、よい空気でも　　　<line-break>吸うとしようか。<end>"
EN: "I won't pry. That's part of the adventurer's code, after all. Well, I've had plenty to drink today. I think I'll step outside for a breath of fresh air.<end>"
KO: "딱히 캐묻지는 않지. 그것도 모험자의 규칙이니까. 자, 오늘도 실컷 마셨군. 밖에 나가 좋은 공기라도 좀 마셔 볼까.<end>"

## 580013
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec058-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ハハハ！　そうすれば、ただでさえ、<line-break>最近は物忘れが激しいんじゃ。<line-break>今のことも忘れてしまうじゃろうて。<end>"
EN: "Hahaha! That should do it. My memory has been getting rather poor of late. No doubt I'll forget all about this as well.<end>"
KO: "하하하! 그러면 안 그래도 요즘 건망증이 심하니 지금 일도 잊어버리겠지.<end>"

## 580014
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec058-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "今のはアンギルダンのとっつぁんさ。<line-break>見ての通り、年だけど、強いのなんの！<line-break>　<line-break>力だけじゃなくて頭も切れる。<line-break>さっき、あんたも、もう少しで<line-break>正体ばれるとこだったろ。<end>"
EN: "That was old Angeerdan. He's old, as you can see, but is he ever strong! And he has the wits to match. He almost saw through you just now, didn't he?<end>"
KO: "방금 사람은 앙길단 아저씨야. 보다시피 나이가 많지만 엄청 강해! 힘만 센 게 아니라 머리도 잘 돌아가. 아까 너도 하마터면 정체를 들킬 뻔했잖아.<end>"

## 580015
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec058-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "物忘れがひどい？　ウソウソ！<line-break>ただ、ああ言ったからには、もう、<line-break>あんたのこと、詮索はしないだろうね。<line-break>誠実で、義に篤い、気のいい爺さんさ。<line-break>酒好きで、おしゃべり好き。<line-break>ちょっと元気すぎて、変な感じだけど。<end>"
EN: "His memory's bad? He made that up! But once he's said he won't pry, you can be sure he won't. He's sincere, honorable, and a good old man. He loves a drink and a chat. He's a little too lively, almost strangely so.<end>"
KO: "건망증이 심하다고? 거짓말이지! 그래도 그렇게 말했으니 이제 네 일을 캐묻진 않을 거야. 성실하고 의리가 깊은 좋은 영감이야. 술 좋아하고 말하기 좋아하고. 조금 지나치게 활기차서 이상해 보이긴 하지만.<end>"

## 580016
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec058-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "悪い人じゃないから、<line-break>この世界のことを聞くといいよ。<line-break>傭兵として世界を巡った男だからね。　<end>"
EN: "He's not a bad man, so you should ask him about this world. He traveled all over it as a mercenary.<end>"
KO: "나쁜 사람은 아니니 이 세계에 대해 물어봐도 좋아. 용병으로 세계를 돌아다닌 남자니까.<end>"

## 580017
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec058-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "じゃね。<line-break><value:$28>。<end>"
EN: "See you, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "그럼 안녕, <value:$28>.<end>"

## 580019
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec058-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "奴は天才じゃった…。が、それゆえに<line-break>あまりに革新的で理想的な改革を<line-break>押し進めようとしたのじゃ。<end>"
EN: "He was a genius. Yet that was precisely why he tried to press ahead with reforms that were far too radical and idealistic.<end>"
KO: "그는 천재였지…. 하지만 그렇기에 너무나 혁신적이고 이상적인 개혁을 밀어붙이려 했던 게야.<end>"

## 580020
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec058-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "しかし、人々は変化を嫌ったため<line-break>改革は一向に進まなかった。<line-break>奴は人々を理解できず、苦しんだ…。<line-break>業を煮やした奴は、自分の政策を<line-break>強硬に実行するための力を闇に求めた。<line-break>…奴は闇におち、魔王となった。<end>"
EN: "But the people resisted change, and his reforms went nowhere. He could not understand them, and it tormented him. At last, his patience exhausted, he turned to darkness for the power to impose his policies by force. He fell into darkness and became the Demon King.<end>"
KO: "하지만 사람들은 변화를 싫어해 개혁은 전혀 진척되지 않았다. 녀석은 사람들을 이해하지 못해 괴로워했지…. 결국 참다못한 그는 자기 정책을 강행할 힘을 어둠에서 찾았다. …녀석은 어둠에 떨어져 마왕이 됐다.<end>"

## 580021
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec058-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "やがて、奴の中で理想は消え失せ、<line-break>闇の力、恐怖の力によって世界を<line-break>支配しようと考えるようになった。<end>"
EN: "Before long, his ideals had vanished, and he began to think of ruling the world through the power of darkness―the power of fear.<end>"
KO: "이윽고 그 안에서 이상은 사라지고 어둠의 힘, 공포의 힘으로 세계를 지배하려고 생각하게 됐다.<end>"

## 580022
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec058-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そして奴は人間らしい愛すらも失った。<line-break>自分の娘の子が自分を倒すであろうと<line-break>妖術宰相ゾフォルが予言すると…<line-break>奴は身ごもった愛娘を殺して、<line-break>森に捨てたのじゃ。奴はもはや<line-break>あせりと敵意の怪物と化しておった…。<end>"
EN: "Then he lost even the capacity for human love. When Zofor, the Sorcerous Chancellor, foretold that his daughter's child would defeat him, Balor killed his beloved daughter, though she was with child, and cast her body into the forest. By then, he had become a monster driven by desperation and hostility.<end>"
KO: "그리고 녀석은 인간다운 사랑마저 잃었다. 자기 딸의 아이가 자신을 쓰러뜨릴 것이라 요술 재상 조포르가 예언하자… 임신한 사랑하는 딸을 죽여 숲에 버렸지. 녀석은 이미 초조함과 적의의 괴물이 되어 있었다….<end>"

## 580023
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec058-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そのバロルを討ったのが、<line-break>殺された娘の胎から奇跡的に　　　　<line-break>生まれた子…勇者ネメアよ。<end>"
EN: "And the one who slew Balor was the child miraculously born from his murdered daughter's womb: Nemea the Hero.<end>"
KO: "그 발로르를 쓰러뜨린 게 살해당한 딸의 배 속에서 기적적으로 태어난 아이… 용사 네메아다.<end>"

## 580024
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec058-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "すまんな、年寄りの昔語りに<line-break>長々とつきあわせてしもうた…。　　<line-break>　<line-break>…ちと、外の空気が吸いとうなった。<end>"
EN: "Forgive me for keeping you so long with an old man's tales. I think I'd like a little fresh air.<end>"
KO: "미안하구나, 늙은이의 옛날이야기에 오래 붙잡아 뒀어…. …잠깐 바깥 공기를 쐬고 싶구나.<end>"

## 580025
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec058-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おお、<value:$28>か。<line-break>よく来たの。まあ、飲め！　　　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>…と、お主は酒は飲まんのじゃったな。<line-break>よい！　牛乳でも茶でもジュースでも<line-break>好きなものをなんでも飲めばいい！<end>"
EN: "Ah, <value:$28>! Good of you to come. Here, have a drink! Oh, that's right―you don't drink alcohol. No matter! Have milk, tea, juice―whatever you like!<end>"
KO: "오오, <value:$28>인가. 잘 왔네. 자, 마셔! …아, 자네는 술을 안 마셨지. 좋아! 우유든 차든 주스든 원하는 걸 마시게!<end>"

## 580026
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec058-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "身体に入るものに悪いものはない。　<line-break>悪いものは身体から出るものの方だと<line-break>昔から言うでな。ハハハ！<end>"
EN: "There's no harm in what goes into the body. As the old saying goes, it's what comes out that's bad! Hahaha!<end>"
KO: "몸 안으로 들어가는 것에 나쁜 건 없다. 나쁜 건 몸에서 나오는 쪽이라고 옛날부터 말하지. 하하하!<end>"

## 580027
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec058-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "のう、こういう噂があるのじゃ。<line-break>エンシャント南東の賢者の森の奥には<line-break>猫屋敷という屋敷があるという。<line-break>これは運命に選ばれたものだけが、<line-break>見つけることのできる屋敷だという。<line-break>一度、行ってみるといい。<end>"
EN: "Here, have you heard this rumor? Deep in Sage's Forest, southeast of Ancient, there stands a mansion called Cat Manor. They say only those chosen by fate can find it. You should try going there sometime.<end>"
KO: "이런 소문이 있더구나. 엔샨트 남동쪽 현자의 숲 깊숙한 곳에는 고양이 저택이라는 곳이 있다더군. 운명에 선택된 자만 발견할 수 있는 저택이라 한다. 한 번 가 보는 것도 좋겠구나.<end>"

## 580028
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec058-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "よいか、<value:$28>、<line-break>冒険者は世界を広く旅して、<line-break>見聞を広めることが大事じゃ。　　　　<line-break>このリベルダムの西には<line-break>ロストール王国の都ロストールがある。<line-break>行ってみるといい。<end>"
EN: "Listen, <value:$28>. An adventurer must travel far and wide and learn about the world. West of Liberdam lies Rostorl, capital of the Kingdom of Rostorl. You should pay it a visit.<end>"
KO: "잘 들어, <value:$28>. 모험자는 세계를 넓게 여행하며 견문을 넓히는 게 중요해. 이 리벨덤 서쪽에는 로스톨 왕국의 수도 로스톨이 있다. 한번 가 보게.<end>"

## 580029
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec058-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "よいか、<value:$28>、<line-break>冒険者と言うからには　　　　<line-break>冒険をせねばならぬ。<line-break>ギルドで仕事を得たり、<line-break>自分で修行のため洞窟や迷宮に<line-break>挑んでみるといいぞ。<end>"
EN: "Listen, <value:$28>. If you call yourself an adventurer, you must have adventures! Take work from the Guild, or test yourself against caves and labyrinths as part of your training.<end>"
KO: "잘 듣게, <value:$28>. 모험가라면 모험을 해야지. 길드에서 일을 받거나 스스로 수련을 위해 동굴이나 미궁에 도전해 보게.<end>"

## 580030
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec058-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "バロルは妖術宰相ゾフォルを使って<line-break>破壊神の円卓騎士と呼ばれる魔人を<line-break>次々に復活させおった。<line-break>円卓騎士ネモ、円卓騎士マゴス、<line-break>そして円卓騎士バルザー。<line-break>…いずれも強力な魔人どもじゃ。<end>"
EN: "Balor used the Sorcerous Chancellor Zofor to revive one demon after another, all known as Knights of the Round Table of the God of Destruction: Nemo, Knight of the Round Table; Magos, Knight of the Round Table; and Baltzer, Knight of the Round Table. Every one of them was a powerful demon.<end>"
KO: "발로르는 요술 재상 조포르를 시켜 파괴신의 원탁기사라 불리는 마인들을 차례로 부활시켰다. 원탁기사 네모, 원탁기사 마고스, 그리고 원탁기사 발자. …모두 강력한 마인들이지.<end>"

## 580031
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec058-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そのうち、ネモとマゴスは<line-break>勇者ネメアに倒されたが<line-break>バルザーはいまだ行方が知れぬ。<line-break>バルザーは魔王バロルに…恋人を殺され<line-break>魔王バロルとたもとを分かって<line-break>姿を消したのでな。<end>"
EN: "Of those three, Nemo and Magos were slain by the Hero Nemea, but Baltzer's whereabouts remain unknown. Balor, Demon King, had killed Baltzer's lover... so Baltzer broke with Balor and disappeared.<end>"
KO: "그 뒤 네모와 마고스는 용사 네메아에게 쓰러졌지만 발자는 아직 행방을 알 수 없다. 발자는 마왕 발로르에게… 연인을 잃고 마왕 발로르와 결별해 자취를 감췄기 때문이지.<end>"

## 580032
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec058-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "勇者ネメアがついに<line-break>皇帝エリュマルクから<line-break>ディンガルを追われおったか…。<line-break>皇帝エリュマルクは、ネメアの叔父よ。<line-break>かつて、ネメアがバロルを倒すときには<line-break>ネメアのよき友であったが…<end>"
EN: "So the Hero Nemea has finally been driven out of Dyneskal by Emperor Elmark... Elmark is Nemea's uncle. Once, when Nemea defeated Balor, he was a good friend to Nemea, but...<end>"
KO: "용사 네메아가 결국 황제 에류마르크에게 딘갈에서 쫓겨났구나…. 황제 에류마르크는 네메아의 숙부다. 예전에 네메아가 발로르를 쓰러뜨릴 때는 좋은 벗이었건만….<end>"

## 580033
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec058-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "バロルのあとを継いで皇帝になってから<line-break>何かにつけ、ネメアと比べられ、<line-break>嫉妬からネメアをうとむようになった。<line-break>それからのエリュマルクはひどかった。<line-break>ネメアをねたみ、殺すことだけを考え、<line-break>死ねと言わんばかりの難行を命じた。<end>"
EN: "After succeeding Balor as emperor, Elmark was forever being compared to Nemea. His jealousy made him resent Nemea. From then on, Elmark was terrible. He envied Nemea and thought only of killing him, assigning ordeals that might as well have been orders to die.<end>"
KO: "발로르의 뒤를 이어 황제가 된 뒤로 무슨 일이든 네메아와 비교당했고, 질투 때문에 네메아를 멀리하게 됐다. 그 뒤의 에류마르크는 끔찍했지. 네메아를 시기하며 죽일 생각만 하고, 죽으라는 것이나 다름없는 난행을 명령했다.<end>"

## 580034
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec058-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そして、今回のネメア追討令か…。<line-break>が、世界にネメアにかなう者はおらぬ。<line-break>事実上の追放じゃな。<end>"
EN: "And now this order to hunt Nemea down... Yet no one in the world can match him. In truth, it's an exile.<end>"
KO: "그리고 이번 네메아 추토령인가…. 하지만 세상에 네메아를 이길 자는 없어. 사실상 추방이군.<end>"

## 580035
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec058-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いっそ、エリュマルクではなく<line-break>ネメアがバロルの死後、<line-break>帝位につくべきだったんじゃ。<line-break>ネメアの下でならば、<line-break>わしはたとえ、<line-break>一兵卒としてでも働いたろう。<end>"
EN: "Nemea should have taken the throne after Balor's death, not Elmark. Under Nemea, I would gladly have served even as a common soldier.<end>"
KO: "차라리 에류마르크가 아니라 네메아가 발로르 사후 제위에 올랐어야 했어. 네메아 아래라면 나는 일개 병졸로라도 일했을 테지.<end>"

## 580036
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec058-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "勇者ネメアは<line-break>ディンガル皇帝エリュマルクに　　　<line-break>その才能をうとまれておった。<line-break>エリュマルクはネメアを除こうと<line-break>それは、さまざまな<line-break>実行不可能で決死のことを命じた。<end>"
EN: "Emperor Elmark of Dyneskal resented the Hero Nemea for his gifts. Wanting Nemea out of the way, Elmark ordered him to undertake all manner of impossible, deadly tasks.<end>"
KO: "용사 네메아는 딘갈 황제 에류마르크에게 그 재능을 미움받았다. 에류마르크는 네메아를 제거하려고 여러 가지 실행 불가능한 결사 임무를 명했다.<end>"

## 580037
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec058-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そのうちのひとつが、ロセン迎撃じゃ。<line-break>ロセンの愚王ペウダは、バロルが死んで<line-break>ディンガルが弱体化したのを好機と…<line-break>残虐なロセンの将軍ランバガンに<line-break>５０００の兵をつけ、<line-break>ディンガルの東部を略奪させたのじゃ。<end>"
EN: "One of those was repelling the Rocen invasion. Peuda, the fool king of Rocen, saw his chance when Balor's death left Dyneskal weakened. He gave the brutal Rocen general Rambagan five thousand troops and sent him to pillage eastern Dyneskal.<end>"
KO: "그중 하나가 로센 요격이었다. 로센의 우왕 페우다는 발로르가 죽고 딘갈이 약해진 것을 기회로 삼아… 잔혹한 로센 장군 람바간에게 5000명의 병사를 붙여 딘갈 동부를 약탈하게 했다.<end>"

## 580038
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec058-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "対して、皇帝エリュマルクは<line-break>ネメアにそれを追討せよと命じた。　　<line-break>ただの２８騎でじゃ。<end>"
EN: "Emperor Elmark ordered Nemea to drive them back―with just twenty-eight mounted warriors.<end>"
KO: "이에 황제 에류마르크는 네메아에게 그것을 토벌하라고 명했다. 겨우 28기만으로 말이지.<end>"

## 580039
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec058-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "結果はネメアの名声を<line-break>さらに高めることになった。　　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>ネメアはわずか２８騎で中央突破し<line-break>将軍ランバガンを生け捕りにして<line-break>１００倍を越える敵を壊滅させたのだ。<end>"
EN: "The result only brought Nemea greater fame. With a mere twenty-eight mounted warriors, he smashed through the enemy center, captured General Rambagan alive, and annihilated a force more than a hundred times the size of his own.<end>"
KO: "결과적으로 네메아의 명성은 더욱 높아졌다. 네메아는 겨우 28기만으로 중앙을 돌파해 장군 람바간을 생포하고 백 배가 넘는 적군을 괴멸시켰다.<end>"

## 580040
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec058-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それ以来、ロセンの愚王ペウダは<line-break>ディンガルを恐れ、ロセンを鎖国し<line-break>城にこもって、毎日震えておるという。<line-break>しかし、この勝利は、ますます<line-break>皇帝エリュマルクの不興と嫉妬を<line-break>買う結果になってしもうた。<end>"
EN: "They say that ever since, Peuda, the fool king of Rocen, has feared Dyneskal so greatly that he closed Rocen to the outside world and now spends every day cowering in his castle. But that victory only earned Nemea still more of Emperor Elmark's displeasure and jealousy.<end>"
KO: "그 뒤로 로센의 어리석은 왕 페우다는 딩갈을 두려워해 로센을 쇄국하고 성에 틀어박혀 날마다 떨고 있다고 한다. 하지만 이 승리는 황제 에류마르크의 미움과 질투를 더욱 사는 결과가 되고 말았다.<end>"

## 580041
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec058-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ま、わしも、数えきれぬほど　　　<line-break>戦いに臨んできたが…<line-break>　<line-break>あの戦いに、ネメアの２８騎の<line-break>ひとりとして参加できたことは<line-break>まこと、晴れがましい思い出じゃ。<end>"
EN: "Well, I've fought in more battles than I can count... but taking part in that one as one of Nemea's twenty-eight is a truly proud memory.<end>"
KO: "나도 셀 수 없이 많은 전투에 나섰지만… 그 싸움에 네메아의 28기 중 하나로 참가할 수 있었던 건 정말 자랑스러운 추억이야.<end>"

## 580042
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec058-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "勇者ネメアは皇帝エリュマルクに　　　<line-break>その才能をうとまれておった。<line-break>　<line-break>エリュマルクはネメアを除こうと、<line-break>実行不可能なさまざまな難事を<line-break>ふりかけた。<end>"
EN: "Emperor Elmark resented the Hero Nemea for his gifts. Wanting Nemea out of the way, Elmark burdened him with all manner of impossible tasks.<end>"
KO: "용사 네메아는 황제 엘류마르크에게 그 재능을 미움받았다. 엘류마르크는 네메아를 제거하려고 실행 불가능한 갖가지 난제를 던졌다.<end>"

## 580043
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec058-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そのひとつが、施文院から、<line-break>絶世の美女と呼ばれていた<line-break>大神官イズをさらってくることじゃ。　<line-break>施文院とは、暗殺集団の告死天使を<line-break>操る地下宗教組織でな。その大神官を<line-break>さらうなど、まさに死を意味していた。<end>"
EN: "One was to abduct the high priestess Iz, famed as a beauty beyond compare, from the Inscription Order. The Inscription Order is an underground religious organization that commands an order of assassins called the Angels of Death. Abducting its high priestess meant certain death.<end>"
KO: "그중 하나가 시문원에서 절세의 미녀라 불리던 대신관 이즈를 납치해 오는 일이었지. 시문원은 암살집단 고사천사를 조종하는 지하 종교조직이다. 그 대신관을 납치한다는 건 말 그대로 죽음을 의미했어.<end>"

## 580044
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec058-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "しかも、なんの正当の理由もなく<line-break>女をさらうというのは、犯罪じゃ。　　<line-break>非常に不名誉なことじゃ。<line-break>皇帝エリュマルクは、それでネメアが<line-break>命令を断れば、それを理由に処刑する<line-break>つもりだったのじゃ。<end>"
EN: "Besides, abducting a woman without any just cause is a crime―a deeply dishonorable act. Emperor Elmark expected Nemea to refuse, giving him grounds to have Nemea executed.<end>"
KO: "게다가 아무 정당한 이유 없이 여자를 납치하는 건 범죄다. 매우 불명예스러운 일이지. 황제 에류마르크는 그래서 네메아가 명령을 거절하면 그걸 이유로 처형할 생각이었던 게다.<end>"

## 580045
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec058-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "しかし、ネメアはその命令を実行し、<line-break>成功させた。施文院から絶世の美女、<line-break>死の天使たちの主、イズを連れ帰った。<line-break>イズの美に心うたれたエリュマルクは<line-break>イズを皇后に立てた。しかし、イズは<line-break>エリュマルクに心を開かなかった。<end>"
EN: "But Nemea carried out the order and succeeded. He returned from the Inscription Order with Iz, the incomparable beauty and mistress of the Angels of Death. Captivated by Iz's beauty, Elmark made her his empress. But Iz never opened her heart to him.<end>"
KO: "하지만 네메아는 그 명령을 실행해 성공시켰다. 시문원에서 절세의 미녀이자 죽음의 천사들의 주인인 이즈를 데려왔다. 이즈의 아름다움에 마음을 빼앗긴 에류마르크는 그녀를 황후로 삼았다. 그러나 이즈는 에류마르크에게 마음을 열지 않았다.<end>"

## 580046
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec058-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "エリュマルクは、イズがネメアに<line-break>心惹かれておるのではないかと疑った。<line-break>ハッ、バカな話じゃ。<line-break>ついにはエリュマルクは、イズを処刑し<line-break>ネメアを、皇后イズと通じていた罪で<line-break>逮捕、処刑するよう命を発した。<end>"
EN: "Elmark suspected that Iz had feelings for Nemea. Hah! What foolishness. At last, Elmark ordered Iz executed, and commanded that Nemea be arrested and put to death for having an affair with Empress Iz.<end>"
KO: "에류마르크는 이즈가 네메아에게 마음을 빼앗긴 게 아닌지 의심했다. 하, 어처구니없는 이야기지. 결국 에류마르크는 이즈를 처형하고 네메아에게 황후 이즈와 내통한 죄를 씌워 체포·처형하라고 명했다.<end>"

## 580047
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec058-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…そして、ネメアは、<line-break>大地震のあと、こつぜんと姿を消した。<line-break>　<line-break>わしには、この一件での、<line-break>ネメアの行動が、まるでわからぬ。<line-break>自由気ままなわしとは違うのじゃろう。<end>"
EN: "And then, after the great earthquake, Nemea suddenly vanished. I cannot make the least sense of his actions in this affair. I suppose he is not a free spirit like me.<end>"
KO: "…그리고 네메아는 대지진 뒤 홀연히 자취를 감췄다. 나는 이번 일에서 네메아가 왜 그런 행동을 했는지 전혀 모르겠구나. 자유분방한 나와는 다른 모양이지.<end>"

## 580048
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec058-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "秩序を守ろうとしたのかもしれぬ。　　<line-break>何かの運命を予兆し、そのために<line-break>行動をとったのかもしれぬ。<line-break>なんにせよ、ひとりの女性に<line-break>決して幸せでない最期をとげさせた。<line-break>…それは許されるべきことではない。<end>"
EN: "Perhaps he was trying to preserve order. Perhaps he foresaw some turn of fate and acted because of it. Whatever the reason, his actions brought one woman to an unhappy end. That must not be forgiven.<end>"
KO: "질서를 지키려 한 것일지도 몰라. 어떤 운명을 예감하고 그 때문에 행동한 것일 수도 있지. 어쨌든 한 여인에게 결코 행복하지 않은 최후를 맞게 했다. …그건 용서받을 일이 아니다.<end>"

## 580049
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec058-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "勇者ネメアは皇帝エリュマルクに　　　<line-break>その才能をうとまれておった。<line-break>　<line-break>エリュマルクはネメアを除こうと、<line-break>実行不可能なさまざまな難事を<line-break>ふりかけた。<end>"
EN: "Emperor Elmark resented the Hero Nemea for his gifts. Wanting Nemea out of the way, Elmark burdened him with all manner of impossible tasks.<end>"
KO: "용사 네메아는 황제 엘류마르크에게 그 재능을 미움받았다. 엘류마르크는 네메아를 제거하려고 실행 불가능한 갖가지 난제를 던졌다.<end>"

## 580050
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec058-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "１２回、数えれば１２回もじゃ。<line-break>そして、ネメアはそのすべてを成し遂げ<line-break>その名声はますます、高くなった。<line-break>わしもネメアのそんな姿に英雄を見た。<line-break>わしはネメアを心底尊敬しておる。<line-break>…１２番目の冒険だけは、別としてな。<end>"
EN: "Twelve times―twelve, if you count them. And Nemea accomplished every one, bringing him ever greater fame. In watching him, I saw a true hero. I respect Nemea from the bottom of my heart... except when it comes to the twelfth adventure.<end>"
KO: "열두 번, 세어 보면 무려 열두 번이지. 그리고 네메아는 그 모든 일을 해냈고 명성은 더욱 높아졌다. 나도 그런 네메아에게서 영웅을 봤네. 진심으로 존경하고 있어. …열두 번째 모험만은 빼고 말이지.<end>"

## 580051
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec058-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "バロルの死後、ロセン軍が攻めてきて、<line-break>ネメアに惨敗したことがあった。<line-break>　<line-break>そのロセンの侵攻は、リベルダムの<line-break>武器商人どもが影で糸を引いていた<line-break>という噂がある。<end>"
EN: "After Balor's death, the army of Rocen invaded and suffered a crushing defeat at Nemea's hands. Rumor has it that Liberdam's arms merchants were pulling the strings behind that invasion.<end>"
KO: "발로르가 죽은 뒤 로센군이 쳐들어왔다가 네메아에게 참패한 적이 있다. 그 로센의 침공은 리벨덤의 무기상들이 뒤에서 조종했다는 소문이 있어.<end>"

## 580052
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec058-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "その後ろには、あの稀代の謀略家<line-break>ファーロスの雌狐とも異名をとる<line-break>ロストール王妃エリスもいたらしい。<line-break>周辺諸国はディンガル帝国を<line-break>よく思っていない。そんなときに<line-break>嫉妬でネメアを追放するとは…。<end>"
EN: "It seems Queen Eris of Rostorl, that peerless schemer known as the Vixen of Pharos, was behind it as well. The neighboring nations bear the Dyneskal Empire no goodwill. And to think Nemea would be exiled out of jealousy at a time like this...<end>"
KO: "그 뒤에는 희대의 모략가, 파로스의 암여우라는 별명까지 가진 로스톨 왕비 에리스도 있었다더군. 주변 국가들은 딘갈 제국을 좋게 보지 않는다. 그런 때 질투 때문에 네메아를 추방하다니….<end>"

## 580053
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec058-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "わざわざ、諸国にスキを見せて　　　<line-break>襲ってくださいと<line-break>言わんばかりではないか！<end>"
EN: "Going out of the way to leave an opening for the other nations is practically inviting them to attack!<end>"
KO: "일부러 여러 나라에 빈틈을 보이면서 덮쳐 달라고 하는 거나 마찬가지 아닌가!<end>"

## 580054
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec058-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "実は、わしも<line-break>ネメア同様、お尋ね者なのじゃ。<line-break>　<line-break>わしには<line-break>ズバズバものを言うクセがある。<line-break>それが、皇帝にうとまれてな。<end>"
EN: "The truth is, I'm wanted too, just like Nemea. I have a habit of speaking my mind without restraint. The emperor took offense at that.<end>"
KO: "사실 나도 네메아처럼 수배자다. 나는 말을 거침없이 하는 버릇이 있지. 그게 황제 눈 밖에 나서 말이야.<end>"

## 580055
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec058-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ああ、言ってなかったやもしれんな。<line-break>わしは、エリュマルク帝に将軍として<line-break>仕えておったこともあったのじゃ。<line-break>直言がうとまれ、処刑されかけたが<line-break>死ぬのはゴメンじゃと脱走して<line-break>また、冒険者に戻ったのよ。<end>"
EN: "Ah, perhaps I never told you. I once served Emperor Elmark as a general. My plain speaking nearly got me executed, but I had no intention of dying, so I escaped and became an adventurer again.<end>"
KO: "아, 말 안 했을지도 모르겠군. 나는 에류마르크 황제를 장군으로 섬긴 적도 있어. 직언이 미움을 사 처형될 뻔했지만 죽는 건 싫어서 탈주하고 다시 모험가가 됐지.<end>"

## 580056
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec058-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ハハハ！　命あっての人生よ！<line-break>わしの人生の教訓を教えてやろう。　<line-break>生きていれば、絶対になんとかなる、<line-break>…と言うほど、人生は甘くはないが<line-break>生きていれば、いいことがある。<end>"
EN: "Hahaha! Life isn't life unless you're alive to live it! Let me teach you the lesson I've learned: I'd like to say that as long as you're alive, everything will always work out... but life isn't so kind. Still, if you live, good things will come.<end>"
KO: "하하하! 목숨이 있어야 인생도 있는 법이지! 내 인생의 교훈을 알려 주마. 살아 있으면 무조건 어떻게든 된다…고 할 만큼 인생이 만만하진 않지만, 살아 있으면 좋은 일도 있다.<end>"

## 580057
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec058-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "例えば、今、こうやって酒が飲める。<line-break>これには、何ものもかえられぬ。<line-break>そうじゃろう、<value:$28>？<end>"
EN: "For instance, here I am, having a drink. Nothing in the world could take the place of that. Wouldn't you agree, <value:$28>?<end>"
KO: "예를 들면 지금 이렇게 술을 마실 수 있지. 이건 무엇과도 바꿀 수 없다. 그렇지, <value:$28>?<end>"

## 580058
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec058-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "人生は酒。<line-break>そして、酒よりも冒険じゃ。　　<line-break>ちと、行ってくるわ。<end>"
EN: "Life is drink, and adventure is better than drink. I'm off for a bit.<end>"
KO: "인생은 술. 그리고 술보다 모험이지. 잠깐 다녀오마.<end>"

## 580059
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec058-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "実はな、やたらと条件のいい仕事があるんだ。<line-break>報酬３００００ギア、期限はなし。<line-break>　<line-break>エンシャントの南東にある死竜の洞窟から<line-break>黄泉の乙女像を持ち帰るだけっていう、<line-break>簡単な依頼だ。<end>"
EN: "Truth is, I've got a job with almost suspiciously good terms. The reward is 30,000 Gea, and there's no deadline. All you have to do is retrieve the Statue of the Underworld Maiden from Dead Dragon Cave, southeast of Ancient. Simple enough.<end>"
KO: "사실 조건이 아주 좋은 일이 하나 있어. 보수 30000기어, 기한 없음. 엔샨트 남동쪽 사룡의 동굴에서 황천의 소녀상을 가져오기만 하면 되는 간단한 의뢰야.<end>"

## 580060
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec058-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "引き受ける<end>"
EN: "Accept<end>"
KO: "수락한다<end>"

## 580061
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec058-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "引き受けない<end>"
EN: "Decline<end>"
KO: "맡지 않는다<end>"

## 580062
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec058-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "けどな、この手のおいしい話には、　<line-break>たいてい裏があるからな。<line-break>なるべくなら関わらない方がいい。<line-break>とは言っても、放っておくのも<line-break>もったいない話だ。<end>"
EN: "But offers this good usually come with a catch. You'd be better off staying out of it if you can. Then again, it'd be a shame to let an opportunity like this go to waste.<end>"
KO: "하지만 이런 맛있는 이야기엔 대개 뒷사정이 있어. 가능하면 얽히지 않는 게 좋아. 그렇다고 그냥 놔두기도 아까운 이야기지만.<end>"

## 580063
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec058-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ま、気が向いたら行ってみな。<line-break>死竜の洞窟の一番奥にあるらしいぜ。<end>"
EN: "Well, if you feel like it, go take a look. It's supposed to be at the very back of Dead Dragon Cave.<end>"
KO: "뭐, 마음이 내키면 가 봐. 사룡의 동굴 가장 깊숙한 곳에 있다더군.<end>"

## 580064
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec058-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お、これは黄泉の乙女像！<line-break>ごくろうさん。<line-break>ほらよ、こいつが報酬だ。<end>"
EN: "Oh, that's the Statue of the Underworld Maiden! Good work. Here, this is your reward.<end>"
KO: "오, 이건 황천의 소녀상! 수고했다. 자, 이게 보수다.<end>"

## 580065
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec058-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "誰も持ってきてくれないんで、<line-break>こりゃあ、ダメかなと思ってた。<line-break>助かったよ。<line-break>なんかあったら、<line-break>またよろしく頼む。<end>"
EN: "No one had brought one in, so I was starting to think it was hopeless. You really helped me out. If anything else comes up, I hope I can count on you again.<end>"
KO: "아무도 가져다주지 않아서 이건 안 되나 했어. 살았군. 무슨 일 있으면 또 부탁할게.<end>"

## 580066
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec058-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>は<line-break>竜殺しの称号を得た。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28> earned the title of Dragonslayer.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>는 용살자의 칭호를 얻었다.<end>"

## 580067
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec058-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "待て。<end>"
EN: "Wait.<end>"
KO: "기다려.<end>"

## 580068
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec058-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "竜の死骸か…。<end>"
EN: "A dragon's corpse...<end>"
KO: "용의 시체인가….<end>"

## 580069
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec058-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "この世界にいる竜は７匹。<line-break>世界を統べる竜王と<line-break>その両翼、翔王、海王の３聖竜。　<line-break>…そして、<line-break>アズラゴーザ、イシュバアル、<line-break>エルアザル、シャンマの４邪竜…。<end>"
EN: "There are seven dragons in this world. The Three Sacred Dragons are the Dragon King, who rules the world, and the Dragon King's two wings: the Soaring King and the Sea King. Then there are the Four Evil Dragons: Azragoza, Ishbaal, Elazar, and Shamma.<end>"
KO: "이 세계의 용은 일곱. 세계를 다스리는 용왕과 그 양익, 상왕과 해왕의 세 성룡…. 그리고 아즈라고자, 이슈바알, 엘아자르, 샨마의 네 사룡…<end>"

## 580070
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec058-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "これまで竜に打ち勝ったものはふたり。<line-break>初代ドワーフ王のボルキスと<line-break>賢王アルキュオネの忘れられた剣士…。<line-break>ボルキスはシャンマを屈服させたのみ。<line-break>こいつは、忘れられた剣士に殺された<line-break>邪竜エルアザルのむくろじゃな…。<end>"
EN: "Only two have ever bested a dragon: Borkis, the first Dwarf King, and the Forgotten Swordsman of Wise King Alcyone. Borkis merely brought Shamma to heel. This is the corpse of Elazar, the Evil Dragon slain by the Forgotten Swordsman.<end>"
KO: "지금까지 용을 이긴 자는 둘. 초대 드워프왕 볼키스와 현왕 알큐오네의 잊힌 검사…. 볼키스는 샨마를 굴복시켰을 뿐. 이건 잊힌 검사에게 죽은 사룡 엘아자르의 유해구나….<end>"

## 580071
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec058-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…まだ、怨念が残っておる。<end>"
EN: "Its hatred still lingers.<end>"
KO: "…아직 원념이 남아 있구나.<end>"

## 580072
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec058-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "同じ冒険者としての助言じゃ。<line-break>この仕事はあきらめて、<line-break>静かに出ていった方がよいぞ。<end>"
EN: "Take some advice from a fellow adventurer: give up this job and leave quietly.<end>"
KO: "같은 모험자로서 하는 조언이야. 이 일은 포기하고 조용히 떠나는 게 좋아.<end>"

## 580073
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec058-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "悪いことは言わん。<line-break>こいつには手を出さぬ方がよい。<end>"
EN: "Take my word for it. You're better off leaving this thing alone.<end>"
KO: "충고하겠는데, 이 녀석에게는 손대지 않는 게 좋아.<end>"

## 580074
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec058-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "こいつはまだ、<line-break>完全には死んでおらぬ。　　<line-break>　<line-break>うかつに近づけば、<line-break>目覚めさせることになろう。<end>"
EN: "This thing isn't completely dead yet. Get too close without due care, and you'll wake it.<end>"
KO: "이 녀석은 아직 완전히 죽지 않았다. 섣불리 다가가면 깨워 버릴지도 모른다.<end>"

## 580075
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec058-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "アンデッド化しておるが、<line-break>邪竜には変わりない。<line-break>その力は、計り知れぬぞ。　　　　　<line-break>もし挑みたいのなら、<line-break>止めはせんが、もっと<line-break>力をつけてからでも遅くはあるまい。<end>"
EN: "It has become undead, but it's still an Evil Dragon. Its power is beyond measure. If you want to challenge it, I won't stop you, but you can always wait until you're stronger.<end>"
KO: "언데드가 되었어도 사룡인 건 변함없다. 그 힘은 헤아릴 수 없을 정도야. 도전하고 싶다면 말리진 않겠지만 좀 더 힘을 기른 뒤라도 늦지 않아.<end>"

## 580076
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec058-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "冒険者をやっているとな、<line-break>時々とんでもない代物に<line-break>ぶち当たることがある。<line-break>まったく、退屈せんわな。<line-break>これだからやめられん。<end>"
EN: "When you're an adventurer, every so often you run into something truly outrageous. It certainly keeps life interesting. That's why I can never give it up.<end>"
KO: "모험자를 하다 보면 가끔 터무니없는 물건과 마주칠 때가 있지. 정말 지루할 틈이 없어. 이래서 그만둘 수가 없다니까.<end>"

## 580077
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec058-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "急いで、しかし動作はゆっくりと<line-break>ここを出るのじゃ。<line-break><value:$28>。<end>"
EN: "Hurry out of here, but move slowly, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "서두르되 움직임은 천천히, 여길 빠져나가자. <value:$28>.<end>"

## 580078
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec058-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なんじゃ、初対面みたいな顔をして。<line-break>静かに来い、<value:$28>！<line-break>急いで、しかし動作はゆっくりと<line-break>ここを出るのじゃ。間違っても<line-break>ダッシュで走ってはならぬ。<end>"
EN: "What's with that look? You'd think we'd never met. Come quietly, <value:$28>! Hurry out of here, but move slowly. Whatever you do, don't sprint.<end>"
KO: "뭐냐, 처음 보는 사람 같은 얼굴을 하고. 조용히 와라, <value:$28>! 서두르되 움직임은 천천히 해서 여길 빠져나가는 거다. 절대로 달려서는 안 된다.<end>"

## 580079
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec058-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ここで会うたも何かの縁じゃ。<line-break>ひとつ名を教えてくれんか。<end>"
EN: "Our meeting here must count for something. Would you tell me your name?<end>"
KO: "여기서 만난 것도 인연이지. 이름 하나 알려 주지 않겠나.<end>"

## 580080
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec058-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうか、<line-break><value:$28>というのか。<line-break>よろしくな。　　　　　　　　　　　　<line-break>わしはアンギルダン。最近は<line-break>リベルダムの酒場にいりびたりじゃ。<line-break>リベルダムに来たら、会いにきてくれ。<end>"
EN: "So you're called <value:$28>. A pleasure. I'm Angeerdan. These days, I spend most of my time at the tavern in Liberdam. Come and see me if you're ever there.<end>"
KO: "그렇군, <value:$28>라고 하나. 잘 부탁하지. 나는 앙길단. 요즘은 리벨덤 술집에 틀어박혀 지내지. 리벨덤에 오면 만나러 와 줘.<end>"

## 580081
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec058-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それではな、<value:$28>。<line-break>また会おうぞ。<end>"
EN: "Farewell for now, <value:$28>. We shall meet again.<end>"
KO: "그럼 잘 있어라, <value:$28>. 또 만나자.<end>"

## 580082
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec058-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "待て。<end>"
EN: "Wait.<end>"
KO: "기다려.<end>"

## 580083
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec058-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "竜の死骸か…。<end>"
EN: "A dragon's carcass...<end>"
KO: "용의 시체인가….<end>"

## 580084
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec058-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "死んだ竜っていや、<line-break>昔、賢王アルキュオネたちに<line-break>倒された邪竜エルアザルだけだ。<line-break>つまり、<line-break>こいつはエルアザルの死体ってわけだ。<end>"
EN: "The only dead dragon I know of is Elazar, the Evil Dragon slain long ago by Wise King Alcyone and the others. Which means this must be Elazar's corpse.<end>"
KO: "죽은 용이라면 옛날 현왕 알큐오네 일행에게 쓰러진 사룡 엘아자르뿐이야. 즉 이건 엘아자르의 시체라는 거지.<end>"

## 580085
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec058-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おーこわ、こいつ…。<line-break>…まだ、怨念が残ってら。<end>"
EN: "Whoa, this thing's scary... Its hatred is still lingering.<end>"
KO: "으, 무서워라 이 녀석…. …아직 원한이 남아 있군.<end>"

## 580086
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec058-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "やっぱり、<line-break>アンデッド化してやがる…。<line-break>こいつは、まだ死んじゃいないぜ。<end>"
EN: "Just as I thought―it's turned undead... This thing isn't quite dead yet.<end>"
KO: "역시 언데드화했군…. 이 녀석은 아직 죽은 게 아니야.<end>"

## 580087
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec058-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なんつっても邪竜だ。<line-break>うかつに近づいて起こしちまったら、<line-break>シャレになんないぜ。<line-break>おとなしく寝かしとくのが<line-break>一番だな。<end>"
EN: "After all, it's an Evil Dragon. If you go blundering too close and wake it up, this'll be no laughing matter. Best to leave it sleeping.<end>"
KO: "어쨌든 사룡이야. 섣불리 다가가 깨웠다간 큰일 나지. 얌전히 재워 두는 게 최고야.<end>"

## 580088
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec058-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "しっかしまた、<line-break>暇つぶしに冒険してたら、<line-break>えらいもんを見つけちまったな。<line-break>ま、ここに長居は無用ってことだ。<line-break>じゃあな。<end>"
EN: "Still, I sure stumbled across one hell of a thing while I was out adventuring to kill time. Anyway, there's no point staying here any longer. See you.<end>"
KO: "참, 심심풀이로 모험하다가 엄청난 걸 찾아버렸군. 뭐, 여기 오래 있을 필요는 없겠지. 그럼 잘 가.<end>"

## 580089
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec058-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "とにかく、ここを出るんだ。<line-break>急いで、しかし動作はゆっくりとな。<end>"
EN: "Anyway, get out of here. Hurry―but move slowly.<end>"
KO: "어쨌든 여길 나가자. 서둘러, 하지만 움직임은 천천히.<end>"

## 580090
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec058-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "しっかしまた、<line-break>暇つぶしに冒険してたら、<line-break>えらいもんを見つけちまったな。<line-break>一度は戦ってみたい気もするが…。<line-break>ま、お前がその気になったら<line-break>誘ってくれよ。じゃ、またな。<end>"
EN: "Still, I sure stumbled across one hell of a thing while I was out adventuring to kill time. Part of me would like to try fighting it... Well, if you decide to take it on, invite me along. See you around.<end>"
KO: "그나저나 심심풀이로 모험하다가 대단한 걸 발견했네. 한 번 싸워 보고 싶기도 하지만…. 네가 마음이 생기면 불러 줘. 그럼 또 보자.<end>"

## 580091
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec058-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いいか、<value:$28>。<line-break>いちおう、冒険者として　　　　<line-break>忠告だけさせてもらう。<line-break>ここで、ウロチョロしすぎると<line-break>邪竜の餌食になる。<line-break>気をつけるんだな。<end>"
EN: "Listen, <value:$28>. As an adventurer, I should at least give you a warning. Poke around here too much and you'll end up as food for the Evil Dragon. Watch yourself.<end>"
KO: "잘 들어, <value:$28>. 일단 모험자로서 충고 하나만 하지. 여기서 너무 돌아다니면 사룡의 먹이가 된다. 조심해.<end>"

## 580092
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec058-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "よ。<line-break>ここで会ったも何かの縁だ。<line-break>ひとつ自己紹介と行こうぜ。<line-break>俺はゼネテス。冒険者だ。<line-break>世界中を旅してる。<line-break>お前さんは？<end>"
EN: "Hey. Since fate's brought us together out here, we might as well introduce ourselves. I'm Xenetes, an adventurer. I travel all over the world. And you are?<end>"
KO: "여어. 여기서 만난 것도 인연이지. 자기소개나 하자고. 난 제네테스, 모험자다. 세계를 돌아다니고 있어. 넌?<end>"

## 580093
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec058-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>…ね。<line-break>なるほど。フフ…　　　　　　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>つまり、ロストールの貴族<line-break><value:$28>・リューガ様って<line-break>わけだ。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>... I see. Heh... So you're the Rostorl noble, <value:$28> Ryuga.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>…군요. 그렇군요. 후후…. 그러니까 로스톨의 귀족 <value:$28> 류가 님이라는 거군요.<end>"

## 580094
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec058-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フフ、それが、こんな所で冒険三昧か。<line-break>エストもそうだが、リューガ家には<line-break>結構、貴族の異端者が多いんだな。<line-break>俺は、結構、変わり者って好きだぜ。<line-break>じゃあな、<value:$28>。<line-break>また会える気がするぜ！<end>"
EN: "Heh. And here you are, adventuring to your heart's content in a place like this. Between you and Est, the Ryuga family seems to produce its fair share of noble misfits. I've always had a soft spot for oddballs. See you around, <value:$28>. Somehow, I think we'll meet again!<end>"
KO: "후후, 그런데 이런 데서 모험 삼매경이라. 에스트도 그렇지만 류가 가문엔 제법 귀족답지 않은 별종이 많군. 난 그런 변종을 꽤 좋아해. 그럼 또 보자, <value:$28>. 다시 만날 것 같은 기분이 드는군!<end>"

## 580095
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec058-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>ね…。<line-break>なるほど？　フフ…。　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>今、話題の<value:$2B>の<line-break><value:$28>様で<line-break>いらっしゃるわけだ。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>, huh...? Interesting. Heh... So you're <value:$28>, the <value:$2B> everyone's talking about.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>군요…. 그렇군요? 후후…. 지금 화제의 <value:$2B>, <value:$28>님이시라는 거군요.<end>"

## 580096
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec058-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フフ、会えてうれしいぜ。　　<line-break>じゃあな、<value:$28><line-break>縁があったらまた会おうや。<end>"
EN: "Heh, glad I got to meet you. See you around, <value:$28>. If we get the chance, let's meet again.<end>"
KO: "후후, 만나서 반갑다. 그럼 잘 가, <value:$28>. 인연이 있으면 또 만나자고.<end>"

## 580097
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec058-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>ね。<line-break>あんた、いい目をしてるよ。　　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>俺の叔母貴がよく言ってる。<line-break>目の輝きは魂の輝きを表すってね。<line-break>あんたの目には無限の可能性を感じる。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>, eh? You've got good eyes. My aunt always says the light in a person's eyes reflects the light of the soul. I see limitless potential in yours.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>구나. 너, 눈빛이 좋네. 우리 숙모가 자주 말했어. 눈의 빛은 영혼의 빛을 나타낸다고. 네 눈에서는 무한한 가능성이 느껴져.<end>"

## 580098
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec058-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おお、<value:$28>か。<line-break>ちょうどいい。<line-break>　<line-break>この前の、邪竜の死体の件じゃが、<line-break>エンシャントのギルドに、死竜の洞窟を<line-break>探索する仕事の依頼が出とってだな…<end>"
EN: "Ah, <value:$28>. Good timing. About that business with the Evil Dragon's corpse the other day: a job has been posted at the Guild in Ancient to explore Dead Dragon Cave...<end>"
KO: "오, <value:$28>인가. 마침 잘 왔군. 지난번 사룡 시체 건인데, 엔샨트 길드에 사룡의 동굴을 탐색하는 의뢰가 나와 있다네….<end>"

## 580099
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec058-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "これの依頼人名が、うさん臭いんじゃ。<line-break>見ろ。システィーナとある。神話の<line-break>黄泉の乙女システィーナと同名じゃ。<line-break>その後、調べていったら、依頼主の<line-break>使者がシャリとかチャリとかいう<line-break>子供だったという。<end>"
EN: "The client's name is suspicious. Look. It says Sistina―the same name as the maiden of the underworld in myth. I looked into it, and apparently the client's messenger was a child called Shalli or Challi or something.<end>"
KO: "이 의뢰인 이름이 수상해. 봐라, 시스티나라고 적혀 있지. 신화 속 황천의 소녀 시스티나와 같은 이름이야. 그 뒤 조사해 보니 의뢰인의 사자는 샤리인지 차리인지 하는 아이였다더군.<end>"

## 580100
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec058-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いたずらかと思えばそうでもない。<line-break>ギルドに多額の預託金と保証金は<line-break>納められている。のう、どう見る？<end>"
EN: "I thought it might be a prank, but apparently not. Considerable sums have been lodged with the Guild as a deposit and security bond. What do you make of it?<end>"
KO: "장난이라고 보기엔 그렇지도 않아. 길드에 큰 액수의 예탁금과 보증금이 들어와 있다. 자, 어떻게 보나?<end>"

## 580101
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec058-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "わしが思うに、闇の者が邪竜の復活を<line-break>もくろんで、冒険者の精気を<line-break>吸わせておったのじゃろう。<line-break>闇がまた、動き出したか…。<line-break><value:$28>、お主も<line-break>くれぐれも気をつけてな。<end>"
EN: "I reckon the forces of darkness were plotting to resurrect the Evil Dragon and had it feeding on adventurers' life force. Could darkness be stirring again...? <value:$28>, you be very careful too.<end>"
KO: "내 생각엔 어둠의 자들이 사룡의 부활을 꾀하며 모험가들의 정기를 빨아먹게 한 모양이군. 어둠이 다시 움직이기 시작했나…. <value:$28>, 너도 각별히 조심하게.<end>"

## 580102
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec058-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "指名手配、<line-break>反逆者アンギルダン・ゼイエン<line-break>罪状：皇帝に対する不服従<end>"
EN: "Wanted: Rebel Angeerdan Zeien. Charge: Disobedience to the Emperor.<end>"
KO: "지명수배. 반역자 앙길단 제이엔. 죄목: 황제에 대한 불복종.<end>"

## 580103
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec058-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おお、<value:$28>か。<line-break>お前も飲みにきたのか。<end>"
EN: "Oh, <value:$28>. Here for a drink too?<end>"
KO: "오, <value:$28>인가. 너도 마시러 왔나.<end>"

## 580104
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec058-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おお、<value:$28>か。<line-break>今日は昔のことが思い出されてな。<line-break>わしの戦友の話、聞いてくれぬか？<end>"
EN: "Ah, <value:$28>. I've found myself thinking of the old days today. Would you hear a story about an old comrade-in-arms of mine?<end>"
KO: "오, <value:$28>인가. 오늘은 옛날 생각이 나는군. 내 전우 이야기를 들어 주겠나?<end>"

## 590001
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec059-part99.toml

## 590002
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec059-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "竜の死骸ですね…。<end>"
EN: "A dragon's carcass...<end>"
KO: "용의 사체네요….<end>"

## 590003
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec059-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "この世界にいる竜は７匹。<line-break>竜王、翔王、海王の３聖竜。　　　<line-break>　<line-break>…そして<line-break>アズラゴーザ、イシュバアル、<line-break>エルアザル、シャンマの４邪竜…。<end>"
EN: "There are seven dragons in this world. The three Sacred Dragons are the Dragon King, the Soaring King, and the Sea King. And then there are the four Evil Dragons: Azragoza, Ishbaal, Elazar, and Shamma.<end>"
KO: "이 세계의 용은 일곱. 용왕, 상왕, 해왕의 세 성룡. …그리고 아즈라고자, 이슈바알, 엘아자르, 샨마의 네 사룡…<end>"

## 590004
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec059-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "これまで竜に打ち勝ったものはふたり。<line-break>賢王アルキュオネの忘れられた剣士と、<line-break>初代ドワーフ王のボルキスのみ。<line-break>ボルキスは邪竜を殺していませんから、<line-break>これは忘れられた剣士に殺された<line-break>邪竜エルアザルの死骸ですね。<end>"
EN: "Only two people have ever defeated a dragon: Wise King Alcyone's Forgotten Swordsman and Borkis, the first Dwarf King. Borkis never killed an Evil Dragon, so this must be the carcass of the Evil Dragon Elazar, slain by the Forgotten Swordsman.<end>"
KO: "지금까지 용을 이긴 자는 둘뿐입니다. 현왕 알큐오네의 잊힌 검사와 초대 드워프 왕 볼키스. 볼키스는 사룡을 죽이지 않았으니, 이건 잊힌 검사에게 죽은 사룡 엘아자르의 시체겠군요.<end>"

## 590005
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec059-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…まだ怨念が残ってます。<line-break>どうやら<line-break>アンデッド化しているようです。<end>"
EN: "Its hatred still lingers. It seems to have become undead.<end>"
KO: "…아직 원념이 남아 있습니다. 아무래도 언데드가 된 모양입니다.<end>"

## 590006
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec059-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "邪竜エルアザルは、<line-break>復活の時の闇の影響を受けて<line-break>いまにも復活しそうですね。<line-break>いずれは、これも<line-break>なんとかしないといけませんが、<line-break>今は保留しておきましょう。<end>"
EN: "The Evil Dragon Elazar has been affected by the darkness from the time of the revival and looks ready to revive at any moment. We'll have to deal with this eventually, but for now, let's leave it be.<end>"
KO: "사룡 엘아자르는 부활할 때의 어둠에 영향을 받아 금방이라도 부활할 것 같군요. 언젠가는 이것도 해결해야겠지만 지금은 보류하죠.<end>"

## 590007
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec059-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あなたも邪竜にケンカをうって<line-break>勝てる自信ができるまで<line-break>ここには近づかない方がいいですよ。<end>"
EN: "You should stay away from this place until you're confident you can pick a fight with an Evil Dragon and win.<end>"
KO: "당신도 사룡에게 싸움을 걸어 이길 자신이 생길 때까지는 여기에 가까이 오지 않는 게 좋아요.<end>"

## 590008
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec059-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "この邪竜は、こうやって<line-break>生き物の精気を吸い取って<line-break>再び、立つ力を蓄えてるんです。<line-break>うかつに動くと、見つかって<line-break>さっきのピクシーのように<line-break>精気を吸い取られますよ。<end>"
EN: "This Evil Dragon drains the life force from living creatures, storing up enough strength to rise again. Make one careless move and it'll spot you and drain your life force, just like that Pixie earlier.<end>"
KO: "이 사룡은 이렇게 생물의 정기를 빨아들여 다시 일어설 힘을 모으고 있습니다. 함부로 움직이면 들켜서 아까 픽시처럼 정기를 빼앗길 거예요.<end>"

## 590009
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec059-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さ、急いで！　…と言っても　　　<line-break>ゆっくりと、静かに<line-break>ここを出ましょう。<end>"
EN: "Come on, hurry! Though by “hurry,” I mean let's get out of here slowly and quietly.<end>"
KO: "자, 서둘러! …라고 해도 천천히, 조용히 여기서 나가자.<end>"

## 590010
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec059-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "邪竜エルアザル、<line-break>復活の時の闇の影響を受けて、<line-break>いまにも復活しそうですね。<line-break>いずれは、あれも<line-break>なんとかしないといけませんが、<line-break>今は保留しておきましょう。<end>"
EN: "The Evil Dragon Elazar has been affected by the darkness at the time of the resurrection and looks as though it could revive at any moment. Eventually, we'll have to do something about that one too, but let's leave it be for now.<end>"
KO: "사룡 엘아자르, 부활할 때의 어둠에 영향을 받아 금방이라도 부활할 것 같군요. 언젠가는 저것도 해결해야겠지만 지금은 보류하죠.<end>"

## 590011
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec059-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あなたも邪竜にケンカをうって<line-break>勝てる自信ができるまで<line-break>あそこでは静かにしてくださいね。　　<end>"
EN: "Until you're confident you can pick a fight with an Evil Dragon and win, please keep a low profile there.<end>"
KO: "당신도 사룡에게 싸움을 걸어 이길 자신이 생길 때까지 거기서는 조용히 있어 주세요.<end>"

## 590012
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec059-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ディンガル帝国の傭兵募集だ。<line-break>詳細は機密でわからないが、<line-break>どうだ、やるかい？<end>"
EN: "The Dyneskal Empire is recruiting mercenaries. The details are secret, so I don't know much about the job. What do you say? Interested?<end>"
KO: "딩갈 제국의 용병 모집이다. 자세한 내용은 기밀이라 모르지만, 어때, 할래?<end>"

## 590013
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec059-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "引き受ける<end>"
EN: "Accept<end>"
KO: "수락한다<end>"

## 590014
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec059-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "引き受けない<end>"
EN: "Decline<end>"
KO: "맡지 않는다<end>"

## 590015
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec059-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうこなくちゃ。<line-break>よしよし、今、登録して　　　　　　　<line-break>紹介状を送るからな…。<end>"
EN: "That's what I like to hear. All right, I'll register you now and send over a letter of introduction...<end>"
KO: "그래야지. 좋아, 지금 등록하고 소개장을 보낼 테니까…<end>"

## 590016
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec059-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "って、ちょっと待った。<line-break>あんた、アキュリュースの傭兵にも　　<line-break>なっているじゃないか！<line-break>敵味方を、かけもちするのはよくない。<line-break>どちらか片方にしてくれ。<line-break>どっちにするんだい？<end>"
EN: "Hold on a moment. You're already working as a mercenary for Aqyurius! You can't hire on with both sides of a war. You'll have to choose one. Which will it be?<end>"
KO: "잠깐만. 당신 아큐류스의 용병도 하고 있잖아! 적과 아군을 동시에 맡는 건 좋지 않아. 둘 중 한쪽만 골라 줘. 어느 쪽으로 할 거야?<end>"

## 590017
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec059-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "やっぱりアキュリュース側<end>"
EN: "Side with Aqyurius after all<end>"
KO: "역시 아큐류스 쪽<end>"

## 590018
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec059-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "心機一転ディンガル側<end>"
EN: "Make a fresh start with Dyneskal<end>"
KO: "심기일전 딘갈 측<end>"

## 590019
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec059-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それじゃ、今まで通り<line-break>アキュリュース側ってことで<line-break>よろしく頼むぜ、<value:$28>。<end>"
EN: "All right, you'll stay with Aqyurius, same as before. I'm counting on you, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "그럼 지금까지처럼 아큐류스 편이라는 걸로 잘 부탁한다, <value:$28>.<end>"

## 590020
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec059-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "って、ちょっと待った。<line-break>あんた、ロストールの傭兵にも　　　　<line-break>なっているじゃないか！<line-break>敵味方を、かけもちするのはよくない。<line-break>どちらか片方にしてくれ。<line-break>どっちにするんだい？<end>"
EN: "Hold on a moment. You're already working as a mercenary for Rostorl! You can't hire on with both sides of a war. You'll have to choose one. Which will it be?<end>"
KO: "잠깐만. 당신 로스톨의 용병도 하고 있잖아! 적과 아군을 동시에 맡는 건 좋지 않아. 둘 중 한쪽만 골라 줘. 어느 쪽으로 할 거야?<end>"

## 590021
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec059-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "やっぱり、今まで通り<end>"
EN: "Keep things as they are after all<end>"
KO: "역시 지금까지대로<end>"

## 590022
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec059-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "何がなんでも、ディンガル側<end>"
EN: "Side with Dyneskal no matter what<end>"
KO: "무슨 일이 있어도 딩갈 편<end>"

## 590023
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec059-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それじゃ、今まで通り<line-break>ロストール、アキュリュース側ってことで<line-break>よろしく頼むぜ、<value:$28>。<end>"
EN: "All right, you'll stay with Rostorl and Aqyurius, same as before. I'm counting on you, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "그럼 지금까지처럼 로스톨·아큐류스 편으로 부탁한다, <value:$28>.<end>"

## 590024
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec059-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<if><value:$2A><greater-equal>50じゃ、書類の方は送っとくから、<line-break>ディンガル政庁の方へ行ってくれ。　　<line-break>そこで詳しい話があるはずだ。<line-break>今後はディンガル兵ってことで。<line-break>んじゃ、よろしく頼んだぜ。<line-break><value:$2B>の<value:$28>。<end>じゃ、書類の方は送っとくから、<line-break>ディンガル政庁の方へ行ってくれ。　　<line-break>そこで詳しい話があるはずだ。<line-break>今後はディンガル兵ってことで。<line-break>んじゃ、よろしく頼んだぜ。<line-break><value:$28>。<end>"
EN: "<if><value:$2A><greater-equal>50I'll send the papers along, so report to the Dyneskal Government Office. You should get the full story there. From now on, you're a Dyneskal soldier.
