I'm counting on you, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "<if><value:$2A><greater-equal>50그럼 서류는 보내 둘 테니 딩갈 정청으로 가 줘. 거기서 자세한 이야기를 들을 수 있을 거야. 앞으로는 딩갈 병사라는 걸로. 그럼 잘 부탁한다. <value:$2B>의 <value:$28>.<end>그럼 서류는 보내 둘 테니 딩갈 정청으로 가 줘. 거기서 자세한 이야기를 들을 수 있을 거야. 앞으로는 딩갈 병사라는 걸로. 그럼 잘 부탁한다. <value:$28>.<end>"

## 590025
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec059-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "って、ちょっと待った。<line-break>あんた、ロストールの傭兵にも　　　　<line-break>なっているじゃないか！<line-break>敵味方を、かけもちするのはよくない。<line-break>どちらか片方にしてくれ。<line-break>どっちにするんだい？<end>"
EN: "Hold on a moment. You're already working as a mercenary for Rostorl! You can't hire on with both sides of a war. You'll have to choose one. Which will it be?<end>"
KO: "잠깐만. 당신 로스톨의 용병도 하고 있잖아! 적과 아군을 동시에 맡는 건 좋지 않아. 둘 중 한쪽만 골라 줘. 어느 쪽으로 할 거야?<end>"

## 590026
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec059-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "やっぱりロストール側<end>"
EN: "Side with Rostorl after all<end>"
KO: "역시 로스톨 측<end>"

## 590027
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec059-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "心機一転ディンガル側<end>"
EN: "Make a fresh start with Dyneskal<end>"
KO: "심기일전 딘갈 측<end>"

## 590028
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec059-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それじゃ、今まで通り<line-break>ロストール側ってことで<line-break>よろしく頼むぜ、<value:$28>。<end>"
EN: "All right, you'll stay with Rostorl, same as before. I'm counting on you, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "그럼 지금까지처럼 로스톨 편이라는 걸로 잘 부탁한다, <value:$28>.<end>"

## 590029
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec059-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうかい。ま、傭兵ってのも<line-break>しがらみが多くて、考えもんだからな。<line-break>この話はここまでだ。<end>"
EN: "Fair enough. Mercenary work comes with plenty of entanglements, so it's worth thinking twice about. Let's leave it at that.<end>"
KO: "그런가. 뭐, 용병도 얽매이는 게 많아서 생각해 볼 문제지. 이 이야기는 여기까지다.<end>"

## 590030
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec059-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ああ、<value:$28>だな。<line-break>手紙がきてるよ。<end>"
EN: "Ah, <value:$28>. There's a letter for you.<end>"
KO: "아, <value:$28>이군. 편지가 와 있어.<end>"

## 590031
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec059-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "久しいな。アンギルダンだ。　　　　　　<line-break><value:$28>、元気でいるか？<line-break>わしは、ますます元気だ。<line-break>　<line-break>お主の活躍を遠くで聞き<line-break>うれしく思っておった。<line-break>さて、本題に入ろう。<end>"
EN: "It has been a long time. This is Angeerdan. <value:$28>, I hope you are well. I am in better health than ever. Word of your exploits has reached me even from afar, and I was glad to hear it. Now, to the matter at hand.<end>"
KO: "오랜만이군. 앙길단이다. <value:$28>, 잘 지냈나? 나는 더욱 건강하다. 멀리서 네 활약을 듣고 기뻤지. 자, 본론으로 들어가자.<end>"

## 590032
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec059-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "わしがディンガル帝国の朱雀将軍　　　　<line-break>南方攻略軍司令官となったことは<line-break>お主も知っているかと思う。<line-break>　<line-break>このたび、皇帝ネメア様より<line-break>ロストール攻略の許可を得て、<line-break>いよいよ、南征を開始することになった。<end>"
EN: "I expect you have heard that I have become the Dyneskal Empire's Vermilion Bird General and commander of the Southern Campaign Army. Emperor Nemea has now authorized the Rostorl Campaign, and at last we are to begin our southern expedition.<end>"
KO: "내가 딩갈 제국의 주작장군이자 남방공략군 사령관이 된 건 너도 알고 있겠지. 이번에 황제 네메아 님께 로스톨 공략 허가를 받아 드디어 남정을 시작하게 됐다.<end>"

## 590033
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec059-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "東のカルラには負けてはおられんからな。<line-break>ついては、お主の力を借りたい。<line-break>副将待遇で迎える。わしのとなりで活躍せよ。<line-break>　<line-break>エンシャントの政庁に来てくれ。<line-break>よい返事を待っている。<line-break>　<line-break>　　　　　　　　　　　アンギルダン<end>"
EN: "I cannot afford to be outdone by Karula in the east. That is why I want your help. I will receive you with the rank of deputy commander. Come distinguish yourself at my side. Come to the Ancient Government Hall. I look forward to a favorable reply. Angeerdan<end>"
KO: "동쪽의 카를라에게 질 수는 없으니 말이다. 그래서 그대의 힘을 빌리고 싶다. 부장 대우로 맞이하겠다. 내 곁에서 활약해 다오. 엔샨트의 정청으로 와 주게. 좋은 대답을 기다리겠다. 앙길단<end>"

## 590034
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec059-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "朱雀将軍から承っております。<line-break>どうぞお通りください！<end>"
EN: "We've received word from the Vermilion Bird General. Please go on through!<end>"
KO: "주작장군께 말씀 들었습니다. 들어가십시오!<end>"

## 590035
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec059-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "よく来てくれた、　　　　　　　　<line-break><value:$28>。<line-break>　<line-break>いよいよ、ロストールへ進攻する。<line-break>ついては、お主に、わしの副将に<line-break>なってほしいのだ。<end>"
EN: "I'm glad you came, <value:$28>. At last, we march on Rostorl. I want you to serve as my second-in-command.<end>"
KO: "잘 와줬다, <value:$28>. 드디어 로스톨로 진공한다. 그래서 네가 내 부장이 되어줬으면 한다.<end>"

## 590036
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec059-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お主のことなら、<line-break>冒険者時代によく知っておる。<line-break>お主がおれば、百人力なのじゃが…。<end>"
EN: "I came to know you well in your adventuring days. With you at my side, I'd have the strength of a hundred soldiers...<end>"
KO: "자네라면 모험자 시절부터 잘 알고 있지. 자네가 있으면 백 명 몫일 텐데….<end>"

## 590037
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec059-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "引き受ける<end>"
EN: "Accept<end>"
KO: "수락한다<end>"

## 590038
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec059-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "断る<end>"
EN: "Refuse<end>"
KO: "거절한다<end>"

## 590039
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec059-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうか、お主さえ来れば<line-break>他に待つものはない。<line-break>さっそく、出撃じゃ！<end>"
EN: "Good. Now that you're here, there's no one else to wait for. We march at once!<end>"
KO: "그런가, 네가 왔으니 더 기다릴 사람은 없다. 당장 출격이다!<end>"

## 590040
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec059-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "皇帝ネメアの世界統一構想のもと　　　<line-break>南方攻略軍司令、朱雀将軍アンギルダンが　<line-break>ついにロストール攻略に動き出した。　　<end>"
EN: "As part of Emperor Nemea's plan to unite the world, Angeerdan, the Vermilion Bird General and commander of the Southern Campaign Army, at last launched the Rostorl Campaign.<end>"
KO: "황제 네메아의 세계 통일 구상 아래 남방 공략군 사령관, 주작장군 앙길단이 마침내 로스톨 공략에 나섰다.<end>"

## 590041
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec059-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　帝国軍が大兵力を投入すると　　　　　<line-break>　　予想したロストール側は、分断の山脈を　　<line-break>　　　越えてくるとは考えず、内海側の　　　　<line-break>　海路を封鎖し、敵の上陸時を叩く作戦に出た。<line-break>　これに対し、ロストール軍副指令ゼネテスは　<line-break>　　帝国が分断の山脈を越えてくると主張、　　<line-break>　山脈がわに兵を割いて防衛線を築くことを　　<line-break>　　提案したが、軍議で即時に却下された。　　<end>"
EN: "Expecting the Empire to commit a vast force, Rostorl ruled out an advance across the Dividing Mountains. Instead, it blockaded the sea routes on the inland-sea side, planning to strike as the enemy landed. Xenetes, deputy commander of the Rostorl Army, argued that the Empire would cross the mountains and proposed assigning troops there to establish a defensive line. The war council rejected his proposal outright.<end>"
KO: "제국군이 대병력을 투입할 것으로 예상한 로스톨 측은 분단의 산맥을 넘어오리라고는 생각하지 않고 내해 쪽 해로를 봉쇄해 적이 상륙할 때 공격하는 작전을 세웠다. 이에 로스톨군 부사령 제네테스는 제국군이 분단의 산맥을 넘을 것이라 주장하며 산맥 쪽에 병력을 나눠 방어선을 구축하자고 제안했으나 군의에서 즉시 기각되었다.<end>"

## 590042
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec059-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　しかし、このゼネテスの予測は正しく、　　<line-break>　　　　アンギルダンは少数の精兵で　　　　　<line-break>　　　分断の山脈を越えると、大街道を　　　　<line-break>　　　　電撃的な早さで行軍してきた。　　　　<line-break>　　　　分断の山脈を軍隊が越えたのは　　　　<line-break>　　　　　　歴史上初の壮挙である。　　　　　<line-break>　　　　　こうしてディンガル軍は　　　　　　<line-break>　　　王都ロストール攻略に王手をかけた。　　<end>"
EN: "But Xenetes had predicted correctly. Angeerdan crossed the Dividing Mountains with a small force of elite troops, then advanced along the great highway with lightning speed. Never before in history had an army crossed those mountains. With this extraordinary feat, the Dyneskal Army brought the Rostorl Campaign to the brink of victory.<end>"
KO: "하지만 제네테스의 예측은 정확했다. 앙길단은 소수 정예병으로 분단의 산맥을 넘어 대가도를 번개 같은 속도로 행군해 왔다. 군대가 분단의 산맥을 넘은 것은 역사상 최초의 위업이었다. 이렇게 딩갈군은 왕도 로스톨 공략을 눈앞에 두게 되었다.<end>"

## 590043
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec059-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　事ここに及び、ロストール軍総司令　　　<line-break>　　　　ノヴィンは急きょ、軍を反転、　　　　<line-break>　　　アンギルダン率いるディンガル軍を　　　<line-break>　　　　　迎え撃つべく、急行した。　　　　　<end>"
EN: "Faced with this turn of events, Novin, commander-in-chief of the Rostorl Army, hastily reversed course and rushed to meet the Dyneskal Army under Angeerdan.<end>"
KO: "사태가 이 지경에 이르자 로스톨군 총사령관 노빈은 급히 군을 돌려 앙길단이 이끄는 딘갈군을 맞아 싸우기 위해 급행했다.<end>"

## 590044
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec059-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "どうにもロストールの将軍は無能よの。<line-break>これでは、その下で戦う<line-break>ロストールの兵がかわいそうだ。<line-break>ロストール軍はわしに戦いを挑むべく<line-break>隊列がバラバラになるのもかまわずに<line-break>夜を日に継いで急行軍で来とるという。<end>"
EN: "Rostorl's generals truly are incompetent. I pity the soldiers who must fight under them. I hear the Rostorl Army is force-marching here day and night to challenge me, without a thought for its formations falling apart.<end>"
KO: "아무리 봐도 로스톨의 장군은 무능하구먼. 이래서는 그 밑에서 싸우는 로스톨 병사들이 불쌍해. 로스톨군은 나와 싸우겠다며 대열이 흐트러지는 것도 아랑곳하지 않고 밤낮없이 급행군해 오고 있다더군.<end>"

## 590045
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec059-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "むざむざ、やられに来るようなものだ。<line-break>最初の一手を間違えたからには、<line-break>もう、ロストールには勝機はない。<line-break>どうやら連中は、地獄へ送って<line-break>頭も身体も冷たくしてやらねば、<line-break>冷静にものを考えられぬと見た。<end>"
EN: "They may as well be marching here to die. Having blundered with their opening move, Rostorl has no hope of victory. It seems they cannot think clearly until I send them to hell and cool both their heads and their bodies.<end>"
KO: "이건 스스로 죽으러 오는 거나 마찬가지다. 첫 수를 잘못 뒀으니 로스톨에 승산은 없어. 녀석들은 지옥으로 보내 머리도 몸도 차갑게 만들어 줘야 냉정하게 생각할 수 있나 보군.<end>"

## 590046
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec059-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "皆の者！　出撃だ！<line-break>さんざんに破り、ディンガル帝国の名を<line-break>恐怖とともに奴らの脳髄にたたき込め！<end>"
EN: "All of you, move out! Crush them utterly, and hammer the name of the Dyneskal Empire, together with the terror it inspires, into their very brains!<end>"
KO: "전원 출격이다! 철저히 짓밟고 딘갈 제국의 이름을 공포와 함께 놈들의 뇌리에 새겨 넣어라!<end>"

## 590047
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec059-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>、よくやった。<line-break>お主が最高殊勲者じゃ！<line-break>　<line-break>この功をもって、わしは<line-break>お主をディンガルの騎士に推薦し、<line-break>ここで仮叙任を行おうと思う。<end>"
EN: "Well done, <value:$28>. Your service was the greatest of all! In recognition of this deed, I shall recommend you for knighthood in Dyneskal and provisionally invest you here and now.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>, 잘했다. 자네가 최고 공훈자다! 이 공적으로 자네를 딘갈 기사로 추천하고 여기서 임시 서임을 하려 한다.<end>"

## 590048
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec059-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ひざまづくがよい。<end>"
EN: "Kneel.<end>"
KO: "무릎을 꿇어라.<end>"

## 590049
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec059-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "我、朱雀将軍アンギルダンは<line-break>皇帝ネメア・ランガスターの　　　　<line-break>代理として<line-break>戦場のことゆえ略式ではあるが、<line-break>汝、<value:$28>を<line-break>ディンガル帝国黒鎧騎士に叙任する。<end>"
EN: "I, Angeerdan, Vermilion Bird General, acting on behalf of Emperor Nemea Lancaster, hereby invest you, <value:$28>, as a Black-Armored Knight of the Dyneskal Empire. As this is a battlefield, the rite must be abbreviated.<end>"
KO: "나 주작장군 앙길단은 황제 네메아 랑가스터를 대신하여, 전장인 까닭에 약식이지만 그대 <value:$28>를 딩갈 제국 흑개 기사에 임명한다.<end>"

## 590050
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec059-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "スキル<line-break>グロリアシルドを手に入れた。<end>"
EN: "Acquired the skill Gloria Shield.<end>"
KO: "스킬 글로리아 실드를 손에 넣었다.<end>"

## 590051
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec059-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そして、これは恩賞じゃ。<line-break>さらに励んでくれ。<end>"
EN: "And this is your reward. Keep up the good work.<end>"
KO: "그리고 이것은 포상이다. 더욱 힘써 주게.<end>"

## 590052
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec059-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>、よくやった。<line-break>すばらしい戦績じゃ！<line-break>　<line-break>この功をもって、わしは<line-break>お主をディンガルの騎士に推薦し、<line-break>ここで仮叙任を行おうと思う。<end>"
EN: "Well done, <value:$28>. A splendid record! In recognition of this achievement, I shall recommend you for knighthood in Dyneskal and provisionally invest you here and now.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>, 잘했다. 훌륭한 전과다! 이 공적으로 나는 자네를 딘갈의 기사로 추천하고 여기서 임시 서임을 하려 한다.<end>"

## 590053
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec059-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>、<line-break>まずまずの戦功じゃな。<line-break>　<line-break>これは恩賞じゃ。<line-break>さらに、励んでくれ。<line-break>期待しておるぞ。<end>"
EN: "A respectable showing, <value:$28>. Here is your reward. Keep striving. I expect great things from you.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>, 제법 전공을 세웠구나. 이건 포상이다. 더욱 힘써라. 기대하고 있겠다.<end>"

## 590054
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec059-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "このようなことも<line-break>あるものなのかもしれんな。<end>"
EN: "Perhaps such things do happen.<end>"
KO: "이런 일도 있을 수 있는 법이지.<end>"

## 590055
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec059-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さて、ロストールの本軍は叩いたが<line-break>まだ若干の王都守備軍が残っておる。<line-break>　<line-break>恐れるほどの敵ではないが、<line-break>地下王国を軍隊が通れないという制約上<line-break>包囲されたときには援軍はない。<end>"
EN: "Now, we have defeated Rostorl's main army, but some of the capital's garrison remains. They are nothing to fear, but an army cannot pass through the Underground Kingdom. If we are surrounded, no reinforcements can reach us.<end>"
KO: "자, 로스톨 본군은 무찔렀지만 아직 왕도 수비군이 조금 남아 있다. 두려워할 적은 아니지만 지하왕국을 군대가 통과할 수 없다는 제약 때문에 포위당하면 원군은 없다.<end>"

## 590056
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec059-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "物資の補給も断たれ、<line-break>我々は危機に陥るじゃろう。<line-break>　<line-break>つまり、奴らが動く前に、<line-break>こちらは辺りから物資を徴発して<line-break>備えを完全にしておく必要がある。<end>"
EN: "Our supply lines would also be cut, leaving us in grave danger. Before the enemy moves, we must requisition supplies from the surrounding area and complete our preparations.<end>"
KO: "물자 보급도 끊기면 우리는 위기에 빠질 것이네. 즉, 놈들이 움직이기 전에 이 주변에서 물자를 징발해 대비를 완전히 해 둘 필요가 있다.<end>"

## 590057
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec059-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "潰走しつつある敵の掃討作戦は行わず、<line-break>各隊は早急に付近の村から<line-break>物資を強制徴発してもらいたい。<end>"
EN: "Do not pursue and destroy the fleeing enemy. Every unit is to requisition supplies by force from the nearby villages without delay.<end>"
KO: "도주 중인 적을 추격하는 작전은 하지 않는다. 각 부대는 서둘러 인근 마을에서 물자를 강제 징발하도록.<end>"

## 590058
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec059-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>殿の言うとおりです。<line-break>それは略奪行為です。<line-break>将軍、思いとどまってください。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28> is right. That is nothing but pillage. General, please reconsider.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>님의 말씀이 맞습니다. 그건 약탈입니다. 장군, 재고해 주십시오.<end>"

## 590059
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec059-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "戦争は血塗られた道よ。　　　<line-break>いかに取り繕おうともな。<line-break>　<line-break>せめて、流される血が少ないように<line-break>努力するまでじゃ。<line-break>責任はわしが持つ。任務の遂行を命ず。<end>"
EN: "War is a bloodstained road, however we may dress it up. All we can do is strive to spill as little blood as possible. I will bear the responsibility. Carry out your orders.<end>"
KO: "전쟁은 피로 물든 길이다. 아무리 꾸며도 말이지. 그저 흘릴 피가 조금이라도 적도록 노력할 뿐이다. 책임은 내가 진다. 임무 수행을 명한다.<end>"

## 590060
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec059-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>様、<line-break>お気持ちはお察しいたします。<line-break>ですが、命令は遂行なさらないと…。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>, I understand how you feel. But the order must still be carried out.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>님, 심정은 이해합니다. 하지만 명령은 수행하셔야….<end>"

## 590061
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec059-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "て、敵襲！<end>"
EN: "E-enemy attack!<end>"
KO: "저, 적습이다!<end>"

## 590062
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec059-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "徴発隊が出払って手薄になった<line-break>中枢部に、敗残兵をまとめて<line-break>ロストール軍が攻め込んできました！<end>"
EN: "The Rostorl Army has rallied its scattered remnants and attacked our command center while the requisition parties are away and our defenses are stretched thin!<end>"
KO: "징발대가 자리를 비워 허술해진 중심부로 패잔병을 모은 로스톨군이 공격해 왔습니다!<end>"

## 590063
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec059-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "バカな…！<line-break>あそこまで崩れ去った兵を<line-break>まとめあげただと？<end>"
EN: "Impossible! How could an army routed that completely have been rallied?<end>"
KO: "말도 안 돼…! 저렇게 무너진 병사들을 다시 규합했다고?<end>"

## 590064
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec059-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ありえない…！<line-break>敵将は誰だ！？<end>"
EN: "That cannot be! Who commands them?<end>"
KO: "말도 안 돼…! 적장은 누구냐!?<end>"

## 590065
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec059-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "総司令ノヴィン・ファーロスの息子<line-break>ゼネテスです！<line-break>　<line-break>奇襲を受けた中枢部隊は壊滅！<line-break>アンギルダン様の生死は不明です！<end>"
EN: "Xenetes, son of Commander-in-Chief Novin Pharos! The command units were taken by surprise and annihilated! Lord Angeerdan's fate is unknown!<end>"
KO: "총사령 노빈 파로스의 아들 제네테스입니다! 기습을 받은 중추부대는 괴멸! 앙길단 님의 생사는 불명입니다!<end>"

## 590066
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec059-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "出撃！　<value:$28>様に続け！<line-break>アンギルダン様をお救いするのだ！！<line-break>出撃！！<end>"
EN: "Move out! Follow <value:$28>! We must save Lord Angeerdan! Move!<end>"
KO: "출격! <value:$28>님을 따르라! 앙길단 님을 구하는 거다!! 출격!!<end>"

## 590067
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec059-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ゼネテス…、<line-break>そうか、お主がおったのか！　　　<line-break>　<line-break>お主ならば、あの敗軍を<line-break>まとめる器量がある！<line-break>くっ、ぬかったわ！<end>"
EN: "Xenetes... So you were here! You have the ability to rally even that defeated army. Damn, I was careless!<end>"
KO: "제네테스…. 그렇군, 네놈이 있었나! 네놈이라면 그 패잔군을 수습할 역량이 있다! 큭, 방심했군!<end>"

## 590068
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec059-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お主がおるとわかっておれば、<line-break>敗残兵の殲滅を優先させたものを！<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "Had I known you were here, I would have made annihilating the defeated remnants my first priority!<end>"
KO: "네놈이 있는 줄 알았다면 패잔병 섬멸을 우선했을 것을!<end>"

## 590069
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec059-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…とっつぁん、<line-break>悪いが、命をもらう。　　<line-break>　<line-break>…ロストールの兵の<line-break>命がかかっているんでな。<end>"
EN: "Sorry, old man, but I'm taking your life. The lives of Rostorl's soldiers depend on it.<end>"
KO: "…아저씨, 미안하지만 목숨을 받겠다. …로스톨 병사들의 목숨이 걸려 있어서 말이야.<end>"

## 590070
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec059-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "はい、そうですかと<line-break>くれてやるわけにはいかんな。<line-break>　<line-break>こっちも<line-break>ディンガルの兵の命が<line-break>かかっておるのでな！<end>"
EN: "I can hardly hand it over just because you ask. The lives of Dyneskal's soldiers are at stake too!<end>"
KO: "네, 그렇습니까 하고 순순히 내줄 수는 없지. 이쪽도 딘갈 병사들의 목숨이 걸려 있으니까!<end>"

## 590071
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec059-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…<value:$28>！　お前も<line-break>アンギルダンのとっつぁんの側か！　<line-break>ちっ、人物は人物を呼ぶんだな。<line-break>こりゃあ、ちょっと分が悪いな。<line-break>ま、いい。なんとか運命の女神を<line-break>振り向かせるのには成功したようだ。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>! You're on old Angeerdan's side too? Damn. People of caliber really do attract one another. The odds are a little against me now. Still, no matter. It seems I've managed to win the Goddess of Fate's favor.<end>"
KO: "…<value:$28>! 너도 앙길단 영감 쪽이냐! 쳇, 인물은 인물을 부르는 법이군. 이거 조금 불리하겠어. 뭐, 됐어. 어떻게든 운명의 여신을 돌아보게 하는 데는 성공한 모양이니까.<end>"

## 590072
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec059-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あばよ、とっつぁん、　　　　　　　<line-break>そして<value:$28>！<line-break>生きて、また会おうぜ！<end>"
EN: "See you, old man―and you too, <value:$28>! Stay alive, and we'll meet again!<end>"
KO: "잘 있어, 아저씨, 그리고 <value:$28>! 살아서 다시 만나자!<end>"

## 590073
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec059-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ほう…。<line-break>ここまでたどり着いてくるとは<line-break>やるな…。<end>"
EN: "Well now... You did well to make it this far.<end>"
KO: "호오…. 여기까지 도착하다니 제법이군….<end>"

## 590074
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec059-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "俺の名は<line-break>ゼネテス・ファーロス。　　　　　　　<line-break>ま、覚えといてくれや。<line-break>あんたのことは知ってる。<line-break><value:$28>…だよな？<line-break>やっぱ、人物は人物を呼ぶんだな。<end>"
EN: "The name's Xenetes Pharos. Remember it. I already know yours. <value:$28>, right? People of caliber really do attract one another.<end>"
KO: "내 이름은 제네테스 파로스. 뭐, 기억해 둬. 네 이야기는 알고 있어. <value:$28>… 맞지? 역시 인물은 인물을 부르는 법이군.<end>"

## 590075
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec059-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "今日のところは、退くとしよう。<line-break>なんとか運命の女神を<line-break>振り向かせるのに成功したようだしな。<line-break>あばよ、とっつぁん、<line-break>そして<value:$28>！<line-break>生きて、また会おうぜ！<end>"
EN: "I'll pull back for today. It seems I've managed to win the Goddess of Fate's favor. See you, old man―and you too, <value:$28>! Stay alive, and we'll meet again!<end>"
KO: "오늘은 이만 물러나지. 어떻게든 운명의 여신을 이쪽으로 돌아보게 하는 데 성공한 모양이니까. 잘 있어, 아저씨. 그리고 <value:$28>! 살아서 또 만나자고!<end>"

## 590076
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec059-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "心配するな！　ノーブル伯！<line-break>叔母貴には黙っておくからな！<end>"
EN: "Don't worry, Count of Noble! I won't tell Auntie!<end>"
KO: "걱정 마라! 노블 백작! 숙모에게는 비밀로 해 둘 테니까!<end>"

## 590077
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec059-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フフ、さすがはゼネテスよ…。<line-break>やりおる。　　　　　　　　　　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>わしがもっと若ければな…。<line-break>そうすれば、お主と力を合わせて、<line-break>あの男を討ち取れていたかもしれんな。<end>"
EN: "Heh. That is Xenetes for you. Quite a man. If only I were younger. Then perhaps you and I together could have brought him down.<end>"
KO: "후후, 역시 제네테스구나…. 제법이야. 내가 좀 더 젊었다면…. 그랬다면 자네와 힘을 합쳐 그 남자를 쓰러뜨릴 수도 있었겠지.<end>"

## 590078
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec059-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "だが、あきらめが肝要よ。負けじゃ。　<line-break>運命の女神のほほえみを<line-break>あの色男に持っていかれたわい！<end>"
EN: "But one must know when to yield. We have lost. That handsome devil has stolen the Goddess of Fate's smile from me!<end>"
KO: "하지만 포기가 중요하지. 졌어. 운명의 여신의 미소를 저 잘생긴 녀석에게 빼앗겼군!<end>"

## 590079
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec059-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "撤退じゃ！　散れ！<line-break>地下王国を抜け、<line-break>生きて帰るのじゃ！<end>"
EN: "Retreat! Scatter! Pass through the Underground Kingdom and make it home alive!<end>"
KO: "퇴각이다! 흩어져라! 지하왕국을 빠져나가 살아서 돌아가라!<end>"

## 590080
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec059-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>！　生きて会おうぞ！<line-break>エンシャントの政庁の前で待っておる。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>! We'll meet again―alive! I'll be waiting in front of the Ancient Government Hall.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>! 살아서 만나자! 엔샨트 정청 앞에서 기다리고 있겠다.<end>"

## 590082
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec059-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　ゼネテスはあえてこれを追撃せず、　<line-break>　　こうして、山越えに始まった　　　<line-break>　ディンガルのロストール侵攻戦は、　<line-break>　　失敗という結末で幕を閉じた。　　<line-break>　ゼネテスの声望は一気に高まり、　　<line-break>　奇跡の名将とまで呼ばれたが、　　　<line-break>　この戦いにおけるロストールの　　　<line-break>　被害は、甚大なものであった…。　　<end>"
EN: "Xenetes deliberately chose not to pursue. Thus the Dyneskal invasion of Rostorl, begun with the crossing of the mountains, ended in failure. Xenetes's renown soared, and he was even hailed as a miracle general. Yet Rostorl had suffered grievous losses in the battle...<end>"
KO: "제네테스는 굳이 이를 추격하지 않았다. 이렇게 산 넘기에서 시작된 딩갈의 로스톨 침공전은 실패로 막을 내렸다. 제네테스의 명성은 단숨에 높아져 기적의 명장이라고까지 불렸지만, 이 전투에서 로스톨이 입은 피해 역시 막대했다….<end>"

## 590083
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec059-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フフ、運命の女神は残酷なものよ！<line-break>ここまで来て、わしを見限るとはな…。<line-break>　　　　　　　　　　　<line-break>ゼネテスよ…。<line-break>お主ほどの男が…ファーロスの…<line-break>跡取りだったとは…、うぐっ…。<end>"
EN: "Heh. The Goddess of Fate is cruel indeed! To abandon me after I had come so far... Xenetes... To think a man like you was... the heir to Pharos... Urgh...<end>"
KO: "후후, 운명의 여신은 잔혹하구나! 여기까지 와서 나를 버리다니…. 제네테스여…. 너 같은 남자가… 파로스의… 후계자였다니… 윽….<end>"

## 590084
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec059-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "退け！　バラバラに地下王国を通れ！<line-break>みな…生き延びるの…じゃ…！<end>"
EN: "Retreat! Scatter and pass through the Underground Kingdom! All of you... survive...!<end>"
KO: "물러나라! 흩어져 지하 왕국을 통과해라! 모두… 살아남는… 거다…!<end>"

## 590085
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec059-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　驚くべき早さで敗残兵をまとめあげた　　<line-break>　　　　　　ゼネテスの奇襲により　　　　　　<line-break>　　　　アンギルダンが討ち取られると　　　　<line-break>　　　　　帝国軍は総崩れとなった。　　　　　<line-break>　　　　生き残ったディンガル兵は　　　　　　<line-break>　　　　今度は分断の山脈の地下、　　　　　　<line-break>　　　　　ドワーフの王国を抜け　　　　　　　<line-break>　ディンガル領へと撤退を余儀なくされた。　　<end>"
EN: "Xenetes rallied the defeated remnants with astonishing speed and launched a surprise attack in which Angeerdan was slain, throwing the entire imperial army into a rout. The surviving Dyneskal soldiers were forced to retreat to Dyneskal territory through the Dwarf kingdom beneath the Dividing Mountains.<end>"
KO: "놀라운 속도로 패잔병을 규합한 제네테스의 기습으로 앙길단이 전사하자 제국군은 완전히 무너졌다. 살아남은 딩갈 병사들은 이번에는 분단의 산맥 지하에 있는 드워프 왕국을 지나 딩갈 영토로 퇴각할 수밖에 없었다.<end>"

## 590086
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec059-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　ゼネテスはあえてこれを追撃せず、　<line-break>　　こうして、山越えに始まった　　　<line-break>　ディンガルのロストール侵攻戦は、　<line-break>　　失敗という結末で幕を閉じた。　　<line-break>　ゼネテスの声望は一気に高まり、　　<line-break>　奇跡の名将とまで呼ばれたが、　　　<line-break>　この戦いにおけるロストールの　　　<line-break>　被害は、甚大なものであった…。　　<end>"
EN: "Xenetes deliberately chose not to pursue. Thus the Dyneskal invasion of Rostorl, begun with the crossing of the mountains, ended in failure. Xenetes's renown soared, and he was even hailed as a miracle general. Yet Rostorl had suffered grievous losses in the battle...<end>"
KO: "제네테스는 굳이 이를 추격하지 않았다. 이렇게 산 넘기에서 시작된 딩갈의 로스톨 침공전은 실패로 막을 내렸다. 제네테스의 명성은 단숨에 높아져 기적의 명장이라고까지 불렸지만, 이 전투에서 로스톨이 입은 피해 역시 막대했다….<end>"

## 590087
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec059-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうか…。残念じゃ。<end>"
EN: "I see... A pity.<end>"
KO: "그런가…. 유감이군.<end>"

## 590088
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec059-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "傭兵志願者か。<line-break>案内する。ついてくるとよい。<end>"
EN: "Here to enlist as a mercenary? I'll show you the way. Follow me.<end>"
KO: "용병 지원자인가. 안내하지. 따라오게.<end>"

## 590089
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec059-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ネメア陛下の世界統一構想に基づき、<line-break>南方攻略軍司令アンギルダン閣下に<line-break>ロストール攻略の大任が下された。<line-break>ついては、大兵力の動員のために、<line-break>傭兵を募集することになった。<line-break>君は、遠征軍に加わる気があるのか？<end>"
EN: "In accordance with His Majesty Nemea's plan to unite the world, His Excellency Angeerdan, commander of the Southern Campaign Army, has been entrusted with the vital task of leading the Rostorl Campaign. Mercenaries are therefore being recruited to help muster the necessary forces. Are you willing to join the expeditionary force?<end>"
KO: "네메아 폐하의 세계 통일 구상에 따라 남방 공략군 사령 앙길단 각하께 로스톨 공략의 대임이 내려졌다. 이에 대병력 동원을 위해 용병을 모집하게 됐다. 자네는 원정군에 참가할 생각이 있나?<end>"

## 590090
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec059-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "加わる<end>"
EN: "Join<end>"
KO: "합류한다<end>"

## 590091
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec059-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "加わらない<end>"
EN: "Don't join<end>"
KO: "합류하지 않는다<end>"

## 590092
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec059-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それならば、さっそく出撃だ。<line-break>あらためて概要を説明しよう。　　　　　　　<line-break><value:$28>君。<line-break>君のことは調べがついている。<line-break>かなり評価できるようだ。<line-break>君には事実を話そう。<end>"
EN: "Then we deploy at once. Let me brief you on the plan. <value:$28>, I've had inquiries made about you, and by all accounts you're quite capable. So I'll tell you the truth.<end>"
KO: "그렇다면 당장 출격이다. 다시 개요를 설명하지. <value:$28> 군. 자네에 대해서는 조사해 두었다. 상당히 높게 평가할 만하더군. 자네에게는 사실을 말하겠다.<end>"

## 590093
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec059-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "大兵力で進攻するというのはウソだ。　　　　<line-break>大兵力の輸送のために海路を使うと<line-break>敵に判断させるためのデマだ。<line-break>実際は精兵を率いて分断の山脈を越え、<line-break>ロストール領内に進攻し、電撃的に<line-break>大街道沿いの都市を占領する。<end>"
EN: "The claim that we'll invade with a massive army is a lie. It's disinformation meant to convince the enemy that we'll transport such a force by sea. In truth, we'll lead elite troops across the Dividing Mountains, advance into Rostorl territory, and swiftly seize the cities along the main highway.<end>"
KO: "대병력으로 진공한다는 건 거짓 정보다. 대병력을 수송하려면 해로를 쓸 거라 적이 판단하게 만들기 위한 유언비어지. 실제로는 정예병을 이끌고 분단의 산맥을 넘어 로스톨 영내로 진공해 대가도 연선 도시를 전격적으로 점령한다.<end>"

## 590094
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec059-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "歴史上、類を見ない大胆な作戦だ。私も<line-break>最初に聞いたときは驚いたが、必ず成功する。<line-break>君にもこの作戦に参加して欲しい。<end>"
EN: "It is a plan of unprecedented daring. I was surprised when I first heard it myself, but it will succeed. I want you to take part.<end>"
KO: "역사상 유례없는 대담한 작전이다. 나도 처음 들었을 때는 놀랐지만 반드시 성공한다. 너도 이 작전에 참가해 주었으면 한다.<end>"

## 590095
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec059-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "期待している。<end>"
EN: "I'm counting on you.<end>"
KO: "기대하고 있다.<end>"

## 590096
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec059-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ロストール軍が急遽、軍を返してきた。<line-break>おとなしくしておればよいものを<line-break>のこのこと、今さら！<line-break>敵は急行軍で隊列もバラバラ、<line-break>疲労しきっている。<line-break>楽勝だ！　存分に活躍せよ。<end>"
EN: "Rostorl's army has suddenly turned back. They should have stayed put, but here they come now, far too late! After that forced march, their ranks are in disarray and their troops exhausted. This will be an easy victory! Go and distinguish yourself.<end>"
KO: "로스톨군이 갑자기 군을 돌려 돌아왔다. 얌전히 있을 것이지 이제 와서 뻔뻔하게! 적은 급행군으로 대열도 흐트러졌고 완전히 지쳐 있다. 식은 죽 먹기다! 마음껏 활약해라.<end>"

## 590097
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec059-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おお、<value:$28>ではないか。<line-break>お主もこの戦役に参加しとったか。<end>"
EN: "Ah, <value:$28>! So you're taking part in this campaign as well.<end>"
KO: "오오, <value:$28> 아닌가. 자네도 이 전쟁에 참가하고 있었군.<end>"

## 590098
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec059-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…では、論功行賞を行う。<line-break>最後は<value:$28>。<end>"
EN: "Now, to bestow honors for valor. Last is <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "…그럼 논공행상을 시작한다. 마지막은 <value:$28>.<end>"

## 590099
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec059-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>、<line-break>お主が最高殊勲者だ。<line-break>　<line-break>この功をもって、わしは<line-break>お主をディンガルの騎士に推薦し、<line-break>ここで仮叙任を行おうと思う。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>, you have earned the highest distinction of all. In recognition of this achievement, I shall recommend you for knighthood in Dyneskal and provisionally invest you here and now.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>, 네가 최고 공훈자다. 이 공으로 널 딘갈의 기사로 추천하고 여기서 임시 서임을 하겠다.<end>"

## 590100
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec059-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>、<line-break>お主はすばらしい戦績を残した。<line-break>　<line-break>この功をもって、わしは<line-break>お主をディンガルの騎士に推薦し、<line-break>ここで仮叙任を行おうと思う。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>, your achievements in battle were superb. In recognition of this achievement, I shall recommend you for knighthood in Dyneskal and provisionally invest you here and now.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>, 자네는 훌륭한 전과를 세웠다. 이 공을 인정해 자네를 딘갈의 기사로 추천하고 여기서 가서임하려 한다.<end>"

## 590101
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec059-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>、<line-break>まずまずの戦功だ。<line-break>　<line-break>これは恩賞じゃ。<line-break>さらに、励むがよい。<line-break>期待しておるぞ。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>, your service was adequate. Here is your reward. Continue to apply yourself. I expect much of you.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>, 제법 전공을 세웠구나. 이것은 포상이다. 더 힘쓰도록 해라. 기대하고 있겠다.<end>"

## 590102
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec059-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>、<line-break>特に功はなし、か。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>, no particular distinction to your name, I see.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>, 특별한 공은 없음, 인가.<end>"

## 590103
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec059-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…お主はわしとともに本軍に残れ。<line-break>以上、解散！<end>"
EN: "You will remain with me in the main force. That is all. Dismissed!<end>"
KO: "…너는 나와 함께 본대에 남아라. 이상, 해산!<end>"

## 590104
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec059-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "わしのやり方に<line-break>不満があるようじゃな。<end>"
EN: "You seem displeased with my methods.<end>"
KO: "내 방식이마음에 들지 않는 모양이군.<end>"

## 590105
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec059-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…戦争は血塗られた道よ。　　<line-break>いかに取り繕おうともな。<line-break>　<line-break>仕方ないことじゃ、とは言わぬ。<line-break>だがわしには、流される血が少なく<line-break>なるように努力することしかできぬ。<end>"
EN: "War is a blood-soaked road, however one may dress it up. I will not call it unavoidable. But all I can do is strive to shed as little blood as possible.<end>"
KO: "…전쟁은 피로 물든 길이야. 아무리 꾸며도 그렇지. 어쩔 수 없는 일이라고는 하지 않겠다. 다만 나는 흘리는 피가 조금이라도 줄도록 노력할 수밖에 없어.<end>"

## 590106
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec059-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "て、て、敵襲ー！<end>"
EN: "E-enemy attack!<end>"
KO: "저, 저, 적습이다ー!<end>"

## 590107
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec059-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ロストールが敗残兵を立て直し、<line-break>き、奇襲っ！　ぐわああっ！<end>"
EN: "Rostorl has rallied its defeated troops and launched a s-surprise attack! Gwaaah!<end>"
KO: "로스톨이 패잔병을 재정비하고, 기, 기습을! 으아아악!<end>"

## 590108
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec059-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "略奪にディンガル兵が散らばってる間に<line-break>本体中枢部をつき、壊乱させる！<line-break>ゼネテス様の作戦は当たってるぞ！<end>"
EN: "While Dyneskal's soldiers are scattered about looting, we'll strike at the heart of their main force and throw it into chaos! Xenetes's plan is working!<end>"
KO: "약탈하느라 딘갈 병사들이 흩어진 사이 본대의 중추를 찔러 무너뜨린다! 제네테스 님의 작전이 적중하고 있어!<end>"

## 590109
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec059-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "俺たち、生き延びられるかもしれねえ！<line-break>いや、こうなったら、生き延びてやる！<line-break>ディンガルの奴らを倒してな！<end>"
EN: "We might actually survive this! No―now that it's come to this, we're going to survive! And we'll take down those Dyneskal bastards while we're at it!<end>"
KO: "우리 살아남을 수 있을지도 몰라! 아니, 이렇게 된 이상 반드시 살아남겠어! 딘갈 녀석들을 쓰러뜨리고 말이야!<end>"

## 590110
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec059-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ゆくぞ！<end>"
EN: "Forward!<end>"
KO: "간다!<end>"

## 590111
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec059-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…<value:$28>！　お前も<line-break>アンギルダンのとっつぁんの側か！　<line-break>人物は人物を呼ぶんだな。<line-break>こりゃあ、ちょっと分が悪いな。<line-break>ま、いい。なんとか運命の女神を<line-break>振り向かせるのには成功したようだ。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>! So you're on old Angeerdan's side too! Talent does seem to attract talent. That leaves me at a bit of a disadvantage. Ah, well. It seems I've managed to get the Goddess of Fate to look my way.<end>"
KO: "…<value:$28>! 너도 앙길단 영감 쪽이냐! 역시 인물은 인물을 끌어들이는군. 이거 조금 불리한데. 뭐, 좋아. 어떻게든 운명의 여신을 돌아보게 하는 데는 성공한 모양이군.<end>"

## 590112
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec059-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ほう、ここまでたどり着いてくるとは<line-break>やるな…。<end>"
EN: "Well now. You've done well to make it this far.<end>"
KO: "호오, 여기까지 도달하다니 제법이군….<end>"

## 590113
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec059-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>よ。<line-break>すまぬが、敗軍の将に<line-break>つきあってくれる気はあるか？<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>, forgive me for asking, but would you accompany a defeated commander?<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>여. 미안하지만 패군의 장수와 함께해 줄 생각이 있느냐?<end>"

## 590114
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec059-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ならば、生きて会おう。<line-break>エンシャントの政庁前で待っておる！<end>"
EN: "Then let us meet again alive. I shall wait for you outside Ancient Government Hall!<end>"
KO: "그렇다면 살아서 만나자. 엔샨트 정청 앞에서 기다리겠다!<end>"

## 590115
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec059-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "退け！<line-break>バラバラに地下王国を通れ！<line-break>みな…生き延びるの…じゃ…！<end>"
EN: "Retreat! Split up and make your way through the Underground Kingdom! All of you... survive...!<end>"
KO: "물러나라! 흩어져 지하 왕국을 통과해라! 모두… 살아남는… 거다…!<end>"

## 590116
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec059-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうか。それならば致し方ない。<line-break>立ち去るがよい。我々は大兵力だ。<line-break>情報が漏れても負けることはない。<end>"
EN: "I see. Then it cannot be helped. You may leave. Our army is vast. Even if word gets out, we will not lose.<end>"
KO: "그런가. 그렇다면 어쩔 수 없지. 물러가라. 우리는 대병력이다. 정보가 새도 질 일은 없다.<end>"

## 590117
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec059-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "朱雀将軍からお話は伺っております！<line-break>将軍は現在、留守にしておられます！<line-break>後日お越しくださいますようお願いいたします！<end>"
EN: "We've received word from the Vermilion Bird General! The general is currently away. Please return another day!<end>"
KO: "주작장군께 말씀은 들었습니다! 장군님은 현재 자리를 비우셨습니다! 후일 다시 와 주시기 바랍니다!<end>"

## 590118
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec059-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "今、担当が席を外しておる。<line-break>またあとで来るといい。<end>"
EN: "The person in charge has stepped away for the moment. Come back later.<end>"
KO: "지금 담당자가 자리를 비웠다. 나중에 다시 오도록 해.<end>"

## 590119
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec059-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "アンギルダンが心配だ<end>"
EN: "I'm worried about Angeerdan.<end>"
KO: "앙길단이 걱정이다<end>"

## 590120
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec059-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あんな男、どうでもいい<end>"
EN: "I don't care about a man like that.<end>"
KO: "그런 남자, 아무래도 좋아.<end>"

## 600001
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec060-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　皇帝ネメアの世界統一構想のもと　　　<line-break>　　南方攻略軍司令、朱雀将軍アンギルダンが　<line-break>　　　ついにロストール攻略に動き出した。<end>"
EN: "Under Emperor Nemea's plan to unite the world, Angeerdan, Vermilion Bird General and commander of the Southern Campaign Army, at last launched the Rostorl Campaign.<end>"
KO: "황제 네메아의 세계 통일 구상 아래 남방 공략군 사령관, 주작장군 앙길단이 마침내 로스톨 공략에 나섰다.<end>"

## 600002
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec060-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　帝国軍が大兵力を投入すると　　　<line-break>　　予想したロストール側は、分断の山脈を<line-break>　　　越えてくるとは考えず、内海側の　　<line-break>　海路を封鎖し、敵の上陸時を叩く作戦に出た。<line-break>　これに対し、ロストール軍副指令ゼネテスは<line-break>　　帝国は分断の山脈を越えてくると主張、　<line-break>　山脈がわに兵を割いて防衛線を築くことを　<line-break>　　提案したが、軍議で即時に却下された。<end>"
EN: "Expecting the Imperial Army to commit a massive force, Rostorl did not believe it would cross the Dividing Mountains. Instead, Rostorl blockaded the sea routes through the inland sea, planning to strike as the enemy landed. Xenetes, deputy commander of the Rostorl Army, argued that the Empire would cross the mountains and proposed assigning troops to establish a defensive line there, but the council of war rejected his proposal outright.<end>"
KO: "제국군이 대병력을 투입할 것이라 예상한 로스톨 측은 분단의 산맥을 넘어올 것이라고는 생각하지 않고 내해 쪽 해로를 봉쇄해 적의 상륙 때 공격하는 작전을 세웠다. 이에 로스톨군 부사령 제네테스는 제국군이 분단의 산맥을 넘어올 것이라 주장하며 산맥 쪽에 병력을 나눠 방어선을 구축하자고 제안했으나 군의에서 즉시 기각되었다.<end>"

## 600003
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec060-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　しかし、このゼネテスの予測は正しく、<line-break>　　　　アンギルダンは少数の精兵で　　　<line-break>　　　分断の山脈を越えると、大街道を　　<line-break>　　　　電撃的な早さで行軍してきた。　　<line-break>　　　　分断の山脈を軍隊が越えたのは　　<line-break>　　　　　　歴史上初の壮挙である。　　　<line-break>　　　　　こうしてディンガル軍は　　　　<line-break>　　　　王都ロストールに王手をかけた。<end>"
EN: "Xenetes's prediction, however, proved correct. Angeerdan crossed the Dividing Mountains with a small force of elite troops, then advanced along the main highway with lightning speed. It was the first time in history that an army had accomplished the feat of crossing the Dividing Mountains. The Dyneskal Army thus stood poised to seize the royal capital of Rostorl.<end>"
KO: "하지만 제네테스의 예측은 정확했다. 앙길단은 소수 정예병으로 분단의 산맥을 넘은 뒤 대가도를 번개 같은 속도로 행군해 왔다. 군대가 분단의 산맥을 넘은 것은 역사상 최초의 위업이었다. 이렇게 딩갈군은 왕도 로스톨을 눈앞에 두게 되었다.<end>"

## 600004
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec060-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　事ここに及び、ロストール軍総司令<line-break>　　　　ノヴィンは急きょ、軍を反転、　<line-break>　　　アンギルダン率いるディンガル軍を<line-break>　　　　　迎え撃つべく、急行した。　　<line-break>　　　　　このとき、ゼネテスは　　　　　<line-break>　　父親でもある総司令に、隊列を整えつつ<line-break>　　　ゆっくりと敵を牽制し、その上で　　<line-break>　　王都守備軍と連係攻撃すべきと説いた。<end>"
EN: "With matters at this pass, Novin, commander-in-chief of the Rostorl Army, hastily reversed course and rushed to intercept the Dyneskal Army led by Angeerdan. Xenetes urged the commander-in-chief, who was also his father, to advance slowly, keeping the enemy in check while restoring the army's formation, and then mount a coordinated attack with the capital's garrison.<end>"
KO: "사태가 이에 이르자 로스톨군 총사령 노빈은 급히 군을 돌려 앙길단이 이끄는 딩갈군을 맞아 싸우러 달려갔다. 이때 제네테스는 아버지이기도 한 총사령에게 대열을 정비하면서 천천히 적을 견제한 뒤 왕도 수비군과 연계 공격해야 한다고 진언했다.<end>"

## 600005
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec060-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　しかし、ここでもまたゼネテスの説得は<line-break>　　　聞き入れられず、隊列が乱れたまま　<line-break>　　　交戦状態に入ったロストール軍は　　<line-break>　　ディンガル軍の攻撃を受け、壊滅した。<line-break>　　　この戦いで、ロストール軍総司令　　<line-break>　　　ノヴィン・ファーロスをはじめ、　　<line-break>　　　　ロストールの名だたる貴族が　　　<line-break>　　　　　多く、その命を落とした。　　　<end>"
EN: "Once again, however, Xenetes's counsel went unheeded. The Rostorl Army entered battle with its ranks still in disarray and was annihilated by the Dyneskal Army's attack. Many of Rostorl's most prominent nobles lost their lives in the battle, including Novin Pharos, commander-in-chief of the Rostorl Army.<end>"
KO: "하지만 여기서도 제네테스의 설득은 받아들여지지 않았다. 대열이 흐트러진 채 전투에 들어간 로스톨군은 딩갈군의 공격을 받아 괴멸했다. 이 전투에서 로스톨군 총사령 노빈 파로스를 비롯해 로스톨의 이름난 귀족 다수가 목숨을 잃었다.<end>"

## 600006
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec060-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ルブルグ伯タルテュバ・リューガは　<line-break>自らの軍を捨て単身逃走。　　　<line-break>命こそ失わずにすんだものの、　　<line-break>その評判は地に落ちた。　　　　<end>"
EN: "Count Tartuba Ryuga of Ruburg abandoned his own army and fled alone. Though he escaped with his life, his reputation was ruined.<end>"
KO: "루브르그 백작 타르튜바 류가는 자기 군대를 버리고 혼자 도주했다. 목숨은 건졌지만 평판은 땅에 떨어졌다.<end>"

## 600007
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec060-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "朱雀将軍アンギルダンは、　　　<line-break>ロストール軍を壊滅させると、　　<line-break>深追いを禁じ、続く王都侵攻に備え、<line-break>全軍に物資の現地調達開始を命じた。<end>"
EN: "After annihilating the Rostorl Army, Vermilion Bird General Angeerdan forbade his forces from pursuing the enemy too far and ordered the entire army to begin procuring supplies locally in preparation for the coming assault on the capital.<end>"
KO: "주작장군 앙길단은 로스톨군을 괴멸시키자 추격을 금하고, 이어질 왕도 침공에 대비해 전군에 현지 물자 조달을 명했다.<end>"

## 600008
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec060-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "アンギルダンの唯一の不安材料だった　<line-break>物資もこれにより補充され、　　　<line-break>ディンガル軍のロストール占領を　　<line-break>阻むものはなくなったかに見えた。　<end>"
EN: "This replenished the supplies that had been Angeerdan's sole concern, and it seemed that nothing remained to prevent the Dyneskal Army from occupying Rostorl.<end>"
KO: "앙길단의 유일한 불안 요소였던 물자도 이로써 보충되어, 딩갈군의 로스톨 점령을 막을 것은 없어 보였다.<end>"

## 600009
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec060-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "だが、ゼネテスは、驚くべき早さで　<line-break>ロストール軍の敗残兵を立て直し、　<line-break>徴発隊が繰り出して手薄になった　　<line-break>帝国軍中枢部を、奇襲した。　　　<line-break>このゼネテスの神速の奇襲により、　<line-break>帝国軍の中枢部は壊滅、　　　　<line-break>アンギルダンも討ち取られ、　　　<line-break>帝国軍は潰走した。　　　　　<end>"
EN: "But with astonishing speed, Xenetes rallied the remnants of the Rostorl Army and launched a surprise attack on the heart of the Imperial Army, left thinly defended after its requisitioning parties had been sent out. Xenetes's lightning assault destroyed the Imperial Army's core, Angeerdan was slain, and the Imperial Army fled in disarray.<end>"
KO: "하지만 제네테스는 놀라운 속도로 로스톨군의 패잔병을 수습하고, 징발대가 출동해 허술해진 제국군 중추부를 기습했다. 제네테스의 신속한 기습으로 제국군 중추부는 괴멸했고, 앙길단도 전사해 제국군은 패주했다.<end>"

## 600010
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec060-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "これによりゼネテスの声望は一気に　<line-break>高まり、奇跡の名将とまで呼ばれたが、<line-break>この戦いでロストールが受けた被害は　<line-break>甚大なものであった。　　　　<end>"
EN: "Xenetes's renown soared as a result, and he came to be hailed as a miraculous commander. Yet the losses Rostorl suffered in the battle were immense.<end>"
KO: "이로써 제네테스의 명성은 단숨에 높아져 기적의 명장이라 불리기까지 했으나, 이 전투에서 로스톨이 입은 피해는 막대했다.<end>"

## 610001
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec061-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "待て。<end>"
EN: "Wait.<end>"
KO: "기다려.<end>"

## 610002
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec061-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おー、恐い恐い。<line-break>ま、そう、かまえなさんなって。<line-break>今日は仕事で来たわけじゃない。<line-break>ま、いいお宝がないか<line-break>探しに来たわけだから、<line-break>仕事と言えば仕事だが…。<end>"
EN: "Ooh, scary, scary. Come on, don't be so tense. I'm not here on business today. Well, I did come looking for treasure, so I suppose that counts as business...<end>"
KO: "오, 무서워라. 뭐, 그렇게 경계하지 마. 오늘은 일 때문에 온 게 아니야. 좋은 보물 없나 찾으러 왔으니 일이라면 일이지만….<end>"

## 610003
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec061-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "よう、また会ったな。<end>"
EN: "Hey, we meet again.<end>"
KO: "여어, 또 만났군.<end>"

## 610004
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec061-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "竜の死骸か…。<end>"
EN: "A dragon's carcass...<end>"
KO: "용의 시체인가….<end>"

## 610005
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec061-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "この世界にいる竜は７匹。<line-break>竜王、翔王、海王の３聖竜。　　　<line-break>　<line-break>…そして<line-break>アズラゴーザ、イシュバアル、<line-break>エルアザル、シャンマの４邪竜…。<end>"
EN: "There are seven dragons in this world. The three Sacred Dragons: the Dragon King, the Soaring King, and the Sea King. And the four Evil Dragons: Azragoza, Ishbaal, Elazar, and Shamma...<end>"
KO: "이 세계의 용은 일곱. 용왕, 상왕, 해왕의 세 성룡. …그리고 아즈라고자, 이슈바알, 엘아자르, 샨마의 네 사룡…<end>"

## 610006
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec061-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "これまで竜に打ち勝ったものはふたり。<line-break>初代ドワーフ王のボルキスと<line-break>賢王アルキュオネの忘れられた剣士…。<line-break>ボルキスはシャンマを屈服させたのみ。<line-break>こいつは、忘れられた剣士に殺された<line-break>邪竜エルアザルの死体ってわけだ。<end>"
EN: "Only two have ever bested a dragon: Borkis, the first Dwarf King, and Wise King Alcyone's Forgotten Swordsman. Borkis merely forced Shamma to submit. This, then, is the carcass of the Evil Dragon Elazar, slain by the Forgotten Swordsman.<end>"
KO: "지금까지 용을 이긴 자는 둘. 초대 드워프왕 볼키스와 현왕 알큐오네의 잊힌 검사…. 볼키스는 샨마를 굴복시켰을 뿐. 이건 잊힌 검사에게 죽은 사룡 엘아자르의 시체라는 거지.<end>"

## 610007
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec061-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…まだ、怨念が残っている。<end>"
EN: "There's still some lingering malice...<end>"
KO: "…아직 원념이 남아 있다.<end>"

## 610008
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec061-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…絶対に、急には動くな。<line-break>ゆっくりと、静かに出て行くんだ。<end>"
EN: "Whatever you do, don't make any sudden moves. Get out slowly and quietly.<end>"
KO: "…절대로 갑자기 움직이지 마. 천천히, 조용히 나가는 거야.<end>"

## 610009
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec061-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "訂正するぜ。死体じゃない。<line-break>最悪だ。<line-break>アンデッド化してやがった。<end>"
EN: "Correction. It's not a carcass. This is the worst. The damned thing's become undead.<end>"
KO: "정정하지. 시체가 아니야. 최악이다. 언데드가 돼 있었어.<end>"

## 610010
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec061-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "うかつに動くと、<line-break>目を覚ますな、こいつ。<line-break>そうなったら、えらい目に会う。<line-break>ここはとっとと<line-break>ずらかった方がいいだろうな。<end>"
EN: "Move carelessly and this thing will wake up. Then there'll be hell to pay. Best get out of here, and fast.<end>"
KO: "섣불리 움직이면 이 녀석이 깨어나겠군. 그러면 큰일이야. 여긴 얼른 빠져나가는 게 좋겠어.<end>"

## 610011
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec061-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "急いで、ここを出ろ。<line-break>だたし、動作はゆっくりとな。<end>"
EN: "Get out of here quickly. Just move slowly.<end>"
KO: "서둘러 여기서 나가. 다만 움직임은 천천히 해.<end>"

## 610012
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec061-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さて、俺は重要な情報を<line-break>お前にやったわけだが、<line-break>…いくら出す？<end>"
EN: "Now then, I've just given you some valuable information. How much will you pay me?<end>"
KO: "자, 내가 중요한 정보를 네게 줬는데… 얼마 낼래?<end>"

## 610013
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec061-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ま、いい、出世払いで、いつか<line-break>払ってもらうことにするさ。<line-break>じゃあな！　<value:$28>。<end>"
EN: "Never mind. You can pay me back someday, once you've made something of yourself. See you around, <value:$28>!<end>"
KO: "뭐, 됐어. 성공하면 그때 갚는 걸로 하지. 잘 가, <value:$28>.<end>"

## 610014
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec061-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ま、いい、出世払いで、いつか<line-break>払ってもらうことにするさ。<line-break>で、名前は？<end>"
EN: "Never mind. You can pay me back someday, once you've made something of yourself. So, what's your name?<end>"
KO: "뭐, 됐어. 성공해서 돈 벌면 언젠가 갚는 걸로 하지. 그래서 이름은?<end>"

## 610015
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec061-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "金を貸しにしておくんだ。<line-break>名前を聞く権利があるだろう？<end>"
EN: "You're in my debt now. That gives me the right to ask your name, doesn't it?<end>"
KO: "돈은 외상으로 해 두지. 이름을 물을 권리 정도는 있겠지?<end>"

## 610016
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec061-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…<value:$28>、ね。<line-break>じゃあな！　出世しろよ！<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>, eh? See you around! Make something of yourself!<end>"
KO: "…<value:$28>, 이랬지. 그럼 잘 있어! 출세해라!<end>"

## 610017
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec061-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "予備メッセージ１<line-break>アンギルダン、ボリューム４<end>"
EN: ""
KO: "예비 메시지 1 앙길단, 볼륨 4<end>"

## 620001
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "や、やめてください！<end>"
EN: "P-please stop!<end>"
KO: "그, 그만해 주세요!<end>"

## 620002
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いや！　やめることなどできん！　　<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>未来の勇者であるこの俺様の<line-break>熱くたかぶる愛は！　誰にも、そう、<line-break>この俺様にすら止めることはできん！<end>"
EN: "No! I can't stop! My blazing passion, the passion of the future Hero, can't be stopped by anyone―not even me!<end>"
KO: "아니! 그만둘 수는 없다! 미래의 용자인 이 몸의 뜨겁게 타오르는 사랑은! 누구도, 그래, 이 몸 자신조차 멈출 수 없다!<end>"

## 620003
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "困ります、お客さん。<line-break>い、いや…！　触らないで！<end>"
EN: "Sir, you can't do this. N-no... Don't touch me!<end>"
KO: "곤란합니다, 손님. 아, 아니…! 만지지 마세요!<end>"

## 620004
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "俺様と２人で愛の冒険に旅立つ。<line-break>それが君の運命なんだ！<end>"
EN: "You and I will set out together on an adventure of love. That is your destiny!<end>"
KO: "이 몸과 둘이 사랑의 모험을 떠난다. 그게 네 운명이야!<end>"

## 620005
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あっ。た、助けてください！<end>"
EN: "Ah! P-please, help me!<end>"
KO: "앗. 사, 살려 주세요!<end>"

## 620006
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "このお客さん、酔っぱらってて…。<end>"
EN: "This customer is drunk...<end>"
KO: "이 손님, 취해 있어서….<end>"

## 620007
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "誰だ貴様ぁ！　　　　　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>どこかで会ったような、<line-break>そうでないような…！<line-break>ううう！　混乱するぅ！<end>"
EN: "Who are you?! I feel as if we've met somewhere before... but then again, maybe not! Ugh! I'm so confused!<end>"
KO: "누구냐 네놈! 어디선가 본 것 같기도 하고 아닌 것 같기도 하고…! 으으! 헷갈린다!<end>"

## 620008
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "グルグル回るぅ！　ヒック！　<line-break>…ぬおっ、そうか！<line-break>それがねらいか！<line-break>俺様を混乱させて<line-break>そのスキに俺様を倒そうとする<line-break>魔王の刺客だな！　貴様！<end>"
EN: "Everything's spinning! Hic! Ah! Now I see! That's your plan, is it? You're an agent of the Demon King, trying to confuse me so you can strike me down while my guard is lowered!<end>"
KO: "빙글빙글 돈다아! 딸꾹! …오오, 그렇군! 그게 목적이냐! 나를 혼란시킨 틈에 쓰러뜨리려는 마왕의 자객이구나! 네놈!<end>"

## 620009
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フッ、ちょうどいい。　　　　<line-break>俺様の愛の伝説の<line-break>わき役にしてやる！<end>"
EN: "Hmph. Perfect timing. I'll make you a supporting player in the legend of my love!<end>"
KO: "훗, 마침 잘됐군. 이 몸의 사랑의 전설에조연으로 넣어 주마!<end>"

## 620010
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なんだ貴様ぁ！　　　<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>フッ、ちょうどいい。<line-break>俺様の愛の伝説の<line-break>わき役にしてやる！<end>"
EN: "Who are you?! Hmph. Perfect timing. I'll make you a supporting player in the legend of my love!<end>"
KO: "뭐냐 네놈! 흥, 마침 잘됐군. 내 사랑의 전설에 조연으로 끼워 주마!<end>"

## 620011
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "こ、こんなはずは…。<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>ええい、まだだ！<line-break>俺様の無敵伝説は<line-break>これから始まるんだ！<end>"
EN: "Th-this can't be... No, it's not over yet! The legend of my invincibility has only just begun!<end>"
KO: "이, 이럴 리가…. 젠장, 아직이다! 이 몸의 무적 전설은 이제부터 시작이야!<end>"

## 620012
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ありがとうございますっ！<line-break>大丈夫ですか？<end>"
EN: "Thank you so much! Are you all right?<end>"
KO: "감사합니다! 괜찮으세요?<end>"

## 620013
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お強いんですね。感心します。<end>"
EN: "You're very strong. I'm impressed.<end>"
KO: "강하시네요. 감탄했어요.<end>"

## 620014
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "失礼ですが、お名前は…。<end>"
EN: "Forgive me, but... may I ask your name?<end>"
KO: "실례지만, 성함은….<end>"

source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "見ててくれたか？　　　　　　　　　<end>"
EN: "Did you see that?<end>"
KO: "보고 있었나?<end>"

## 620017
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "これが俺様の愛の…<line-break>溢れんばかりにほとばしる愛の力！！<end>"
EN: "This is the power of my love... my boundless, surging love!<end>"
KO: "이것이 이 몸의 사랑의… 넘쳐흐르는 사랑의 힘!!<end>"

## 620018
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "恐れずに飛び込んでくるといい。<end>"
EN: "Don't be afraid. Just dive right in.<end>"
KO: "두려워 말고 뛰어들면 된다.<end>"

## 620019
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "俺様の愛の翼は、<line-break>いつでも君を包み込むぜ！　　　<end>"
EN: "The wings of my love will always be there to enfold you!<end>"
KO: "이 몸의 사랑의 날개는 언제든 널 감싸 줄 거야!<end>"

## 620020
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "だ、大丈夫ですかっ！<end>"
EN: "A-are you all right?!<end>"
KO: "괘, 괜찮으세요!?<end>"

## 620021
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ごめんなさい…私なんかのために…。<line-break>…ケガをさせてしまって…。<end>"
EN: "I'm sorry... You were hurt because of someone like me...<end>"
KO: "미안해요… 저 같은 사람 때문에…. 다치게 해서….<end>"

## 620022
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あ、あの失礼ですが、お名前は…。<end>"

KO: "저, 실례지만 성함은….<end>"

source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あっ、<value:$28>さん！<end>"
EN: "Oh, <value:$28>!<end>"
KO: "앗, <value:$28> 씨!<end>"

## 620025
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あ、あの…。<line-break>先ほどはありがとうございました。<end>"
EN: "Um... thank you for earlier.<end>"
KO: "저, 저기…. 아까는 감사합니다.<end>"

## 620026
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あ、あの…。<line-break>先ほどはありがとうございました。<line-break>　<line-break>……あのー、<line-break>このあと、お暇ですか？<end>"
EN: "Um... thank you for earlier. I was wondering... are you free after this?<end>"
KO: "저, 저기…. 아까는 감사합니다. ……저기, 이 뒤에 시간 괜찮으세요?<end>"

## 620027
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ご、ごめんなさい。<line-break>そうですよね。<end>"
EN: "S-sorry. Of course.<end>"
KO: "미, 미안해요. 그렇죠.<end>"

## 620028
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それじゃあ、<value:$28>さん。<line-break>私は仕事に戻ります。<line-break>　<line-break>また、この酒場にも<line-break>いらしてくださいね。<line-break>ありがとうございましたー！<end>"
EN: "Well then, <value:$28>, I'd better get back to work. Please come by the tavern again sometime. Thank you again!<end>"
KO: "그럼 <value:$28> 씨. 저는 일하러 돌아갈게요. 또 이 술집에도 들러 주세요. 감사합니다!<end>"

## 620029
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ほぉーらあ！<line-break><value:$28>、<line-break>起きなさい！<end>"
EN: "Come on, <value:$28>, wake up!<end>"
KO: "자아! <value:$28>, 일어나!<end>"

## 620030
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "もう！　またなの？<line-break>何度もベッドから落ちたりして<line-break>ほんっとに、しょうがないんだから。<end>"
EN: "Honestly! Again? You keep falling out of bed. What am I going to do with you?<end>"
KO: "정말! 또야? 몇 번이나 침대에서 떨어지고 정말 못 말린다니까.<end>"

## 620031
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ほぉーら、目が覚めたでしょ。<line-break>あの変なゴブリンを追うわよ。<line-break>はやく下に来なさい。<end>"
EN: "There, you're awake now. We're going after that weird Goblin. Hurry up and come downstairs.<end>"
KO: "자아, 정신이 들었지? 그 이상한 고블린을 쫓자. 빨리 아래로 내려와.<end>"

## 620032
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それでは、ごゆっくりどうぞ。　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "Please, take your time.<end>"
KO: "그럼 편히 쉬세요.<end>"

## 620033
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "すごいゴブ！　すごいゴブ！<line-break>人間が俺たちを襲わないだけでなく、<line-break>頭まで下げてるでゴブ～！<end>"
EN: "Amazing, gob! Amazing, gob! The humans aren't attacking us, and they're even bowing to us, gob!<end>"
KO: "대단한 고브! 대단한 고브! 인간이 우리를 습격하지도 않을 뿐더러 머리까지 숙이는 고브~!<end>"

## 620034
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "む、わかったでゴブ！<end>"
EN: "Ah, now I understand, gob!<end>"
KO: "으, 알겠는 고브!<end>"

## 620035
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "すべては、この禁断の聖杯の<line-break>おかげゴブ！<line-break>　<line-break>この聖杯を手に入れて以来、<line-break>体中にみなぎる英知が<line-break>そう告げているゴブ！<end>"
EN: "It's all thanks to this Forbidden Grail, gob! Ever since I acquired it, the wisdom surging through my body has told me so, gob!<end>"
KO: "모든 건 이 금단의 성배 덕분이다 고브! 이 성배를 손에 넣은 뒤로 온몸에 넘치는 지혜가 그렇게 알려 준다 고브!<end>"

## 620036
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あーっ！　あんたたち！<end>"
EN: "Hey! You lot!<end>"
KO: "아앗! 너희들!<end>"

## 620037
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "む、ガサツ女にその他１名！<end>"
EN: "Hm. The uncouth woman and one other person!<end>"
KO: "음, 거친 여자와 기타 1명!<end>"

## 620038
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "誰がガサツ女よ！<line-break>その聖杯、返してもらうわ！<end>"
EN: "Who are you calling uncouth? Give back that Grail!<end>"
KO: "누가 거친 여자라는 거야! 그 성배, 돌려받겠어!<end>"

## 620039
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうはいかないでゴブ。この聖杯は、<line-break>天から我らゴブゴブ団に<line-break>与えられたものゴブ！<end>"
EN: "I think not, gob. This Grail was bestowed upon the Gob-Gob Gang by the heavens, gob!<end>"
KO: "그럴 수는 없다 고브. 이 성배는 하늘이 우리 고브고브단에게 내려 준 것이다 고브!<end>"

## 620040
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "何が天から与えられたよ！<line-break>盗んだくせに！<end>"
EN: "Bestowed by the heavens, my foot! You stole it!<end>"
KO: "뭐가 하늘이 내린 거야! 훔친 주제에!<end>"

## 620041
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "む、ガサツ女が狂暴化したゴブ！<line-break>危険ゴブ！<line-break>ゴブゴブ団、退却ゴブ！<end>"
EN: "The uncouth woman has gone berserk, gob! Danger, gob! Gob-Gob Gang, retreat!<end>"
KO: "으, 거친 여자가 흉폭해졌다 고브! 위험하다 고브! 고브고브단, 후퇴 고브!<end>"

## 620042
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あ、おねーさん、<line-break>お代はここに置いておくゴブ！<end>"
EN: "Oh, miss, we'll leave the money here, gob!<end>"
KO: "아, 누나, 계산은 여기 두고 갈게 고브!<end>"

## 620043
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "オルナット、<line-break>メシ食ってる場合じゃないゴブ！<end>"
EN: "Ornut, this is no time to be eating, gob!<end>"
KO: "오르나트, 밥 먹고 있을 때가 아니다 고브!<end>"

## 620044
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ああ、メシ～。<end>"
EN: "Ahh, food...<end>"
KO: "아아, 밥~.<end>"

## 620045
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ったく、逃げ足だけは早いわね！<line-break><value:$28>、追いましょ。<end>"
EN: "Damn, running away is the one thing they're good at! <value:$28>, let's go after them.<end>"
KO: "정말, 도망치는 건 빠르다니까! <value:$28>, 쫓아가자.<end>"

## 620046
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "バイアシオン最大の都市。<line-break>文化と商業の都…。<line-break>　<line-break>さっすがに大きな街よね、<line-break>リベルダムって！<end>"
EN: "Vyashion's largest city, a center of culture and commerce... Liberdam really is enormous!<end>"
KO: "바이아시온 최대의 도시. 문화와 상업의 수도…. 역시 큰 도시네, 리벨덤은!<end>"

## 620047
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "とにかく、この広い街並みから　　<line-break>あいつら３匹を捜さなきゃ。<line-break>　<line-break>まずは、ギルドに行ってみましょ。<line-break>あいつらがいなくても、<line-break>ついでに冒険者登録できるし。<end>"
EN: "Anyway, we've got to find those three somewhere in this huge city. Let's try the Guild first. Even if they're not there, we can register as adventurers while we're at it.<end>"
KO: "아무튼 이 넓은 거리에서 그 세 녀석을 찾아야 해. 우선 길드에 가 보자. 녀석들이 없어도 겸사겸사 모험가 등록도 할 수 있고.<end>"

## 620048
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あ、あの…、<line-break>冒険者登録したいんでゴブが…。<end>"
EN: "Um, excuse me... I'd like to register as an adventurer, gob.<end>"
KO: "저, 저기… 모험자 등록을 하고 싶다 고브….<end>"

## 620049
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "では、まずここに<line-break>パーティー名を記入してくれ。　<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "First, write your party's name here.<end>"
KO: "그럼 먼저 여기에 파티 이름을 적어줘.<end>"

## 620050
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "な、なんかドキドキするゴブ。<end>"
EN: "I-I'm getting kind of nervous, gob.<end>"
KO: "뭔가 두근두근한다 고브.<end>"

## 620051
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ガント親分、がんばるゴブ。<end>"
EN: "You can do it, Boss Gant, gob.<end>"
KO: "간트 두목, 힘내 고브.<end>"

## 620052
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ごぶごぶだん、　　　　　　　　<line-break>でいいのかい？　　　　　　　　<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "The Gob-Gob Gang. Is that right?<end>"
KO: "고브고브단, 이면 되는 거야?<end>"

## 620053
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "よろしくお願いするでゴブ。<end>"
EN: "Much obliged, gob.<end>"
KO: "잘 부탁한다 고브.<end>"

## 620054
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "幻術計振れておらず、<line-break>犯罪登録にも当てはまらず…。　<line-break>　<line-break>これで登録は終了だ。<line-break>あんたらも今日から冒険者だよ。<line-break>がんばりな。<end>"
EN: "No response from the illusion gauge, and no match in the criminal registry... That completes your registration. You're adventurers as of today. Good luck out there.<end>"
KO: "환술 계통에도 해당되지 않고, 범죄 등록에도 걸리지 않고…. 이걸로 등록은 끝이다. 너희도 오늘부터 모험자야. 힘내.<end>"

## 620055
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "団長！<end>"
EN: "Captain!<end>"
KO: "단장!<end>"

## 620056
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "これでゴブゴブ団も<line-break>公的に認められたゴブ！<line-break>お前たちにも苦労をかけたゴブ！<end>"
EN: "The Gob-Gob Gang is officially recognized now, gob! I've put you through a lot, gob!<end>"
KO: "이걸로 고브고브단도 공식적으로 인정받았다 고브! 너희에게도 고생시켰다 고브!<end>"

## 620057
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "団長！<end>"
EN: "Captain!<end>"
KO: "단장!<end>"

## 620058
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "親分！<end>"
EN: "Boss!<end>"
KO: "두목!<end>"

## 620059
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あーっ！　あんたたち！<end>"
EN: "Ah! You lot!<end>"
KO: "아앗! 너희들!<end>"

## 620060
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "む、またもやガサツ女と<line-break>その他１名。<end>"
EN: "Hmph. That crude woman again, accompanied by one other.<end>"
KO: "흠, 또 그 거친 여자와 기타 한 명인가.<end>"

## 620061
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "親分、どうするゴブ？<end>"
EN: "What do we do, Boss, gob?<end>"
KO: "두목, 어떻게 할 고브?<end>"

## 620062
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "冒険者になって早々に<line-break>問題を起こすのは得策ではないゴブ。<line-break>ここはバラバラに逃げるゴブ。<line-break>マルーン、オルナット、<line-break>スラムで落ち合うゴブ！<line-break>ゴブゴブ団、退却！<end>"
EN: "It would be unwise to cause trouble so soon after becoming adventurers, gob. Split up and run, gob. Maroon, Ornut―we'll meet in the Slums, gob! Gob-Gob Gang, retreat!<end>"
KO: "모험자가 되자마자 문제를 일으키는 건 좋은 수가 아닌 고브. 여기서는 흩어져 도망가는 고브. 마룬, 오르나트, 빈민가에서 만나는 고브! 고브고브단, 퇴각!<end>"

## 620063
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "くすっ、やっぱりゴブリンはバカね。<line-break>合流先を私たちに教えるなんて。<line-break>　<line-break>ついでだからさっさと<line-break>冒険者登録を終わらせちゃって、<line-break>スラムであいつらを待ち伏せましょ。<end>"
EN: "Heh. Goblins really are stupid. They told us exactly where they're meeting. We may as well hurry up and finish registering as adventurers, then lie in wait for them in the Slums.<end>"
KO: "후훗, 역시 고블린은 바보네. 합류 장소를 우리에게 알려 주다니. 내친김에 얼른 모험자 등록을 끝내고 빈민가에서 녀석들을 기다리자.<end>"

## 620064
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "冒険者登録かい？<line-break>そこに名前を書いてくれ。　　<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "Looking to register as an adventurer? Write your name there.<end>"
KO: "모험자 등록이야? 거기에 이름을 써 줘.<end>"

## 620065
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "幻術計振れておらず<line-break>犯罪登録にも当てはまらず…。<line-break>　<line-break>おめでとう。<line-break>あんたも冒険者として<line-break>登録されたよ。<end>"
EN: "No reaction from the illusion gauge, and no match in the criminal registry... Congratulations. You're officially registered as an adventurer.<end>"
KO: "환술계에도 해당하지 않고 범죄 등록에도 걸리지 않고…. 축하해. 당신도 모험자로 등록됐어.<end>"

## 620066
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "これからは、バリバリ仕事して<line-break>名をあげていってくれよ。<line-break>がんばりな！<end>"
EN: "Now get out there, take on plenty of work, and make a name for yourself. Good luck!<end>"
KO: "이제부터 팍팍 일해서 이름을 날려 줘. 힘내!<end>"

## 620067
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "じゃ、行きましょ。<line-break>スラムであいつらを捕まえるのよ。<end>"
EN: "Come on, let's go. We're catching them in the Slums.<end>"
KO: "그럼 가자. 슬럼에서 녀석들을 잡는 거야.<end>"

## 620068
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "まだ、登録が済んでないのに　<line-break>どこへ行くつもりよ。<line-break>　<line-break>カウンターのギルドのおやじに<line-break>話しかければ、すぐ終わるわ。<line-break>さ、早く登録しちゃいましょ。<end>"
EN: "You haven't even registered yet. Where do you think you're going? Just talk to the guy at the Guild counter and you'll be done in no time. Come on, let's get you registered.<end>"
KO: "아직 등록도 안 끝났는데 어디 갈 생각이야. 카운터의 길드 아저씨에게 말 걸면 바로 끝나. 자, 얼른 등록하자.<end>"

## 620069
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お嬢さん、<line-break>少しは落ち着きましたか？<end>"
EN: "Have you calmed down a little, young lady?<end>"
KO: "아가씨, 좀 진정됐습니까?<end>"

## 620080
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "団長、遅いゴブ。<line-break>またどっかで買い食いしてるゴブね。<end>"
EN: "Captain, you're late, gob. Off buying snacks somewhere again, are you, gob?<end>"
KO: "단장, 늦다 고브. 또 어디서 군것질하고 있는 거 고브.<end>"

## 620081
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "はっ！<end>"
EN: "Ah!<end>"
KO: "핫!<end>"

## 620082
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "とうとう捕まえたわ。これで<line-break>あの変な必殺技も使えないわよね。<end>"
EN: "I've finally got you. Now you can't use that strange finishing move of yours, can you?<end>"
KO: "드디어 잡았어. 이제 그 이상한 필살기도 못 쓰겠지.<end>"

## 620083
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "うーん、不覚ゴブ～！<end>"
EN: "Oof, I let my guard down, gob!<end>"
KO: "으음, 방심한 고브~!<end>"

## 620084
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "マルーン、遅れてすまん。<line-break>オルナットが腹減ったとうるさいから、<line-break>つい買い食いしてしまったゴブ。<end>"
EN: "Sorry I'm late, Maroon. Ornut kept complaining about being hungry, so I stopped to get something to eat, gob.<end>"
KO: "마룬, 늦어서 미안하다 고브. 오르나트가 배고프다고 시끄러워서 그만 길거리 음식을 사 먹었다 고브.<end>"

## 620085
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "親分だって、<line-break>そのあといろいろ寄り道したゴブ。<end>"
EN: "You went off on all sorts of detours after that too, Boss, gob.<end>"
KO: "두목도 그 뒤로 여기저기 들렀다 고브.<end>"

## 620086
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "団長～！<end>"
EN: "Boss!<end>"
KO: "단장~!<end>"

## 620087
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "む、マルーンがやられているゴブ！<end>"
EN: "What? Maroon's been captured, gob!<end>"
KO: "마, 마룬이 당하고 있다 고브!<end>"

## 620088
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "来たわね。<line-break>このゴブリンと聖杯とを交換よ。<end>"
EN: "There you are. Hand over the Grail in exchange for this goblin.<end>"
KO: "왔군. 이 고블린과 성배를 교환하는 거야.<end>"

## 620089
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "団長～。<line-break>助けてゴブ～。<end>"
EN: "Boss! Save me, gob!<end>"
KO: "단장~. 도와줘 고브~.<end>"

## 620090
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ほら、どうするの？<end>"
EN: "Well? What are you going to do?<end>"
KO: "자, 어떻게 할 거야?<end>"

## 620091
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…マルーン、すまんでゴブ。<end>"
EN: "Maroon... I'm sorry, gob.<end>"
KO: "…마룬, 미안하다 고브.<end>"

## 620092
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ええ～！？<end>"
EN: "Whaaat?!<end>"
KO: "에에~!?<end>"

## 620093
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "聖杯がなければ、また、<line-break>人間におびえる暮らしが待ってるゴブ。<end>"
EN: "Without the Grail, we'll go right back to living in fear of humans, gob.<end>"
KO: "성배가 없으면 또 인간에게 벌벌 떨며 사는 생활이 기다린다 고브.<end>"

## 620094
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それだけはもうイヤゴブ！　　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>ゴブゴブ団の団長として、個人より<line-break>組織を優先しなければならないことも<line-break>あるのでゴブ。<end>"
EN: "I never want to live like that again, gob! As captain of the Gob-Gob Gang, there are times when I must put the organization before the individual, gob.<end>"
KO: "그것만은 이제 싫다 고브! 고브고브단의 단장으로서 개인보다 조직을 우선해야 할 때도 있다 고브.<end>"

## 620095
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そ、そんな～！　ひどいゴブ～！<end>"
EN: "N-no! That's horrible, gob!<end>"
KO: "그, 그런~! 너무하잖아고블~!<end>"

## 620096
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "だが、お前の尊い犠牲は忘れないゴブ！<line-break>さらばゴブ！<end>"
EN: "But I won't forget your noble sacrifice, gob! Farewell, gob!<end>"
KO: "하지만 네 고귀한 희생은 잊지 않겠다 고브! 잘 가라 고브!<end>"

## 620097
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "親分、どこに逃げるゴブ？<end>"
EN: "Where are you running to, Boss, gob?<end>"
KO: "두목, 어디로 도망가 고브?<end>"

## 620098
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "内海をわたって、エンシャントの<line-break>南東にある賢者の森の奧に行くゴブ。<line-break>そこで何かが呼んでいる気がするゴブ！<end>"
EN: "Across the inland sea, deep into Sage's Forest southeast of Ancient, gob. Something there seems to be calling, gob!<end>"
KO: "내해를 건너 엔샨트 남동쪽 현자의 숲 깊숙이 가는 고브. 거기서 무언가가 부르고 있는 것 같은 고브!<end>"

## 620099
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ちょ、ちょっと、待ちなさいよ！<end>"
EN: "W-wait just a minute!<end>"
KO: "자, 잠깐, 기다려!<end>"

## 620100
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ゴブゴブ団団員として<line-break>誇り高く死ぬ覚悟はできているゴブ。<line-break>　<line-break>煮て食うなり、焼いて食うなり、<line-break>好きにするゴブ！<end>"
EN: "As a member of the Gob-Gob Gang, I'm ready to die with pride, gob. Whether you boil me or roast me, eat me however you like, gob!<end>"
KO: "고브고브단 단원으로서 자랑스럽게 죽을 각오는 되어 있다 고브. 삶아 먹든 구워 먹든 마음대로 해라 고브!<end>"

## 620101
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ゴブリンなんて食べないわよ。<end>"
EN: "I don't eat goblins!<end>"
KO: "고블린 같은 건 안 먹어.<end>"

## 620102
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "じゃあ、何で人間は<line-break>ゴブリンを襲うゴブ？<end>"
EN: "Then why do humans attack goblins, gob?<end>"
KO: "그럼 왜 인간은 고블린을 공격하는 고브?<end>"

## 620103
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ゴブリンが人間を襲うからよ！<end>"
EN: "Because goblins attack humans!<end>"
KO: "고블린이 인간을 습격하니까!<end>"

## 620104
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ゴブリンも、人間がゴブリンを襲うから<line-break>自衛のために戦っているだけゴブ！<end>"
EN: "Goblins only fight in self-defense because humans attack us, gob!<end>"
KO: "고블린도 인간이 고블린을 공격하니까 자위하려고 싸우는 것뿐이다 고브!<end>"

## 620105
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あんたたちはそんなこと<line-break>考えてないでしょ。<line-break>あんたたちは本能で人を襲うのよ。<end>"
EN: "You don't think about any of that. You attack people on instinct.<end>"
KO: "너희는 그런 걸 생각하진 않잖아. 너희는 본능으로 사람을 덮쳐.<end>"

## 620106
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうね、ゴブリンと口ゲンカしてる<line-break>場合じゃなかったわ。<line-break>　<line-break>あいつらは港から内海を<line-break>渡るつもりよ。追いましょ！<line-break>港で捕まえるのよ！<end>"
EN: "Right, this is no time to argue with a goblin. They're planning to cross the Inland Sea from the harbor. Let's go after them! We'll catch them at the harbor!<end>"
KO: "그래, 고블린과 말싸움할 때가 아니었어. 저 녀석들은 항구에서 내해를 건널 생각이야. 쫓아가자! 항구에서 잡는 거야!<end>"

## 620107
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ゴブリン質になってしまったゴブ～。<end>"
EN: "I've become a goblin hostage, gob!<end>"
KO: "고블린 체질이 되어버렸다 고브~.<end>"

## 620108
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "陸路をたどってたら、追いつけないわ！<line-break>今なら港で追いつけるかもしれない。<line-break>港に急ぎましょ！<end>"
EN: "We'll never catch them if we keep going overland! We might still catch them at the port. Let's hurry there!<end>"
KO: "육로로 가면 따라잡을 수 없어! 지금이라면 항구에서 따라잡을지도 몰라. 항구로 서두르자!<end>"

## 620109
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "仕事なんかしてる場合じゃないわ！<line-break>あいつらを追わなきゃ。<end>"
EN: "This is no time to be working! We have to go after them.<end>"
KO: "일 같은 거 할 때가 아니야! 저 녀석들을 쫓아가야 해.<end>"

## 620110
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "親分！　あいつらゴブ！<end>"
EN: "Boss! It's them, gob!<end>"
KO: "두목! 저놈들 고브!<end>"

## 620111
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なんで、あいつらには<line-break>こっちの居場所がわかるゴブ？　<line-break>　<line-break>オルナット、<line-break>早く船に乗るゴブ！<end>"
EN: "How do they know where we are, gob? Ornut, hurry and get on the ship, gob!<end>"
KO: "왜 녀석들은 우리가 있는 곳을 아는 고브? 오르나트, 빨리 배에 타는 고브!<end>"

## 620112
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あいつらは、エンシャント行きの<line-break>船に乗り込むつもりだわ。<line-break>追うわよ！<end>"
EN: "They're planning to board the ship to Ancient. After them!<end>"
KO: "저 녀석들은 엔샨트행 배를 탈 생각이야. 쫓아가자!<end>"

## 620113
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "何すんのよ。<line-break>あいつらを見失っちゃったじゃない！<end>"
EN: "What do you think you're doing? Now we've lost them!<end>"
KO: "뭐 하는 거야. 녀석들을 놓쳤잖아!<end>"

## 620114
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おぉーっと、ガキどもぉ、<line-break>いいこと教えてやる…。<line-break>船に乗るにはな、金がいるんだよ！<end>"
EN: "Not so fast, brats. Let me teach you something... You need money to board a ship!<end>"
KO: "어이, 꼬맹이들. 좋은 거 하나 알려 주지…. 배를 타려면 말이야, 돈이 필요해!<end>"

## 620115
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "私たちはお客よ。<line-break>ほら。<end>"
EN: "We're paying passengers. See?<end>"
KO: "우리는 손님이야. 봐.<end>"

## 620116
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "これはこれは。ようこそ。<line-break>ごゆっくりなさってください。　　<line-break>快適な船旅をお約束しますよ。<end>"
EN: "Well, well. Welcome aboard. Make yourselves comfortable. I promise you a pleasant voyage.<end>"
KO: "이런 이런. 어서 오십시오. 느긋하게 쉬다 가세요. 쾌적한 뱃길을 약속드리지요.<end>"

## 620117
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "もう！　<line-break><value:$28>、急ぐわよ！<end>"
EN: "Come on! Hurry, <value:$28>!<end>"
KO: "정말! <value:$28>, 서두르자!<end>"

## 620118
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "確かにこの船に乗ったはずなのに…。<line-break>あいつら、どこへ行ったのかしら？<end>"
EN: "I'm sure they boarded this ship... Where could they have gone?<end>"
KO: "분명 이 배에 탔을 텐데…. 녀석들, 어디로 간 걸까?<end>"

## 620119
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "結局、船の中では<line-break>見つからなかったわね。<end>"

## 620120
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "親分、腹減ったゴブ～。<end>"
EN: "Boss, I'm hungry, gob.<end>"
KO: "두목, 배고프다 고브~.<end>"

## 620121
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "む、仕方ないゴブ。<line-break>奴らに見つからないように<line-break>荷物の中に隠れてたゴブからな。<end>"
EN: "Hmm. Can't be helped, gob. We've been hiding in the cargo so they wouldn't find us.<end>"
KO: "음, 어쩔 수 없다 고브. 놈들에게 들키지 않으려고 짐 속에 숨어 있었으니 고브.<end>"

## 620122
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あーっ！！<end>"
EN: "Ah!!<end>"
KO: "아앗!!<end>"

## 620123
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "団長、逃げるゴブ！<end>"
EN: "Captain, run, gob!<end>"
KO: "단장, 도망치는 고브!<end>"

## 620124
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "む、ガサツ女とその他一名。<line-break>そしてマルーン！<end>"

KO: "흥, 거친 여자와 그 밖의 한 명. 그리고 마룬!<end>"

## 620125
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "やっぱり見つかったゴブ。<line-break>これなら普通に船に乗って<line-break>見つかった方がましだったでゴブ。<end>"
EN: "They found us after all, gob. We'd have been better off boarding normally and getting caught, gob.<end>"
KO: "역시 들킨 고브. 이럴 거면 그냥 배를 타고 들키는 편이 나았던 고브.<end>"

## 620126
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "オルナット、<line-break>あきらめてはいかんでゴブ！<end>"
