KO: "오르나트, 포기하면 안 된다 고브!<end>"

## 620127
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あきらめなければ、<line-break>あの地平の向こうにきっと、<line-break>幸せの大地が待ってるゴブ。<end>"
EN: "If we never give up, the land of happiness will surely be waiting beyond that horizon, gob.<end>"
KO: "포기하지 않으면 저 지평선 너머에 분명 행복의 땅이 기다리고 있다 고브.<end>"

## 620128
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "親分、自分が間違ってたゴブ！<end>"
EN: "Boss, I was wrong, gob!<end>"
KO: "두목, 내가 틀렸어 고브!<end>"

## 620129
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あんたたち！<line-break>今度は逃がさないわ！<end>"
EN: "You lot! You're not getting away this time!<end>"
KO: "너희들! 이번엔 놓치지 않을 거야!<end>"

## 620130
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "わかってるわよ。<line-break>所持金が船賃には足りないんでしょ。<line-break>　<line-break>任せときなさいって。<line-break>こういう時にこそ、<line-break>私の美貌が役に立つのよ。<end>"
EN: "I know. We don't have enough money for the fare, right? Leave it to me. This is exactly when my beauty comes in handy.<end>"
KO: "알고 있어. 가진 돈이 뱃삯에 모자라지? 맡겨 두라니까. 이럴 때야말로 내 미모가 도움이 되는 거야.<end>"

## 620131
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "何よ、その疑わしそうな目は。<line-break>いいわ、女の武器の使い方、<line-break>見せてあげるから。<end>"
EN: "What's with that doubtful look? Fine. I'll show you how a woman uses her charms.<end>"
KO: "뭐야, 그 의심스러운 눈은. 좋아, 여자의 무기 쓰는 법을 보여 줄게.<end>"

## 620132
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "うるうるうる…。<end>"
EN: "Sniffle, sniffle...<end>"
KO: "울먹울먹울먹….<end>"

## 620133
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "んだあ？　船賃はあるのか？<line-break>　<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "Huh? You got the fare?<end>"
KO: "뭐야? 뱃삯은 있냐?<end>"

## 620134
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ボルダンのおじさぁん。<line-break>船賃はないのぉ。　　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>でもぉ、エンシャントでは<line-break>一人暮らしのおばあさんが<line-break>病気になっててぇ、<end>"
EN: "Oh, Mr. Boldan... We don't have any money for the fare. But there's an old woman living all alone in Ancient, and she's sick...<end>"
KO: "볼단 아저씨~. 뱃삯은 없어~. 하지만 엔샨트에서는 혼자 사는 할머니가 병이 나서….<end>"

## 620135
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お見舞いの品を届けに<line-break>行かなくちゃいけないのぉ。<line-break>お願いぃ、お船に乗せてぇ。<end>"
EN: "We have to bring her a get-well gift. Pleeease, let us on your ship.<end>"
KO: "문병 선물을 전하러 가야 해애. 부탁이야아, 배 좀 태워 줘어.<end>"

## 620137
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…なんだか気持ち悪いが、　<line-break>ま、いいだろ。<line-break>　<line-break>そっちの男はともかく、<line-break>こっちの姉ちゃんは、<line-break>へへ、結構いい感じだしな。<end>"
EN: "...Something about that makes my skin crawl, but all right. Forget the guy―this young lady's pretty easy on the eyes. Heh heh.<end>"
KO: "…뭔가 기분 나쁘지만 뭐, 됐어. 저쪽 남자는 그렇다 치고, 이쪽 누나는 헤헤, 꽤 괜찮으니까.<end>"

## 620138
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "船に乗んな！<end>"
EN: "Get aboard!<end>"
KO: "배에 타지 마!<end>"

## 620139
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "見なさい！<line-break>これが美貌のなせる技よ！<end>"
EN: "See? That's the power of beauty!<end>"
KO: "보라고! 이게 미모가 만들어 내는 기술이야!<end>"

## 620140
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "っるさいわねー。文句言ってないで、<line-break>早く船に乗るわよ。<end>"
EN: "Oh, shut up. Quit complaining and get on the ship already.<end>"
KO: "시끄럽네. 불평 말고 얼른 배에 타.<end>"

## 620141
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いや、<value:$28>の<line-break>言ってるのは文句じゃなくてゴブね。<line-break>　<line-break>その、危ないゴブよ。<line-break>もっと、自分を大切にした方が<line-break>いいゴブよ。<end>"
EN: "No, <value:$28> wasn't complaining, gob. It's just... that was dangerous, gob. You should take better care of yourself.<end>"
KO: "아니, <value:$28>이 하는 말은 불평이 아니다 고브. 그게, 위험하다 고브. 좀 더 자신을 소중히 하는 게 좋다 고브.<end>"

## 620142
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あのガサツ女、<line-break>顔はかわいいのに…。　　　　　<line-break>お前も女運悪いゴブね。<line-break>でも、マジメにがんばってれば、<line-break>きっと幸せになれるゴブよ。<end>"
EN: "That crude woman is so pretty, too... You sure have rotten luck with women, gob. But keep working hard and I'm sure you'll find happiness someday.<end>"
KO: "그 거친 여자, 얼굴은 귀여운데…. 너도 여자 운이 없구나 고브. 그래도 성실하게 노력하면 분명 행복해질 거다 고브.<end>"

## 620143
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ううー、何であんなに<line-break>こき使われなきゃならないのよー！<end>"
EN: "Ugh, why did they have to work me to the bone like that?!<end>"
KO: "으으, 왜 그렇게 혹사당해야 하는 거야아!<end>"

## 620144
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>はいいわよ。<line-break>ボーイだもん。　　　　　　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>なんで、か弱き乙女の私が、<line-break>ボルダンの筋肉ダルマに混じって、<line-break>貨物を運ばなくちゃいけないのよ！<end>"
EN: "<value:$28> has it easy. He's a boy. Why does a frail maiden like me have to haul cargo alongside all those musclebound Boldans?!<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>는 좋겠지. 남자니까. 왜 가녀린 소녀인 내가 볼단의 근육 덩어리들 사이에 끼어서 짐을 날라야 하는 거야!<end>"

## 620145
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "至極妥当な気がするゴブ。<end>"
EN: "That seems entirely reasonable, gob.<end>"
KO: "지극히 타당한 것 같다 고브.<end>"

## 620146
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "っるさいわねー。<line-break>おかげでなんか体中に筋肉が<line-break>ついちゃったような気がするわ。<end>"
EN: "Oh, shut up. Thanks to all that work, I feel like I've grown muscles all over.<end>"
KO: "시끄럽네! 덕분에 온몸에 근육이 붙어 버린 것 같잖아.<end>"

## 620147
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

EN: "Irene's ＳＴＲ increased by 3.<end>"
KO: "아이린의 STR이 3 올랐다.<end>"

## 620148
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "よお、あんたすげえな。<line-break>ボルダンの男衆も顔負けだぜ。<line-break>ガハハハ…！<line-break>おかげで仕事がはかどった。<line-break>これは少ねえが駄賃だ。<line-break>とっときな。<end>"
EN: "Hey, you're something else! You put the Boldan men to shame. Gahahaha! The work went a lot faster thanks to you. It ain't much, but here's a little something for your trouble. Take it.<end>"
KO: "오, 자네 대단하군. 볼단 남자들도 울고 가겠어. 가하하하! 덕분에 일이 잘됐어. 적지만 품삯이야. 받아 둬.<end>"

## 620149
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

KO: "100기어를 손에 넣었다.<end>"

## 620151
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 620152
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 620153
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "快適な船旅だったゴブ。<end>"
EN: "That was a comfortable voyage, gob.<end>"

## 620154

## 620155
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それもこれも聖杯の力ゴブ！<line-break>この聖杯はきっと我らを<line-break>幸せの地へと連れていってくれるゴブ！<end>"
EN: "We owe it all to the power of the Grail, gob! This Grail is sure to lead us to the land of happiness, gob!<end>"
KO: "이것도 전부 성배의 힘인 고브! 이 성배는 분명 우리를 행복의 땅으로 데려다줄 고브!<end>"

## 620156
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あーっ！！<end>"
EN: "Ah!!<end>"
KO: "아앗!!<end>"

## 620157
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "む、ガサツ女とその他１名。<line-break>そしてマルーン！<end>"
EN: "Hmm. The uncouth woman and one other person. And Maroon!<end>"
KO: "흥, 거친 여자와 그 밖의 한 명. 그리고 마룬!<end>"

## 620158
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あんたたち、<line-break>今度は逃がさないわよ！<end>"
EN: "You lot aren't getting away this time!<end>"
KO: "너희들, 이번엔 놓치지 않을 거야!<end>"

## 620159
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "む、ゴブゴブ団、我らの幸せの待つ<line-break>賢者の森へ、進軍ゴブ！<end>"
EN: "Hmm. Gob-Gob Gang, march on Sage's Forest, where our happiness awaits, gob!<end>"
KO: "자, 고브고브단! 우리 행복이 기다리는 현자의 숲으로 진군 고브!<end>"

## 620160
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>、ボーっとしてないで<line-break>連中を賢者の森まで追いかけるのよ！<end>"
EN: "Don't just stand there, <value:$28>! Chase them all the way to Sage's Forest!<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>, 멍하니 있지 말고 저들을 현자의 숲까지 쫓아가자!<end>"

## 620161
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "く、苦しいゴブ…。<end>"
EN: "I-it hurts, gob...<end>"
KO: "크, 괴로운 고브….<end>"

## 620162
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "賢者の森まで、追っかけたけど<line-break>見失っちゃったわ…。<end>"
EN: "I chased them all the way to the Sage's Forest, but I lost them...<end>"
KO: "현자의 숲까지 쫓아갔는데 놓쳐 버렸어….<end>"

## 620163
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "当たり前ゴブ、団長がお前らごときに<line-break>逃げ足で負けるはずがないゴブ！<end>"
EN: "Of course, gob! The captain would never be outrun by the likes of you, gob!<end>"
KO: "당연하다 고브, 단장이 너희 따위에게 도망치는 속도로 질 리 없다 고브!<end>"

## 620164
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なによ！<line-break>っるさいわねー！<end>"
EN: "What?! Oh, shut up!<end>"
KO: "뭐야! 시끄럽다니까-!<end>"

## 620165
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ゴブー！<end>"
EN: "Gob!<end>"
KO: "고부~!<end>"

## 620166
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

EN: "Ow!<end>"
KO: "아야!<end>"

## 620167
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ううん、大丈夫。<line-break>油断したわ、逃げられちゃうなんて。<end>"
EN: "No, I'm fine. I let my guard down. I can't believe they got away.<end>"
KO: "응, 괜찮아. 방심했네, 놓쳐 버리다니.<end>"

## 620168
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "とにかく、急いであいつらを<line-break>追わなきゃ。<end>"
EN: "Anyway, we have to hurry after them.<end>"
KO: "어쨌든 빨리 녀석들을 쫓아가야 해.<end>"

## 620169
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "行きましょ、<value:$28>。<end>"
EN: "Come on, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "가죠, <value:$28>.<end>"

## 620170
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "今さら何言ってるのよ。<line-break>ロストールに帰りたくないの？　<line-break>　<line-break>聖杯も取り戻さずに、おめおめと<line-break>迷子の猫が舞い戻るみたいに<line-break>帰っても、恥ずかしくないの？<end>"
EN: "What are you talking about, after all this? Don't you want to go back to Rostorl? Wouldn't you be ashamed to slink back home like a lost kitten, without even recovering the Grail?<end>"
KO: "이제 와서 무슨 소리야. 로스톨로 돌아가기 싫어? 성배도 되찾지 못하고 길 잃은 고양이처럼 돌아가도 안 부끄러워?<end>"

## 620171
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "もう、うつむいてないで！<line-break>ほぉーら、行くわよ！<end>"
EN: "Oh, stop hanging your head! Come on, let's go!<end>"
KO: "이제 고개 숙이고 있지 마! 자, 가자!<end>"

## 620172
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "親分、ここゴブか。<end>"
EN: "Boss, is this the place, gob?<end>"
KO: "두목, 여기 고브인가.<end>"

## 620173
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "む。<end>"
EN: "Hm.<end>"
KO: "음.<end>"

## 620174
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "よくぞ来た。<line-break>闇の子らよ…。<end>"
EN: "You have done well to come, children of darkness...<end>"
KO: "잘 왔다. 어둠의 아이들이여….<end>"

## 620175
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ね、猫がしゃべったゴブ。<end>"
EN: "Th-the cat talked, gob.<end>"
KO: "고, 고양이가 말했어 고브.<end>"

## 620176
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フフ、驚くことはない。　　　　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>俺は猫の姿に封じ込められてはいるが、<line-break>本来は魔界の将。<line-break>闇の神器、禁断の聖杯の管理者…<end>"
EN: "Heh. There is no cause for alarm. Though I am sealed within the form of a cat, I am truly a general of the demon realm, keeper of the Forbidden Grail, a Dark Artifact...<end>"
KO: "후후, 놀랄 것 없다. 나는 고양이 모습에 갇혀 있지만 본래 마계의 장수. 어둠의 신기, 금단의 성배의 관리자다….<end>"

## 620177
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "破壊神ウルグの円卓騎士　　　　　　　<line-break>名前を知るものネモ様なのだからな！<line-break>　<line-break>さあ、その聖杯の力で俺の封印を解け！<line-break>そして、ともに破壊と絶望の世界を、<line-break>お前たち闇の者の帝国を築くのだ！！<end>"
EN: "For I am Lord Nemo, he who knows the Name, one of Ulugh the God of Destruction's Knights of the Round Table! Now use the Grail's power to break my seal! Together we shall build a world of destruction and despair, an empire for you creatures of darkness!<end>"
KO: "파괴신 울그의 원탁기사, 이름을 아는 자 네모 님이시니까 말이다! 자, 그 성배의 힘으로 내 봉인을 풀어라! 그리고 함께 파괴와 절망의 세계를, 너희 어둠의 자들의 제국을 세우는 거다!!<end>"

## 620178
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "うそ臭いゴブ。<end>"
EN: "Sounds fake, gob.<end>"
KO: "수상쩍다 고브.<end>"

## 620179
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "破壊と絶望の世界なんていらないゴブ。<line-break>今でもメシはおいしいゴブ。<line-break>それで、十分ゴブ。<end>"
EN: "We don't need a world of destruction and despair, gob. Food tastes good already, gob. That's enough, gob.<end>"
KO: "파괴와 절망의 세계 따윈 필요 없는 고브. 지금도 밥은 맛있는 고브. 그걸로 충분한 고브.<end>"

## 620180
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "こんな不細工で性格悪そうな子猫が<line-break>我らに幸せをくれるとは思えないゴブ。<line-break>他の人がいるはずゴブ。<end>"
EN: "Can't see an ugly little kitten who looks this mean bringing us happiness, gob. It must be someone else, gob.<end>"
KO: "이렇게 못생기고 성격 나빠 보이는 새끼 고양이가 우리에게 행복을 줄 것 같지 않다 고브. 다른 사람이 있을 거다 고브.<end>"

## 620181
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "こいつら～。　　　　　　　　　<line-break>お前らを操ってたのは俺なんだぞ！<line-break>　<line-break>聖杯を盗むようにしむけたのも、　<line-break>ここに来るようにしむけたのも、<line-break>全部俺なんだよ！<end>"
EN: "Why, you... I'm the one who's been controlling you! I made you steal the Grail, and I led you here! It was all me!<end>"
KO: "이 녀석들! 너희를 조종한 건 나라고! 성배를 훔치게 한 것도, 여기 오게 한 것도 전부 나야!<end>"

## 620182
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "俺の言うことを<line-break>さっさと聞いてだな、<line-break>早く俺をもとの姿に戻せ！　　　　<end>"
EN: "Now hurry up and do as I say! Restore me to my true form at once!<end>"
KO: "내 말 좀 빨리 듣고, 얼른 나를 원래 모습으로 되돌려 놔!<end>"

## 620183
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "はーん。最近、妙におとなしいと<line-break>思ったら、そんなことを<line-break>企んでいたんですね、ネモ？<end>"
EN: "Ah. I wondered why you'd been so strangely quiet lately. So this is what you were plotting, Nemo?<end>"
KO: "하아. 요즘 이상하게 얌전하다 했더니 그런 걸 꾸미고 있었군요, 네모?<end>"

## 620184
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "んにゃーん、にゃーん。<end>"
EN: "Mrrrow, meow.<end>"
KO: "냐아앙, 냐아앙.<end>"

## 620185
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ごまかしてもムダです。<line-break>私は、この屋敷を住みかにしていた<line-break>猫の顔をすべて覚えていますから。<line-break>もっとも、今となっては、あなたの<line-break>不気味な気配におびえて、この屋敷に<line-break>やってくる猫はいなくなりましたがね。<end>"
EN: "There is no use pretending. I remember the face of every cat that made this manor its home. Though by now, your sinister presence has frightened them all away. No cats come here anymore.<end>"
KO: "속여도 소용없습니다. 저는 이 저택을 보금자리로 삼았던 고양이들의 얼굴을 모두 기억하고 있으니까요. 물론 지금은 당신의 섬뜩한 기척에 겁먹어 이 저택에 찾아오는 고양이는 없어졌지만요.<end>"

## 620186
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "この人ゴブ！<line-break>ピンときたゴブ！　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>やさしそうだし、美形だし、<line-break>結構実力ありそうだし、<line-break>実は性格悪そうだし。<end>"
EN: "It's this person, gob! I can feel it, gob! Looks kind, handsome, and pretty strong―and secretly mean, too.<end>"
KO: "이 사람이다 고브! 느낌이 왔다 고브! 상냥해 보이고, 잘생겼고, 꽤 실력도 있어 보이고, 사실 성격은 나빠 보이고.<end>"

## 620187
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "この人こそ<line-break>我らを幸せの大地に　　　<line-break>導いてくれるゴブ！<end>"
EN: "This is the one who will lead us to a land of happiness, gob!<end>"
KO: "이분이야말로 우리를 행복의 땅으로 이끌어 줄 분이다 고브!<end>"

## 620188
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ゴブゴブ団の<line-break>ガント団長さんですね？<end>"
EN: "You are Captain Gant of the Gob-Gob Gang, are you not?<end>"
KO: "고브고브단의 간트 단장님이시죠?<end>"

## 620189
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "は、はいゴブ！<end>"
EN: "Y-yes, gob!<end>"
KO: "네, 넵 고브!<end>"

## 620190
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "団員の方が森の中でモンスターに<line-break>襲われて大変でしたよ。<line-break>　<line-break>マルーンさん、大丈夫です。<line-break>いらっしゃい。<end>"
EN: "One of your members was attacked by monsters in the forest. It was quite an ordeal. It's all right, Maroon. Come in.<end>"
KO: "단원분이 숲에서 몬스터에게 습격당해 큰일이었어요. 마룬 씨, 괜찮습니다. 어서 오세요.<end>"

## 620191
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "団長！<end>"
EN: "Captain!<end>"
KO: "단장!<end>"

## 620192
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "マルーン！<end>"
EN: "Maroon!<end>"
KO: "마룬!<end>"

## 620193
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あいつらから逃げて、団長を追ってたら<line-break>モンスターに囲まれて…<line-break>　<line-break>このオルファウス様が<line-break>助けてくれなかったら、<line-break>どうなってたかわからないゴブ！<end>"
EN: "I escaped from them and went after you, Captain, but then monsters surrounded me... If Lord Orphaus hadn't saved me, who knows what would've happened, gob!<end>"
KO: "그놈들에게서 도망쳐 단장을 쫓다가 몬스터에게 포위됐고… 이 오르파우스 님이 구해 주지 않았다면 어떻게 됐을지 모른다 고브!<end>"

## 620194
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…スマンでゴブ。<line-break>苦労をかけたゴブ。<end>"
EN: "I'm sorry, gob. I've put you through a lot, gob.<end>"
KO: "…미안하다고브. 고생시켰다고브.<end>"

## 620195
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "団長！　気にしないでほしいゴブ！<line-break>団長が組織のために、苦しい決断を<line-break>したことはわかってるゴブ！<end>"
EN: "Captain! Please don't worry about it, gob! I know you had to make a painful decision for the sake of the organization, gob!<end>"
KO: "단장! 신경 쓰지 말아 달라 고브! 단장이 조직을 위해 괴로운 결단을 내렸다는 건 알고 있다 고브!<end>"

## 620196
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "オルファウス様！　お願いゴブ！<line-break>我らを幸せにして欲しいゴブ！<end>"
EN: "Lord Orphaus! Please, gob! Make us happy, gob!<end>"
KO: "올파우스 님! 부탁이다 고브! 우리를 행복하게 해줬으면 한다 고브!<end>"

## 620197
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それは無理ですね。<end>"
EN: "I cannot do that.<end>"
KO: "그건 무리네요.<end>"

## 620198
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "どうしてゴブ？<end>"
EN: "Why not, gob?<end>"
KO: "왜 고브?<end>"

## 620199
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "幸せは、最終的には自分の手で<line-break>つかむものだからですよ。<end>"
EN: "Because in the end, happiness is something you must seize with your own hands.<end>"
KO: "행복은 결국 자신의 손으로 붙잡는 것이니까요.<end>"

## 620200
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そんな…。<end>"
EN: "But...<end>"
KO: "그럴 수가….<end>"

## 620201
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それにその聖杯…。<line-break>それは手放した方がいいですよ、　<line-break>ガント団長さん。<line-break>それは破壊神の闇の神器といって、<line-break>強い力を与えてくれますが、<line-break>同時に人を不幸にしますから。<end>"
EN: "And that Grail... You would be wise to relinquish it, Captain Gant. It is a Dark Artifact belonging to the God of Destruction. It grants great power, but it also brings misery to people.<end>"
KO: "그리고 그 성배…. 그건 놓아버리는 게 좋습니다, 간트 단장님. 파괴신의 어둠의 신기라 불리는 물건으로 강한 힘을 주지만 동시에 사람을 불행하게 하니까요.<end>"

## 620202
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それはできないゴブ！<line-break>これがないと我らはもとの暮らしに…！<end>"
EN: "We can't do that, gob! Without it, we'll go back to living the way we did before...!<end>"
KO: "그럴 수 없다 고브! 이게 없으면 우리는 원래 생활로…!<end>"

## 620203
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それはありません。よくも悪くも、<line-break>あなたは禁断の聖杯の力を<line-break>手に入れてしまったのです。<line-break>たとえ聖杯を失っても、　　　<line-break>その力を失うことは<line-break>できないでしょう。<end>"
EN: "No, you will not. For better or worse, you have already acquired the power of the Forbidden Grail. Even if you lose the Grail itself, you will probably be unable to lose that power.<end>"
KO: "그럴 일은 없습니다. 좋든 나쁘든 당신은 금단의 성배의 힘을 손에 넣었습니다. 성배를 잃는다 해도 그 힘까지 잃을 수는 없을 겁니다.<end>"

## 620204
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "だけど…。<end>"
EN: "But...<end>"
KO: "하지만….<end>"

## 620205
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あーっ、あんたたち！<end>"
EN: "There you are!<end>"
KO: "아ー, 너희들!<end>"

## 620206
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おやおや、またお客様だ。<end>"
EN: "Oh my, another guest.<end>"
KO: "이런 이런, 또 손님이군.<end>"

## 620207
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あんたたち、今度こそ逃がさないわよ！<line-break>聖杯を返しなさい！<end>"
EN: "You're not getting away this time! Give back the Grail!<end>"
KO: "너희들, 이번엔 절대 못 도망가! 성배를 돌려줘!<end>"

## 620208
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "オルファウス様、さよならゴブ！<line-break>今は聖杯は渡せないゴブ！　だけど、<line-break>いつか我らなりに答えを出すゴブ！<end>"
EN: "Farewell, Lord Orphaus, gob! We can't surrender the Grail yet, gob! But someday we'll find our own answer, gob!<end>"
KO: "오르파우스 님, 안녕 고브! 지금은 성배를 줄 수 없다 고브! 하지만 언젠가 우리 나름의 답을 내겠다 고브!<end>"

## 620209
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "どいてよ！<line-break>あいつら逃げちゃうじゃない！<end>"
EN: "Move! They're getting away!<end>"
KO: "비켜! 저 녀석들 도망가잖아!<end>"

## 620210
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "私の家に争いを持ち込まないで<line-break>ほしいですねえ。<end>"
EN: "I would rather you not bring your quarrels into my home.<end>"
KO: "제 집에 다툼을 끌고 오지는 말아 주세요.<end>"

## 620211
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…無限のソウルを持つ者？<line-break><line-break><line-break>フフフ。<line-break>運命の女神はどうしてこう<line-break>あざといことが好きなんでしょうねえ。<end>"
EN: "The Bearer of the Infinite Soul...? Heh heh heh. Why is the Goddess of Fate so fond of such transparent little contrivances?<end>"
KO: "…무한의 소울을 가진 자? 후후후. 운명의 여신은 어째서 이렇게 얄미운 장난을 좋아하는 걸까요.<end>"

## 620212
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>、下がって…。<line-break>あなたのかなう…相手じゃ…ない…。<end>"
EN: "Get back, <value:$28>... This is... beyond you...<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>, 물러나…. 네가 감당할… 상대가… 아니야….<end>"

## 620213
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お願い、<value:$28>。<line-break>私、あなたを守りたいの…。<end>"
EN: "Please, <value:$28>. I want to protect you...<end>"
KO: "부탁해, <value:$28>. 나, 당신을 지키고 싶어….<end>"

## 620215
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "みなさん、そう肩肘を張らないで。<line-break>どうでしょう、<line-break>中でお茶にでもしませんか？<end>"
EN: "Please, everyone, there is no need to be so tense. Why don't we go inside for some tea?<end>"
KO: "여러분, 그렇게 긴장하지 마세요. 어떠세요, 안에서 차라도 한잔하지 않으시겠어요?<end>"

## 620216
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "私も、あなたたちに<line-break>お話ししたいことがありますから。<end>"
EN: "There are matters I wish to discuss with you as well.<end>"
KO: "저도 여러분께 드릴 말씀이 있으니까요.<end>"

## 620217
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "どうするの？<line-break><value:$28>。<end>"
EN: "What do we do, <value:$28>?<end>"
KO: "어떻게 할 거야? <value:$28>.<end>"

## 620218
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "だめよ。だって…。<end>"
EN: "No. We can't. Because...<end>"
KO: "안 돼. 왜냐면….<end>"

## 620219
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…そうね。<line-break>殺すつもりなら、<line-break>とっくにそうしてるわよね。<line-break>…くやしいけど、恐かった。<end>"
EN: "I suppose so. If he meant to kill us, he'd have done it already. I hate to admit it, but I was afraid.<end>"
KO: "…그렇네. 죽일 생각이었다면 진작 그랬겠지. …분하지만 무서웠어.<end>"

## 620220
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "どうですか？<end>"
EN: "Well? How is it?<end>"
KO: "어떻습니까?<end>"

## 620221
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あ、あの、<line-break>とてもおいしかったです。　　　<line-break>　<line-break>あの、ほんと、<line-break>お世辞なんかじゃなくて…、<line-break>とても、やさしい味がしました。<end>"
EN: "Um... it was delicious. Really, I'm not just being polite. It had such a... gentle taste.<end>"
KO: "아, 저기, 정말 맛있었어요. 정말 빈말이 아니라… 아주 다정한 맛이 났어요.<end>"

## 620222
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ありがとう。私が作ったんです。<line-break>何よりのほめ言葉ですよ。<end>"
EN: "Thank you. I made it myself. I couldn't ask for higher praise.<end>"
KO: "고마워요. 제가 만든 거예요. 최고의 칭찬이네요.<end>"

## 620223
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あ、あの…。<line-break>す、すみませんでした。<end>"
EN: "Um... I-I'm sorry.<end>"
KO: "저, 저기…. 죄, 죄송했습니다.<end>"

## 620224
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いいえ、私も手荒な真似をしました。<line-break>ところで、あのゴブリンたちは<line-break>あなたのお友達ですか？<end>"
EN: "No, I was rather rough with you as well. By the way, are those Goblins friends of yours?<end>"
KO: "아닙니다, 저도 거칠게 행동했습니다. 그런데 그 고블린들은 당신 친구입니까?<end>"

## 620225
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そんなんじゃないです！<line-break>あいつらは私たちの町から<line-break>聖杯を盗んで逃げたんです。　<line-break>私たちは<line-break>それを取り戻しにきたんです。<end>"
EN: "Of course not! They stole the Grail from our town and ran away. We came to get it back.<end>"
KO: "그런 게 아니에요! 저 녀석들은 우리 마을에서 성배를 훔쳐 달아났어요. 우리는 그걸 되찾으러 온 거예요.<end>"

## 620226
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ふぅーん、それなら、<line-break>あのゴブリンは町の恩人ですね。<end>"
EN: "Hmm. In that case, that Goblin is the town's benefactor.<end>"
KO: "흐음, 그렇다면 그 고블린은 이 마을의 은인이네요.<end>"

## 620227
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なんで！<line-break>…どうしてですか？<end>"
EN: "What?! Why would you say that?<end>"
KO: "왜! …어째서입니까?<end>"

## 620228
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あれは闇の神器というもののひとつで、<line-break>強大な力を持つと同時に<line-break>人に不幸を呼ぶ魔道器なのです。<end>"
EN: "It is one of the Dark Artifacts: a magical device that possesses tremendous power, but also brings misfortune upon people.<end>"
KO: "저건 어둠의 신기 중 하나로, 강대한 힘을 지닌 동시에 사람에게 불행을 부르는 마도기입니다.<end>"

## 620229
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "でも、あれはずーっと<line-break>町にまつられてて、別に何も…。<end>"
EN: "But it was enshrined in our town for ages, and nothing ever happened...<end>"
KO: "하지만 그건 계속 마을에 모셔져 있었고, 딱히 아무 일도….<end>"

## 620230
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それは神器が眠っていたからです。<line-break>それをこのバカ猫が<line-break>起こしてしまいましたがね。<end>"
EN: "That is because the Artifact was asleep. This foolish cat went and awakened it, however.<end>"
KO: "그건 신기가 잠들어 있었기 때문입니다. 그런데 이 멍청한 고양이가 깨워 버렸죠.<end>"

## 620231
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "でも、私たち、あの聖杯を求めて<line-break>町を出たんです。あれを取り戻さないと<line-break>ロストールに戻れません…。<line-break>あ、私は、騎士になるから<line-break>町には戻らないつもりですけど…。<line-break><value:$28>は…。<end>"
EN: "But we left town to find that Grail. If we don't get it back, we can't return to Rostorl... Well, I mean, I'm going to become a knight, so I wasn't planning to go back anyway. But <value:$28>...<end>"
KO: "하지만 우리는 그 성배를 찾으러 마을을 나왔어요. 그걸 되찾지 않으면 로스톨로 돌아갈 수 없어요…. 아, 저는 기사가 될 거라 마을엔 돌아가지 않을 생각이지만…. <value:$28>는….<end>"

## 620232
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "アイリーン。人は自分をオリに<line-break>閉じこめてしまわない限り、<line-break>なんだってできるんですよ。　　　　<line-break>夜も更けてきました。<line-break>今日はここで休んでいってください。<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "Irene, people can do anything, so long as they don't lock themselves inside a cage of their own making. It's getting late. Please stay here and rest tonight.<end>"
KO: "아이린. 사람은 스스로를 우리에 가두지만 않으면 무엇이든 할 수 있어요. 밤도 깊었네요. 오늘은 여기서 쉬고 가세요.<end>"

## 620233
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ゴブリンの諸君の行き先は　　　　　<line-break>その間に、私が探知しておきましょう。<end>"
EN: "In the meantime, I'll find out where those Goblins are headed.<end>"
KO: "고블린 여러분이 향하는 곳은 그사이에 제가 탐지해 두겠습니다.<end>"

## 620234
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "よろしく、お願いします。<end>"
EN: "Please do. Thank you.<end>"
KO: "잘 부탁드립니다.<end>"

## 620235
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "神器を守る隠れ里…ミイスの村が　　<line-break>焼き払われていました。<line-break>　<line-break>破壊神の円卓騎士アーギルシャイア、<line-break>ついに闇の神器のひとつ<line-break>忘却の仮面を手に入れたようですね。<end>"
EN: "Miis Village, the hidden settlement charged with guarding an Artifact, has been burned to the ground. It appears that Argyleshire, one of the God of Destruction's Knights of the Round Table, has finally obtained one of the Dark Artifacts: the Mask of Oblivion.<end>"
KO: "신기를 지키는 숨겨진 마을… 미이스 마을이 불타 버렸습니다. 파괴신의 원탁기사 아르길샤이어가 마침내 어둠의 신기 중 하나인 망각의 가면을 손에 넣은 모양이군요.<end>"

## 620236
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ネモ、知っているんでしょう？<line-break>アーギルシャイアの次のねらいを。　<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "Nemo, you know what Argyleshire is after next, don't you?<end>"
KO: "네모, 알고 있지? 아르길샤이어의 다음 목표를.<end>"

## 620237
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あんたの予想通りだ。<end>"
EN: "Just as you predicted.<end>"
KO: "네 예상대로야.<end>"

## 620238
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あの気まぐれ女、<line-break>お母さんになりたいんだとよ。<line-break>世界を破壊する究極の怪物のな。　　　<line-break>人間が作ったデスギガースを見て<line-break>怪物を作ることを思いついたらしい。<line-break>その製法を知るために聖杯がいると。<end>"
EN: "That fickle woman says she wants to be a mother―to the ultimate monster that'll destroy the world. Apparently, seeing the Death Gigas humans created gave her the idea of making a monster of her own. She needs the Grail to learn how it's done.<end>"
KO: "그 변덕스러운 여자가 엄마가 되고 싶다더군. 세계를 파괴하는 궁극의 괴물의 말이지. 인간이 만든 데스기가스를 보고 괴물을 만들 생각을 한 모양이야. 그 제조법을 알기 위해 성배가 필요하다고.<end>"

## 620239
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それで、あなたはあのゴブリンを使って<line-break>聖杯を確保しようとしたんですね。<end>"
EN: "So you used those Goblins to secure the Grail.<end>"
KO: "그래서 당신은 그 고블린을 이용해 성배를 확보하려 했군요.<end>"

## 620240
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "一応、俺の管理物だからな。<line-break>仲間とは言え、取られたくはない。<end>"
EN: "It's under my care, after all. Even if she is a comrade, I don't want her taking it.<end>"
KO: "일단 내 관리 물건이니까. 동료라 해도 빼앗기고 싶진 않아.<end>"

## 620241
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "結果的には、<line-break>それがいい方向に働きましたね。<line-break>　<line-break>ロストールがミイス同様に<line-break>焼かれずにすんだんですからね。<line-break>感謝してますよ、ネモ。<end>"
EN: "In the end, it worked out for the best. Rostorl was spared from being burned like Miis. You have my thanks, Nemo.<end>"
KO: "결과적으로는 그게 좋은 방향으로 작용했군요. 로스톨이 미이스처럼 불타지 않고 끝났으니까요. 고맙습니다, 네모.<end>"

## 620242
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あんたの方も<line-break>俺に言うべき事があるだろう？<end>"
EN: "You've got something you ought to tell me too, don't you?<end>"
KO: "너도 나한테 해야 할 말이 있잖아?<end>"

## 620243
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>のことですか？　<line-break>あの子はいい子ですよ。<line-break>　<line-break>竜王が覚醒し、闇の門が開いた。<line-break>勇者ネメアは突如帝都から姿を消し、<line-break>無限のソウルの持ち主が現れた。<end>"
EN: "You mean <value:$28>? That's a good child. The Dragon King has awakened, and the Dark Gate has opened. The Hero Nemea suddenly vanished from the Imperial Capital, and the bearer of the Infinite Soul appeared.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28> 말인가요? 그 아이는 좋은 아이예요. 용왕이 각성하고 어둠의 문이 열렸죠. 용자 네메아는 갑자기 제도에서 모습을 감췄고 무한의 소울을 지닌 자가 나타났습니다.<end>"

## 620244
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "また、大きく<line-break>時代が動き出しそうですねえ。　　　<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "It seems the age is about to undergo another great change.<end>"
KO: "또 크게 시대가 움직이기 시작할 것 같네요.<end>"

## 620245
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あんた、あの子供に聖杯を<line-break>管理させようとしているな？<end>"
EN: "You're planning to put that child in charge of the Grail, aren't you?<end>"
KO: "너, 그 아이에게 성배를 맡기려는 거지?<end>"

## 620246
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ええ、私の手には余りますから。　<line-break>館には破壊神の騎士も<line-break>同居していることですし。<line-break>無限のソウルの持ち主なら<line-break>管理者として申し分ないでしょう？<end>"
EN: "Yes. It is more than I can manage, especially with a knight of the God of Destruction living under the same roof. The bearer of the Infinite Soul would make an ideal custodian, wouldn't you agree?<end>"
KO: "네, 제 손에는 벅차니까요. 저택에는 파괴신의 기사도 함께 살고 있고. 무한의 소울 소유자라면 관리자로 부족함 없겠죠?<end>"

## 620247
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "アーギルシャイアは残虐だ。<line-break>あいつがねらってるのに大丈夫か？<end>"
EN: "Argyleshire is vicious. Are you sure the child will be safe with her after it?<end>"
KO: "아르길샤이어는 잔혹해. 녀석이 노리고 있는데 괜찮겠어?<end>"

## 620248
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "大丈夫ですよ。<line-break>私、信じてますから。<end>"
EN: "It will be all right. I have faith.<end>"
KO: "괜찮아요. 전 믿고 있으니까요.<end>"

## 620249
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "（未使用）<end>"
EN: ""
KO: "(미사용)<end>"

## 620250
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "知恵を付けたゴブリンが<line-break>初めてした自己弁護が自衛のため、　　<line-break>だなんてね。<line-break>知恵を付けたばかりの人間も、<line-break>何でそんなに争ってばかりいるのかって<line-break>神様に聞かれたとき、<end>"
EN: "Imagine that―the first justification the Goblins offered after gaining intelligence was that it was all in self-defense. When humans had only just gained intelligence, and a god asked why they were always fighting...<end>"
KO: "지혜를 얻은 고블린이 처음 한 자기변명이 자위를 위해서라니. 막 지혜를 얻은 인간도 왜 그렇게 싸움만 하냐고 신에게 질문받았을 때,<end>"

## 620251
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "きっと、そう、答えたんでしょうね…。<end>"
EN: "...I suppose that was their answer too.<end>"
KO: "분명 그렇게 대답했겠죠….<end>"

## 620252
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "今日、なんかゴメンね。<line-break>私、あせってたみたい。<line-break>ほんとに言いたかったのは、それだけ。<end>"
EN: "I'm sorry about today. I think I got a little worked up. That's all I really wanted to say.<end>"
KO: "오늘은 왠지 미안해. 내가 좀 조급했던 것 같아. 정말 하고 싶었던 말은 그것뿐이야.<end>"

## 620253
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ねえ。今日はありがと。　　　<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>オルファウスさんに<line-break>手もなくひねられたとき…、<line-break>正直、恐かった。<end>"
EN: "Hey. Thanks for today. When Orphaus won so effortlessly... honestly, I was scared.<end>"
KO: "있지. 오늘은 고마워. 오르파우스 씨에게 손도 못 쓰고 당했을 때… 솔직히 무서웠어.<end>"

## 620254
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あなたみたいなのでも<line-break>いてくれて、<line-break>正直、すごく安心したわ。　　<line-break>うん…あなたの背中見てると、<line-break>すごく安心した…。<end>"
EN: "Even having someone like you with me was honestly a huge relief. Yeah... just looking at your back made me feel so safe.<end>"
KO: "당신 같은 사람이라도 있어 줘서 솔직히 정말 안심했어. 응… 당신 등을 보고 있으면 정말 마음이 놓여….<end>"

## 620255
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ねえ。<line-break>…やっぱり、<line-break>ロストールに戻りたい？<end>"
EN: "Hey... do you still want to go back to Rostorl?<end>"
KO: "있지…. 역시 로스톨로 돌아가고 싶어?<end>"

## 620256
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あ、別に答えにくかったら<line-break>答えなくていいのよ。<end>"
EN: "Oh, if you'd rather not answer, you don't have to.<end>"
KO: "아, 대답하기 곤란하면 굳이 안 해도 돼.<end>"

## 620257
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "何も答えてくれない、か…。<line-break>そっか…、そうだよね。<line-break>フェルムちゃんが…いるもんね…。<end>"
EN: "No answer, huh... I get it. Of course you do. Ferme's there, after all...<end>"
KO: "아무 대답도 안 해 주는구나…. 그렇지… 그렇겠지. 페름이… 있으니까….<end>"

## 620258
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ゴメン。<line-break>怒った？<line-break>なんかしゃべってよ。　　　<line-break>フェルムちゃんとあなたが<line-break>何かあるとかそーゆーことを<line-break>言ってるんじゃないから。<end>"
EN: "Sorry. Are you angry? Say something. I wasn't trying to say there was anything going on between you and Ferme.<end>"
KO: "미안. 화났어? 뭐라도 말해 봐. 페름과 네 사이에 뭐가 있다는 그런 얘기를 하는 건 아니니까.<end>"

## 620259
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ね、私があなたを守ってあげる。<line-break>…だから、聖杯を取り戻しても、<line-break>ずっと一緒に旅しない？<end>"
EN: "Listen, I'll protect you. So even after we get the Grail back... will you keep traveling with me?<end>"
KO: "있잖아, 내가 널 지켜 줄게. …그러니까 성배를 되찾은 뒤에도 계속 같이 여행하지 않을래?<end>"

## 620260
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…寝てるわ。<line-break>…相変わらず子供ねえ。<end>"
EN: "...Asleep. Still such a child.<end>"
KO: "…자고 있네. …여전히 애라니까.<end>"

## 620261
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "でも、聞かれなくてよかった…。　<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>言わずにはいられなかったけど、<line-break>聞かれたら今までと同じじゃ<line-break>いられなかったもの…。<end>"
EN: "But I'm glad you didn't hear... I couldn't stop myself from saying it, but if you'd heard, we couldn't have gone on as before...<end>"
KO: "하지만 묻지 않아서 다행이야…. 말하지 않고는 견딜 수 없었지만, 물었다면 지금까지와 똑같을 수는 없었을 테니까….<end>"

## 620262
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>、<line-break>あなたは、私が、守ってあげる…。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>... I'll protect you.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>, 당신은 내가 지켜 줄게….<end>"

## 620263
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おはよう、<value:$28>。<line-break>今日はもう旅に出るんですか？<end>"
EN: "Good morning, <value:$28>. Are you setting off on your journey already?<end>"
KO: "좋은 아침, <value:$28>. 오늘도 벌써 여행을 떠나는 건가요?<end>"

## 620264
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうですか。　　　　　　　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>でも、覚えていてください。<line-break>これはあなたを巡る長い物語の<line-break>ほんの序章にすぎません。<end>"
EN: "I see. But remember: this is only the prologue to a long story centered on you.<end>"
KO: "그렇군요. 하지만 기억해 주세요. 이건 당신을 둘러싼 긴 이야기의 겨우 서장일 뿐입니다.<end>"

## 620265
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あ、そうだ。これも何かの縁です。<line-break>私もあなたの物語を<line-break>お手伝いさせてもらいましょうか。<line-break>この転送機はあなたの仲間を<line-break>世界中のたいていの所から<line-break>呼び出せるんです。<end>"
EN: "Ah, yes. Since our paths have crossed, perhaps I might lend a hand in your story as well. This teleportation device can summon your companions from almost anywhere in the world.<end>"
KO: "아, 그렇지. 이것도 인연입니다. 저도 당신의 이야기를 도와드릴까요? 이 전송기는 당신의 동료를 세계 대부분의 장소에서 불러낼 수 있습니다.<end>"

## 620266
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "やれやれ、鎧を着たまま<line-break>眠っていたんですか…。　　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>逆に、別れたい仲間を、望む所に<line-break>送り出してやることもできます。<end>"
EN: "Goodness, did you sleep in your armor? It can also send any companions you wish to part with wherever they would like to go.<end>"
KO: "이런, 갑옷을 입은 채로 자고 있었습니까…. 반대로 헤어지고 싶은 동료를 원하는 곳으로 보내 줄 수도 있습니다.<end>"

## 620267
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "もっとも、今後は呼び出す前に　　　　<line-break>相手の方に都合を確認します。<line-break>　<line-break>そして彼らの意志を最優先しますから、<line-break>こんな形で呼び出すことは<line-break>ありませんけどね。<end>"
EN: "Of course, from now on, I'll check whether someone is available before summoning them. Their wishes will come first, so no one will be summoned like this again.<end>"
KO: "물론 앞으로는 부르기 전에 상대 사정을 먼저 확인할 겁니다. 그리고 그들의 의사를 최우선으로 할 테니 이런 식으로 불러내는 일은 없겠지만요.<end>"

## 620268
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "パーティーの編成がしたくなったら　　<line-break>ここに来てください。<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "Come here whenever you wish to change your party.<end>"
KO: "파티 편성을 하고 싶어지면 여기로 와 주세요.<end>"

## 620269
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "くすくす、すみません。<line-break>起こしてしまったようですねえ。<line-break>おはよう、アイリーン。<end>"
EN: "Hee hee, forgive me. It seems I've woken you. Good morning, Irene.<end>"
KO: "후후, 죄송해요. 깨워 버린 모양이네요. 좋은 아침, 아이린.<end>"

## 620270
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お、おはようございます。<end>"
EN: "G-good morning.<end>"
KO: "아, 안녕하세요.<end>"

## 620271
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ああ、昨日のゴブリンたちですが、<line-break>エンシャントに向かったようですよ。<end>"
EN: "Ah, those Goblins from yesterday appear to have gone to Ancient.<end>"
KO: "아, 어제 그 고블린들이라면 엔샨트로 향한 것 같아요.<end>"

## 620272
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "オルファウスさん、<line-break>ありがとうございます。<end>"
EN: "Thank you, Orphaus.<end>"
KO: "오르파우스 씨, 감사합니다.<end>"

## 620273
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ほぉーら、<line-break>ぐずぐずしてないで行くわよ。<end>"
EN: "Come on, no dawdling. We're leaving!<end>"
KO: "자아, 꾸물거리지 말고 가자.<end>"

## 620274
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いい娘じゃないですか。<line-break>いってらっしゃい。<end>"
EN: "She's a good girl, isn't she? Safe travels.<end>"
KO: "좋은 아가씨잖아요. 다녀오세요.<end>"

## 620275
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "聞きたいことがあれば<line-break>答えますよ。<end>"
EN: "If there's anything you'd like to ask, I'll be happy to answer.<end>"
KO: "궁금한 게 있으면 대답해 드리죠.<end>"

## 620276
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "無限のソウルとは<end>"

JP: "アーギルシャイアについて<end>"

## 620281
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml

## 620282
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そんなこと、口にしましたっけ。<line-break>まあ、秘密です。<line-break>　<line-break>ま、おいおいわかりますよ。<line-break>とりあえずはギルドで<line-break>仕事をしていってください。<end>"
EN: "Did I mention that? Well, it's a secret. You'll understand in time. For now, take on some work at the Guild.<end>"
KO: "제가 그런 말을 했던가요. 뭐, 비밀입니다. 차차 알게 될 거예요. 우선 길드에서 일을 해 주세요.<end>"

## 620283
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "他人のために働くことで、　　　<line-break>魂を成長させていけば<line-break>いずれ、わかってきますよ。<end>"
EN: "As you work for the sake of others and nurture your soul, the answer will come to you.<end>"
KO: "다른 사람을 위해 일하며 영혼을 성장시켜 가면 언젠가 알게 될 겁니다.<end>"

## 620284
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "将来、旅の途中で出会った仲間を<line-break>呼び出すための装置です。　　　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>一緒に旅する仲間を<line-break>変更したくなったら<line-break>この猫屋敷に来てください。<end>"
EN: "This device will let you summon companions you meet on your travels. If you ever want to change who travels with you, come to Cat Manor.<end>"
KO: "앞으로 여행 중 만난 동료를 불러내기 위한 장치입니다. 함께 여행할 동료를 바꾸고 싶어지면 이 고양이 저택으로 와 주세요.<end>"

## 620285
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "一緒に旅をしたい仲間を呼び出したり、<line-break>別行動を取ることを決めた仲間を<line-break>送り出したりできますよ。<end>"
EN: "You can summon companions you wish to travel with, or send off those who have decided to go their separate ways.<end>"
KO: "함께 여행하고 싶은 동료를 불러오거나 따로 행동하기로 한 동료를 돌려보낼 수 있어요.<end>"

## 620286
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "破壊神ウルグの復活に<line-break>用いられた強力な魔道器です。　<line-break>１２種類あるといいます。<line-break>禁断の聖杯は<line-break>その中のひとつですね。<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "They are powerful magical artifacts used to resurrect Ulugh, the God of Destruction. There are said to be twelve of them, and the Forbidden Grail is one.<end>"
KO: "파괴신 울그의 부활에 쓰인 강력한 마도기입니다. 12종류가 있다고 하지요. 금단의 성배도 그중 하나입니다.<end>"

## 620287
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "禁断の聖杯については<line-break>今、ゴブゴブ団という<line-break>３匹のゴブリンが持っています。<line-break>とりあえず、現在<line-break>彼らはエンシャントにいますよ。<end>"
EN: "The Forbidden Grail is currently held by three Goblins called the Gob-Gob Gang. For now, they're in Ancient.<end>"
KO: "금단의 성배는 지금 고브고브단이라는 세 고블린이 가지고 있습니다. 현재는 엔샨트에 있어요.<end>"

## 620288
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ここにいるブサイクな猫です。　　　　<line-break>口は悪いけど、無害ですよ。<line-break>　<line-break>もともとは老人星の精霊で<line-break>天空の賢者とも呼ばれていましたが<line-break>地上に堕ちて、魔人になりました。<end>"
EN: "That ugly cat over there. He has a foul mouth, but he's harmless. He was once the spirit of the Old Man Star, known as the Sage of the Heavens, but he fell to earth and became a demon.<end>"
KO: "여기 있는 못생긴 고양이예요. 입은 험하지만 무해해요. 원래는 노인성의 정령으로 천공의 현자라고도 불렸지만 지상으로 떨어져 마인이 되었습니다.<end>"

## 620289
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "破壊神の強力な魔人、<line-break>１２人の円卓騎士のひとりで<line-break>名前を知るものという異名もあります。<end>"
EN: "He is one of the twelve Knights of the Round Table, the God of Destruction's mighty demons, and bears the epithet He Who Knows Names.<end>"
KO: "파괴신의 강력한 마인, 12인의 원탁기사 중 하나로 '이름을 아는 자'라는 이명도 있습니다.<end>"

## 620290
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "円卓騎士には他に<line-break>アーギルシャイアなどがいますね。　　<line-break><end>"
EN: "The other Knights of the Round Table include Argyleshire.<end>"
KO: "원탁기사에는 그 밖에도 아르길샤이어 등이 있습니다.<end>"

## 620291
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "破壊神ウルグの円卓騎士の１人です。<line-break>妖艶な美女の姿をしています。<line-break>　<line-break>きわめて、気まぐれで残酷です。<line-break>すでに神器を手に入れるため<line-break>ミイスの村を焼き払っています。<end>"
EN: "She is one of Ulugh's Knights of the Round Table and takes the form of a bewitching beauty. She is exceptionally capricious and cruel. She has already burned Miis Village to the ground to obtain a divine artifact.<end>"
KO: "파괴신 울그의 원탁기사 중 한 명입니다. 요염한 미녀의 모습을 하고 있습니다. 극도로 변덕스럽고 잔혹합니다. 이미 신기를 얻기 위해 미이스 마을을 불태웠습니다.<end>"

## 620292
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "究極生物を作ろうとして、<line-break>その製法を神器、禁断の聖杯から　　<line-break>得ようと、聖杯をねらっています。<line-break>円卓騎士には他に<line-break>うちのネモなどがいますね。<line-break><end>"
EN: "She seeks the Grail, hoping to learn how to create an Ultimate Life-Form from the divine artifact known as the Forbidden Grail. The other Knights of the Round Table include our own Nemo.<end>"
KO: "궁극 생물을 만들려고, 그 제조법을 신기, 금단의 성배에서 얻기 위해 성배를 노리고 있습니다. 원탁기사에는 그 밖에도 저희 네모 같은 이들이 있죠.<end>"

## 620293

## 620296
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 620297
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ひどいです！　旅に出るなんて、<line-break>何も、教えてくれませんでした！　　　<line-break>　<line-break>そのあとも、何も…<line-break>なんの連絡も、くれませんでした！<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "How could you! You left on a journey without telling me anything! And since then, you haven't sent word... not even once!<end>"
KO: "너무해요! 여행을 떠난다는 말도 아무것도 알려 주지 않았어요! 그 뒤에도 아무것도… 아무 연락도 주지 않았고요!<end>"

## 620298
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "確かに、私、<line-break>力ではアイリーンさんにかないません！<line-break>冒険では足手まといです！<line-break>でも！　でも！<line-break>せめて一言…！<end>"
EN: "I know I can't match Irene's strength! I'd only hold you back on an adventure! But... But! You could at least have said something...!<end>"
KO: "확실히 저는 힘으로는 아이린 씨를 이길 수 없어요! 모험에서는 짐만 돼요! 그래도! 그래도! 적어도 한마디만…!<end>"

KO: "윽.<end>"

## 620300

## 620301

jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 620302
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

KO: "…방금… 키스로… 알았어요.<end>"

## 620303
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "私、信じてますから…。<line-break>また、帰ってきてくださいね。<end>"

KO: "저, 믿고 있으니까요…. 다시 돌아와 주세요.<end>"

## 620304
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

KO: "아…<end>"

source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 620307
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 620308
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

KO: "…저, <value:$28>님을 기다려도 되는 건가요?<end>"

## 620309
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…ちゃんと答えてください。<line-break>私、<value:$28>さんを<line-break>信じていていいんですね？<end>"
EN: "...Please give me a straight answer. I can keep believing in you, can't I, <value:$28>?<end>"
KO: "…제대로 대답해 주세요. 저, <value:$28>님을 믿어도 되는 거죠?<end>"

## 620310
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "また、早く…、<line-break>帰ってきてくださいね…。　<line-break>　<line-break>なんか…恥ずかしくて…、<line-break><value:$28>さんの顔が<line-break>まともに、見られません。<end>"
EN: "Please... come back soon... I'm so embarrassed... I can't even look you in the face, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "또 빨리… 돌아와 주세요…. 왠지… 부끄러워서… <value:$28>님 얼굴을 제대로 못 보겠어요.<end>"

## 620311
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あ、<value:$28>さん！ <line-break>帰ってきてくださったんですね。<line-break>嬉しいです！<line-break>今日もアイリーンさんは<line-break>ご一緒じゃないんですね。<end>"
EN: "Oh, <value:$28>! You're back. I'm so happy! Irene isn't with you again today, is she?<end>"
KO: "아, <value:$28>님! 돌아오셨군요. 기뻐요! 오늘도 아이린 씨는 함께가 아니시네요.<end>"

## 620312
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "アイリーンさんは、あなたまで<line-break>巻き込むことはないと思います。<line-break>　<line-break>ご自分は騎士になるために<line-break>故郷を捨てたんですから、<line-break>帰れないのは仕方ないですけど。<end>"
EN: "I don't think Irene has to drag you into it too. She left her home to become a knight, so it can't be helped that she can't go back.<end>"
KO: "아이린 씨는 당신까지 끌어들이진 않을 거라고 생각해요. 본인은 기사가 되기 위해 고향을 버렸으니 돌아갈 수 없는 건 어쩔 수 없지만요.<end>"

## 620313
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>さんは<line-break>ロストールに帰ってきても　　　<line-break>おかしくないと思います。<end>"
EN: "But there's no reason you can't come back to Rostorl, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>님은 로스톨로 돌아와도 이상하지 않다고 생각해요.<end>"

## 620314
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "アイリーンさんって、力が一番<line-break>って考える人みたいですよね。<line-break>　<line-break>例えば…愛…とか、<line-break>力よりも、ずっと、ずっと<line-break>大切なものがあるのに…。<end>"
EN: "Irene seems to think strength is the most important thing. But there are things... like love... that are far, far more important than strength...<end>"
KO: "아이린 씨는 힘이 제일이라고 생각하는 사람 같죠. 예를 들면… 사랑… 같은, 힘보다 훨씬 훨씬 소중한 것도 있는데….<end>"

## 620315
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あ、…私、<line-break>少し、言いすぎちゃいましたね。　<line-break>　<line-break>…すいません。<line-break>アイリーンさんのこと、<line-break>悪く言う気はなかったんです。<end>"
EN: "Oh... I may have said too much. ...I'm sorry. I didn't mean to speak badly of Irene.<end>"
KO: "아… 제가 조금 심하게 말했네요. …죄송해요. 아이린 씨를 나쁘게 말할 생각은 없었어요.<end>"

## 620316
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ただ、<value:$28>さんと<line-break>会えないとき、<line-break>私、ものすごく不安なんです。　　<line-break>こんなに笑顔でいられるのも<line-break>あなたと一緒のときだけなんです。<line-break>それで、つい…。<end>"
EN: "It's just that when I can't see you, <value:$28>, I get terribly anxious. The only time I can smile like this is when I'm with you. So I just...<end>"
KO: "다만 <value:$28> 씨를 만나지 못할 때는 정말 불안해요. 이렇게 웃을 수 있는 것도 당신과 함께 있을 때뿐이에요. 그래서 그만….<end>"

## 620317
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>さん。<line-break>元気を…くださいますか？<end>"

KO: "<value:$28>님. 제게 힘을… 주시겠어요?<end>"

source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "うっ。<end>"

KO: "윽.<end>"

source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml

KO: "아, 괘, 괜찮아여!<end>"

## 620320
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ありがとうございます…<line-break>これでしばらく笑顔でいられます。<end>"
EN: "Thank you... That'll keep me smiling for a while.<end>"
KO: "감사합니다… 이걸로 한동안은 웃으며 지낼 수 있겠어요.<end>"

## 620321
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

KO: "또… 기운이 없어지기 전에 저한테 기운을 주러 돌아와 주세요.<end>"

## 620323
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あー、<value:$28>さん<line-break>いらっしゃいませー！　　　　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>今、ちょうど、ヒマなんで、<line-break>私が考えた新メニューを<line-break>作っていたところなんです。<end>"
EN: "Oh, <value:$28>! Welcome! Things are slow right now, so I was just trying out a new dish I came up with.<end>"
KO: "아, <value:$28> 씨 어서 오세요! 지금 마침 한가해서 제가 생각한 새 메뉴를 만들고 있었어요.<end>"

## 620324
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "これなんですけど。<line-break>おいしかったら、メニューに<line-break>加えてもらおうかなって思うんです。<line-break>あ、<value:$28>さん、<line-break>よかったら、試食して<line-break>感想とか聞かせてもらえませんか？<end>"
EN: "Here it is. If it's good, I thought I'd ask them to add it to the menu. Would you taste it and tell me what you think, <value:$28>?<end>"
KO: "이거 말인데요. 맛있으면 메뉴에 넣어 볼까 싶어서요. 아, <value:$28> 씨, 괜찮으시면 한번 맛보고 소감 좀 들려주시겠어요?<end>"

## 620325

## 620327

jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 620328
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 620329
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "本当ですか！　うれしいです！<line-break>料理、これが始めてで、<line-break>味見もしてないから心配だったんです。<line-break>あ、まだまだ、<line-break>おかわりありますよ。　<line-break>いかがですか？<end>"
EN: "Really?! I'm so happy! This is my first time cooking, and I didn't taste it myself, so I was worried. Oh, there's plenty more. Would you like another serving?<end>"
KO: "정말요! 기뻐요! 요리는 이게 처음이고 맛도 안 봐서 걱정했어요. 아, 아직 더 있어요. 더 드실래요?<end>"

## 620330

jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

EN: "You're busy...? Well, I suppose it can't be helped.<end>"
KO: "바쁘신가요…. 그럼 어쩔 수 없네요.<end>"

## 620331

KO: "감사했습니다~. 또 들러 주세요~.<end>"

## 620332
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…そ、そんな…、そんな…。<end>"
EN: "...N-no... No...<end>"
KO: "…그, 그럴 수가…, 그럴 수가….<end>"

## 620333
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 620334
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

EN: "Oh, <value:$28>!<end>"

## 620335
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ちょうど、お客さんが<line-break>とぎれて暇だったんです。　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>…来てくださって、うれしいです。<line-break>…もしかして、私に会いに<line-break>来てくださったんですか？<end>"
EN: "Things were slow, so I had nothing to do. ...I'm glad you came. Did you perhaps come to see me?<end>"
KO: "마침 손님이 끊겨서 한가했어요. …와 주셔서 기뻐요. …혹시 저를 만나러 오신 건가요?<end>"

## 620336
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "うん<end>"
EN: "Yes<end>"
KO: "응<end>"

## 620337
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いや別に…<end>"
EN: "Not really...<end>"
KO: "아니, 별건 아니야…<end>"

## 620338
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "うれしいです！<end>"
EN: "I'm so happy!<end>"
KO: "기뻐요!<end>"

## 620339
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうなんですか…。<end>"
EN: "Oh... I see.<end>"
KO: "그런가요….<end>"

## 620340
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あ、<value:$28>さん、<line-break>そういえば、このあいだですね…。<end>"
EN: "Oh, <value:$28>. Speaking of which, the other day...<end>"
KO: "아, <value:$28>님. 그러고 보니 지난번에 말이죠….<end>"

## 620341
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あ、はいー！<line-break>わかりましたー！<end>"
EN: "Oh, yes! I'll be right there!<end>"
KO: "아, 네에! 알겠습니다아!<end>"

## 620342
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "すいません！<line-break>仕事が入っちゃったんです。　　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>もう！　何もこんなタイミングで<line-break>仕事、頼まなくてもいいのに！<line-break>ごめんなさい。また、来てくださいね。<end>"
EN: "I'm sorry! I have to get back to work. Honestly! They didn't have to ask me right now! Sorry. Please come again.<end>"
KO: "죄송해요! 일이 들어와 버렸어요. 정말! 하필 이런 타이밍에 일을 부탁하지 않아도 되잖아! 미안해요. 또 와 주세요.<end>"

## 620343
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あ、<value:$28>さん<line-break>いらっしゃいませー！　　　　　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>来てくださってうれしいです！<line-break>今日はお客さんがいなくて…<line-break>とっても考えこむ時間が多くて…。<end>"
EN: "Oh, <value:$28>! Welcome! I'm so glad you came! There haven't been any customers today, so I've had far too much time to think...<end>"
KO: "아, <value:$28> 씨, 어서 오세요! 와 주셔서 기뻐요! 오늘은 손님이 없어서… 생각에 잠길 시간이 너무 많았거든요….<end>"

## 620344
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ずっと、あなたのことばかり<line-break>考えちゃって、なんかさびしくて…<line-break>だから、来てくださってうれしいです！<line-break>…あのー<line-break>…<value:$28>さん。<line-break>今、お時間、いいですか？<end>"
EN: "I couldn't stop thinking about you, and I got a little lonely... So I'm happy you came! Um... <value:$28>, do you have a moment?<end>"
KO: "계속 당신 생각만 나서 왠지 외로웠어요… 그래서 와 주셔서 기뻐요! …저기… <value:$28> 씨. 지금 시간 괜찮으세요?<end>"

## 620345
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ホントですか？<line-break>じゃ、近くの丘までデートしませんか？<end>"
EN: "Really? Then would you like to go on a date to the nearby hill?<end>"
KO: "정말요? 그럼 근처 언덕까지 데이트하러 갈래요?<end>"

## 620346
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いいんです、仕事なんて！<line-break>さ、行きましょ！<end>"
EN: "Forget work! Come on, let's go!<end>"
KO: "일 같은 건 됐어요! 자, 갑시다!<end>"

## 620347
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フフフ、とっても楽しかったです。<line-break>今日はホントにありがとうございます。<end>"
EN: "Hee hee. That was so much fun. Thank you so much for today.<end>"
KO: "후후후, 정말 즐거웠어요. 오늘 정말 감사합니다.<end>"

## 620348
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ガルドランさんにからまれてたとき、　<line-break>助けてくれましたよね。<line-break>　<line-break>あのとき、ホントうれしかったんです。<line-break>私って、ただの酒場の娘ですから…<line-break>誰も、守ってくれないんですよ。<end>"
EN: "When Girldlan was bothering me, you helped me, remember? I was so happy then. I'm only a tavern girl... No one ever protects me.<end>"
KO: "갈드란 씨에게 시비 걸렸을 때 도와주셨죠. 그때 정말 기뻤어요. 저는 그냥 술집 아가씨라서… 아무도 지켜 주지 않거든요.<end>"

## 620349
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "周りのお客さんって、<line-break>私がいくら困ってたって、<line-break>ああいうとき、ただ笑ってるんです。　<line-break>私、別にお姫様じゃないですから、<line-break>誰も守ってはくれないんです。<line-break>ときどき、泣きたくなっちゃいます。<end>"
EN: "All the customers around me do is laugh at times like that, no matter how much trouble I'm in. I'm no princess, so no one protects me. Sometimes I feel like crying.<end>"
KO: "주변 손님들은 제가 아무리 곤란해도 그럴 때 그냥 웃기만 해요. 전 공주님도 아니니 아무도 지켜주지 않죠. 가끔은 울고 싶어져요.<end>"

## 620350
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "けど、泣いちゃうと、もとの顔に<line-break>戻れないから、笑顔でいるんです。<line-break>しゃべり方もこんなにしてるんです。　<line-break>…だから、あなたが助けてくれたとき、<line-break>ホントにうれしかったんです。<line-break>心と顔とが一緒に笑えたんです。<end>"
EN: "But if I cried, I wouldn't be able to put my usual face back on, so I keep smiling. That's why I talk the way I do, too. ...So when you helped me, I was truly happy. My heart and my face were smiling together.<end>"
KO: "하지만 울어 버리면 원래 얼굴로 돌아갈 수 없으니까 웃는 얼굴로 있는 거예요. 말투도 이렇게 하고 있고요. …그래서 당신이 구해 줬을 때 정말 기뻤어요. 마음과 얼굴이 함께 웃을 수 있었거든요.<end>"

## 620351
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "でも、私、臆病ですから、そのときは　<line-break>控えめに受け取めてたんです。けど、<line-break>あなたはずっと会いに来てくれて…。<line-break><value:$28>さん。<line-break>私にあなたがホントの笑顔と勇気を<line-break>くれたんです。<end>"
EN: "But I'm timid, so at the time I tried not to read too much into it. But you kept coming to see me... <value:$28>, you gave me a true smile and courage.<end>"
KO: "하지만 저는 겁이 많아서 그때는 조심스럽게 받아들였어요. 그런데 당신은 계속 만나러 와 줬고…. <value:$28> 씨. 당신이 제게 진짜 웃음과 용기를 줬어요.<end>"

## 620352
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "これからも、私に<line-break>笑顔と勇気を<end>たくさん分けてくれますか？<end>"
EN: "Will you keep sharing your smiles and courage with me?<end>Lots and lots of them?<end>"
KO: "앞으로도 제게 미소와 용기를<end>많이 나눠 주시겠어요?<end>"

## 620353
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "うっ！<end>"
EN: "Mmph!<end>"
KO: "윽!<end>"

## 620354
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "わらひ、きょふのはひめへの<line-break>デートのこと…わふれまへん！<end>"
EN: "I... I'll neber forget our firsht date today...!<end>"
KO: "저, 오늘 처음 데이트한 거… 잊지 않게써요!<end>"

## 620355
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "やさしいキス…。<end>"
EN: "Such a gentle kiss...<end>"
KO: "부드러운 키스….<end>"

## 620356
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "私、今日の初めての<line-break>デートのこと…忘れません！<end>"
EN: "I'll never forget our first date!<end>"
KO: "저, 오늘 처음 했던 데이트… 잊지 않을게요!<end>"

## 620363
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec062-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あ、いらっしゃいませー。<line-break>ここはロストールで一番大きな酒場<line-break>なんですよ。<line-break>あのー、ご用がありましたら<line-break>何でも私に言いつけてくださいね！<end>"

## 620364

## 630001

jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "うーん、困ったな。<end>"
EN: "Hmm. This is a problem.<end>"

## 630002
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おお、<value:$28>か。<end>"
EN: "Oh, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "오, <value:$28>인가.<end>"

## 630003
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "困ったことになったよ。<line-break>あの張り紙なんだが…。<end>"
EN: "I've got a problem. It's about that notice...<end>"
KO: "곤란하게 됐어. 그 벽보 말인데….<end>"

## 630004
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "わるいきぞくが<line-break>わたしのおにんぎょうを<line-break>とっていってしまいました。<line-break>　<line-break>おばあちゃんのかたみで、<line-break>とてもだいじなものです。<line-break>とりかえしてください。<end>"
EN: "A bad noble took my doll away. It's a keepsake from my grandmother, and it's very precious to me. Please get it back.<end>"
KO: "나쁜 귀족이 제 인형을 가져가 버렸어요. 할머니의 유품이라 정말 소중한 물건이에요. 되찾아 주세요.<end>"

## 630005
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ほうしゅう：３ぎあ<line-break>　　　　　　スラムのハンナ<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "Reward: 3 gia ― Hannah from the Slums<end>"
KO: "보수: 3기아 슬럼의 한나<end>"

## 630006
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "小さい女の子が来て、<line-break>はり紙させてくれって言うから　　　　　<line-break>そこにはらせてあげたんだ。<line-break>しかし、まさか、そんな内容だとは…。<line-break>貴族にケンカ売ってるようなもんだよ。<line-break>困ったなあ。<end>"
EN: "A little girl came in asking if she could put up a notice, so I let her post it over there. I never thought it'd say anything like that... It's practically picking a fight with the nobility. What a mess.<end>"
KO: "어린 여자아이가 와서 벽보를 붙여 달라고 하길래 거기 붙이게 해 줬어. 그런데 그런 내용일 줄은…. 귀족에게 싸움을 거는 거나 다름없잖아. 곤란하네.<end>"

## 630007
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "でも、はがすのは、かわいそうだしなあ。<line-break>俺も貴族は嫌いだし。でもなあ。<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "But I'd feel rotten taking it down. I don't like the nobility either. Still...<end>"
KO: "하지만 떼어 내는 건 불쌍하고. 나도 귀족은 싫어하지만, 그래도 말이지….<end>"

## 630008
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そこの人！<end>"
EN: "You there!<end>"
KO: "거기 당신!<end>"

## 630009
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ボーっとしていたらダメです。<line-break>もうすぐタルテュバが来ます。<line-break>さあ、早くこちらへ！！<end>"
EN: "Don't just stand there! Tartuba will be here any moment. Hurry, this way!<end>"
KO: "멍하니 있으면 안 됩니다. 곧 타르튜바가 옵니다. 자, 빨리 이쪽으로!!<end>"

## 630010
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おお、よかった。<line-break>タルテュバに見つかる前に<line-break>隠れられたようじゃの。<end>"
EN: "Oh, thank goodness. Looks like we managed to hide before Tartuba found us.<end>"
KO: "오오, 다행이구나. 타르튜바에게 들키기 전에숨은 모양이로군.<end>"

## 630011
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "タルテュバというのは悪い貴族です。<line-break>貴族にあまりいい人はいませんが、<line-break>タルテュバはその中でも…最低です。<end>"
EN: "Tartuba is a wicked noble. Not that there are many good nobles, but even among them... Tartuba is the worst.<end>"
KO: "타르튜바는 나쁜 귀족입니다. 귀족 중에 좋은 사람이 별로 없지만 타르튜바는 그중에서도… 최악이에요.<end>"

## 630012
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さあ、クズども、早く出てこい！<line-break>タルテュバ様のお出ましだぞ！　　<line-break>　<line-break>今日はお前らと遊んでやろう。<line-break>たっぷりと死ぬまでなア！<end>"
EN: "Come on, you scum! Get out here! The great Tartuba has arrived! Today, I think I'll play with you until you're good and dead!<end>"
KO: "자, 쓰레기들아, 빨리 나와라! 타르튜바 님께서 나오셨다! 오늘은 너희와 놀아 주지. 실컷 죽을 때까지!<end>"

## 630013
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "来たようです。<end>"
EN: "It sounds like Tartuba's here.<end>"
KO: "온 모양입니다.<end>"

## 630014
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ガラの悪い人を引き連れて、<line-break>目に付いたスラムの人を<line-break>ひとり残らず殴り倒すんです…。<end>"
EN: "Tartuba brings a gang of thugs along and beats down every last person in the Slums who catches Tartuba's eye...<end>"
KO: "험상궂은 사람들을 데리고 다니며 눈에 띄는 슬럼 사람들을 한 명도 남김없이 두들겨 패는 거예요….<end>"

## 630015
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あいつは、　　　　　　　　　　　　<line-break>ムカつくことがあると、<line-break>ここへ人狩りに来るのさ。　<line-break>だから、あいつが<line-break>こっちに向かっているって聞いたら、<line-break>近くの家に隠れるようにしてるのさ。<end>"
EN: "Whenever something gets under Tartuba's skin, this is where the noble comes to hunt people. So when we hear Tartuba is headed this way, we hide in the nearest house.<end>"
KO: "그 녀석은 화나는 일이 있으면 여기로 사람 사냥을 하러 와. 그래서 그 녀석이 이쪽으로 온다는 말을 들으면 근처 집에 숨곤 해.<end>"

## 630016
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なんだお前ら！<line-break>出てこないつもりなのかア！？<end>"
EN: "What's this, you lot? You mean you're not coming out?!<end>"
KO: "뭐냐 너희들! 나올 생각이 없는 거냐!?<end>"

## 630017
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "へっ、当たり前じゃねえか。<line-break>こっちは貧乏人、相手はお貴族様。<line-break>出ていっても、いたぶられるだけだ。<line-break>こうやって<line-break>嵐が通り過ぎるのを<line-break>待つしかないのさ。<end>"
EN: "Hah. Of course not. We're poor, and Tartuba's a high-and-mighty noble. If we go out there, we'll only be tormented. All we can do is stay put and wait for the storm to pass.<end>"
KO: "흥, 당연하잖아. 우리는 가난뱅이, 상대는 귀족 나리야. 나가 봐야 괴롭힘만 당하지. 이렇게 폭풍이 지나가기를 기다릴 수밖에 없어.<end>"

## 630018
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "くっ、クズどもが<line-break>コソコソ隠れやがって…。<line-break>さては、陰で俺を笑ってるんだな！<end>"
EN: "Damn you, hiding away like that... You're laughing at me behind my back, aren't you?!<end>"
KO: "큭, 쓰레기들이 숨어서 꼼지락거리기는…. 설마 뒤에서 날 비웃고 있는 거냐!<end>"

## 630019
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "タルテュバはねえ<line-break>この前、わたしの大切なお人形を<line-break>取っていっちゃったんだよ。<end>"
EN: "Tartuba took my precious doll away the other day.<end>"
KO: "타르튜바가 있잖아, 전에 내 소중한 인형을 가져가 버렸어.<end>"

## 630020
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "へっ、人形ですんでよかったな。<line-break>命を持ってかれなかっただけ<line-break>ましだぜ。<end>"
EN: "Hah. You're lucky it was only a doll. At least you got away with your life.<end>"
KO: "흥, 인형으로 끝나서 다행인 줄 알아. 목숨을 빼앗기지 않은 것만 해도 낫지.<end>"

## 630021
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "くそっ！　どいつもこいつも<line-break>俺のことバカにしやがって！<line-break>なめやがって！　なめやがってエ！<line-break>俺は名家リューガ家の出なんだ。<line-break>お前らとは格が違うんだ！<line-break>それなのに、バカにしやがって…。<end>"
EN: "Damn you! Every last one of you is making a fool of me! How dare you! How dare you! I was born into the illustrious House of Ryuga. I'm far above the likes of you! And still you mock me...<end>"
KO: "젠장! 이놈이고 저놈이고 다 날 바보 취급해! 얕보지 마! 얕보지 말라고오! 난 명문 류가 가문 출신이야. 너희랑 격이 다르다고! 그런데도 날 바보 취급하다니….<end>"

## 630022
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ああ、こんなときに<line-break>ゼネテスさんがいてくれたら…。<line-break>　<line-break>あの人なら、<line-break>すぐにタルテュバを<line-break>追い帰してくれるでしょうに。<end>"
EN: "Oh, if only Xenetes were here... He would send Tartuba packing in no time.<end>"
KO: "아아, 이럴 때 제네테스 씨가 있어 줬다면…. 그 사람이라면 바로 타르튜바를 쫓아냈을 텐데.<end>"

## 630023
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "クズのくせに…クズのくせに…<line-break>クズのくせに…クズのくせに…<line-break>クズのくせに…クズの……………！！<end>"
EN: "Scum like you... scum like you... scum like you... scum like you... scum like you... Scum like...!<end>"
KO: "쓰레기 주제에… 쓰레기 주제에… 쓰레기 주제에… 쓰레기 주제에… 쓰레기 주제에… 쓰레기……………!!<end>"

## 630024
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "殺す！！<line-break>お前ら全員、ブチのめしてやるゥ！<line-break>　<line-break>リベルダムから取り寄せた<line-break>戦闘用モンスターを連れてこい！！<end>"
EN: "I'll kill you! I'll smash every last one of you! Bring me the battle monster I had shipped in from Liberdam!<end>"
KO: "죽여 버리겠다!! 너희 전부 박살 내 주마! 리벨덤에서 들여온 전투용 몬스터를 데려와!!<end>"

source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml

## 630027

## 630028
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "俺に逆らった罰だア！<line-break>こいつで、薄汚いスラムごと<line-break>ぶっつぶしてやる！<end>"
EN: "This is what you get for defying me! I'll use this thing to crush the whole filthy Slums!<end>"
KO: "나한테 거역한 벌이다! 이걸로 더러운 슬럼째 박살 내 주마!<end>"

## 630029
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ギルドの貼り紙を見てくれたの？<line-break>お人形を取り戻してくれるの？<end>"

KO: "길드의 게시물을 보고 왔어? 내 인형을 되찾아줄 거야?<end>"

## 630030
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ちょっとお待ちなさい。<line-break>モンスターと戦う気なの？<line-break>死ににいくようなものよ！<end>"
EN: "Wait! You mean to fight that monster? You'll be throwing your life away!<end>"
KO: "잠깐 기다려. 몬스터와 싸울 생각이야? 죽으러 가는 거나 마찬가지야!<end>"

## 630031
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "やめろ。<line-break>スラムに住んでいる以上、　<line-break>貴族には逆らえない。<line-break>…命が惜しけりゃ、<line-break>そう思ってあきらめるんだ。<end>"
EN: "Don't. As long as we live in the Slums, we can't defy the nobles. If you value your life... accept that and let it go.<end>"
KO: "그만둬. 슬럼에 사는 이상 귀족에게 거역할 수 없어. …목숨이 아깝다면 그렇게 생각하고 포기해.<end>"

## 630032
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "やっと出てきたかア、ゴミめ。　　　<line-break>…だが遅い！<line-break>　<line-break>お前らのせいで、俺の気分は最悪だ！<line-break>せっかく、うさ晴らしに来たのに<line-break>余計に気をめいらせやがって…。<end>"
EN: "So the trash finally crawls out. Too late! You've all put me in a foul mood! I came here to vent my frustrations, and you've only made me feel worse...<end>"
KO: "드디어 나왔나, 쓰레기. …하지만 늦었어! 너희 때문에 기분이 최악이야! 모처럼 화 풀러 왔는데 더 우울해졌잖아….<end>"

## 630033
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

EN: "This is what you get for defying me! Let it devour you! Die! Hya-ha-ha-ha...<end>"
KO: "나한테 거역한 벌이다! 이놈에게 잡아먹혀 죽어라아아! 햐하하하….<end>"

## 630034
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

EN: "Hey, champion of justice. Think you're going to hog all the glory? Let me lend a hand too.<end>"
KO: "여, 정의의 사도님. 좋은 건 혼자 다 가져가려고? 나도 돕게 해 줘.<end>"

## 630035
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml

## 630036
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml

## 630044
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml

## 630045
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "くそっ、覚えていろ！<line-break>いつか痛い目にあわせてやる。　<line-break>　<line-break>タルテュバ＝リューガ様の力を…<line-break>お前がクズだってことを…<line-break>痛いほど思い知らせてやる。<end>"

## 630049
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "し、し、知りません！　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>タルテュバ様が女の子から<line-break>取り上げた人形なんて、<line-break>き、聞いたこともありません。<end>"

## 630050
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ゆ、許してください！<line-break>俺がしゃべったってバレたら、<line-break>殺されちまう。<line-break>タルテュバ様が、人形を<line-break>女にプレゼントしたなんて<line-break>俺には言えません！<end>"

## 630051
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml

## 630053

jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 630054
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "盗んだ人形を王女様にプレゼントとは、<line-break>タルテュバも粋なことをするもんだ。<line-break>　<line-break>だが、やっかいだなぁ、こいつは。<line-break>どうする、<value:$28>？<end>"

## 630055

## 630057
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "手がないこともない。　　　<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>ロストール城の城門近くに<line-break>秘密通路があるって噂を<line-break>聞いたことがある。<end>"
