## 630058
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "その通路は、<line-break>普通に探していたら<line-break>扉さえ見つけられないんだが、<line-break>この銀竜の首飾りを使えば<line-break>見つけられるらしい。<end>"
EN: "You could search all you like and never even find the door, but apparently this Silver Dragon Necklace can reveal it.<end>"
KO: "그 통로는 보통 찾아서는 문조차 발견할 수 없지만, 이 은룡의 목걸이를 쓰면 찾을 수 있다더군.<end>"

## 630059
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "昔は、緊急時の逃げ道に<line-break>使われていたらしいから、<line-break>王族の部屋に続いているかもしれん。<line-break>だまされたと思って、<line-break>この鍵、使ってみるかい？<end>"
EN: "It was apparently used as an escape route in emergencies, so it may lead to the royal chambers. Want to take a chance and try this key?<end>"
KO: "옛날엔 비상시 탈출로로 쓰였다는군. 왕족의 방으로 이어져 있을지도 몰라. 속는 셈 치고 이 열쇠를 써 볼래?<end>"

## 630060
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "大丈夫ですか？<line-break>無理はしないでくださいね。<end>"
EN: "Are you all right? Please don't push yourself.<end>"
KO: "괜찮으세요? 무리하지 마세요.<end>"

## 630061
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "がんばってね！<line-break>でも、危なさそうだったらやめてね。<line-break>お人形なくてもガマンするから。<end>"
EN: "Good luck! But if it looks dangerous, stop, okay? I can go without the doll.<end>"
KO: "힘내! 하지만 위험해 보이면 그만둬. 인형 없어도 참을 테니까.<end>"

## 630062
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ま、なんかあったら<line-break>助けに行ってやるから、<line-break>心配しないで行ってきな。<end>"
EN: "Go on, then. If anything happens, I'll come help you, so don't worry.<end>"
KO: "뭐, 무슨 일 생기면 도와주러 갈 테니까 걱정 말고 다녀와.<end>"

## 630063
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<if><value:$29><equal>%0お兄ちゃん、お兄ちゃん。<line-break>お人形取り返してくれて、ありがとうね。<line-break>お人形もよろこんでたよ！<end>お姉ちゃん、お姉ちゃん。　　<line-break>お人形取り返してくれて、ありがとうね。<line-break>お人形もよろこんでたよ！<end>"
EN: "<if><value:$29><equal>%0Big brother, big brother! Thanks for getting my doll back. My doll was happy too!<end>Big sister, big sister! Thanks for getting my doll back. My doll was happy too!<end>"
KO: "<if><value:$29><equal>%0오빠, 오빠. 인형을 되찾아 줘서 고마워. 인형도 기뻐했어!<end>언니, 언니. 인형을 되찾아 줘서 고마워. 인형도 기뻐했어!<end>"

## 630064
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "タルテュバを追い払ってくれて<line-break>ありがとうございました。<end>"
EN: "Thank you for driving Tartuba away.<end>"
KO: "타르튜바를 쫓아내 주셔서 감사합니다.<end>"

## 630065
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<if><value:$29><equal>%0お兄ちゃん、お兄ちゃん！　　　　　　<line-break>あたしの宝物のお人形ね、<line-break>　<line-break>この間、いじわるな貴族に<line-break>取られちゃったけど、<line-break>ゼネテスが取り返してくれたんだよ。<end>お姉ちゃん、お姉ちゃん！　　　　　　<line-break>あたしの宝物のお人形ね、<line-break>　<line-break>この間、いじわるな貴族に<line-break>取られちゃったけど、<line-break>ゼネテスが取り返してくれたんだよ。<end>"
EN: "<if><value:$29><equal>%0Big brother, big brother! You know my very favorite doll? A mean noble took it away from me the other day, but Xenetes got it back for me.<end>Big sister, big sister! You know my very favorite doll? A mean noble took it away from me the other day, but Xenetes got it back for me.<end>"
KO: "<if><value:$29><equal>%0오빠, 오빠! 내 보물 인형 있지, 전에 심술궂은 귀족한테 빼앗겼는데 제네테스가 되찾아 줬어.<end>언니, 언니! 내 보물 인형 있지, 전에 심술궂은 귀족한테 빼앗겼는데 제네테스가 되찾아 줬어.<end>"

## 630066
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そういえば、ゼネテスのおじちゃん　　<line-break>さいきん来ないなぁ…。<line-break>　<line-break>おじちゃんのお嫁さんに<line-break>なってあげるってやくそくしたのに…。<line-break>女の子を待たせるなんて、ダメね。<end>"
EN: "Come to think of it, Mr. Xenetes hasn't been around lately... I even promised I'd be his bride. He shouldn't keep a girl waiting.<end>"
KO: "그러고 보니 제네테스 아저씨 요즘 안 오네…. 아저씨 신부가 되어 주겠다고 약속했는데…. 여자애를 기다리게 하면 안 되지.<end>"

## 630067
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "タルテュバという貴族の男が、<line-break>ときどき、スラムに現れて暴れるんです。<line-break>　<line-break>わたしたちのことを、<line-break>クズだ、ゴミだと言って…。<line-break>もう、何人も殺されています。<end>"
EN: "A nobleman named Tartuba comes to the Slums from time to time and goes on a rampage. He calls us scum, garbage... He's already killed several of us.<end>"
KO: "타르튜바라는 귀족 남자가 가끔 슬럼에 나타나 난동을 부려요. 우리를 쓰레기라고 부르며…. 벌써 몇 명이나 죽었어요.<end>"

## 630068
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<if><value:$29><equal>%0お兄ちゃん、お兄ちゃん！<line-break>あたしの宝物のお人形ね、　　　　　　　　　<line-break>　<end>お姉ちゃん、お姉ちゃん！<line-break>あたしの宝物のお人形ね、　　　　　　　　　<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "<if><value:$29><equal>%0Big brother, big brother! You know my very favorite doll?<end>Big sister, big sister! You know my very favorite doll?<end>"
KO: "<if><value:$29><equal>%0오빠, 오빠! 내 보물인 인형 말이야,<end>언니, 언니! 내 보물인 인형 말이야,<end>"

## 630069
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "この前、いじわるな貴族に取られちゃったけど<line-break>ノエルっていうお姉ちゃんが<line-break>取り返してくれたんだよ。<line-break>ノエルお姉ちゃんはね、<line-break>ちっちゃいけど強いんだよ。<end>"
EN: "A mean noble took it away from me the other day, but a girl named Noel got it back for me. Noel may be little, but she's strong!<end>"
KO: "전에 심술궂은 귀족에게 빼앗겼는데 노엘이라는 언니가 되찾아 줬어. 노엘 언니는 작지만 강해.<end>"

## 630070
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "タルテュバという貴族の男が、<line-break>ときどき、スラムに現れて暴れるんです。<line-break>　<line-break>わたしたちのことを、<line-break>クズだ、ゴミだと言って…。<line-break>もう、何人も殺されています。<end>"
EN: "A nobleman named Tartuba comes to the Slums from time to time and goes on a rampage. He calls us scum, garbage... He's already killed several of us.<end>"
KO: "타르튜바라는 귀족 남자가 가끔 슬럼에 나타나 난동을 부려요. 우리를 쓰레기라고 부르며…. 벌써 몇 명이나 죽었어요.<end>"

## 630071
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<if><value:$29><equal>%0お兄ちゃん、この前はありがとね。<line-break>タルテュバも<line-break>あれでこりてくれるといいんだけど。<end>お姉ちゃん、この前はありがとね。<line-break>タルテュバも<line-break>あれでこりてくれるといいんだけど。<end>"
EN: "<if><value:$29><equal>%0Big brother, thanks for what you did the other day. I hope Tartuba learned a lesson from it.<end>Big sister, thanks for what you did the other day. I hope Tartuba learned a lesson from it.<end>"
KO: "<if><value:$29><equal>%0오빠, 저번엔 고마웠어. 타르튜바도 이번 일로 정신 차리면 좋겠는데.<end>언니, 저번엔 고마웠어. 타르튜바도 이번 일로 정신 차리면 좋겠는데.<end>"

jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 630074
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "これ、やくそくのお金。<line-break>ほうしゅう…ね。<end>"
EN: "Here's the money I promised. Your reward, I mean.<end>"
KO: "이거, 약속한 돈. 보수…야.<end>"

## 630075
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そいつは受け取るのが礼儀だ。<end>"
EN: "It's only polite to accept it.<end>"
KO: "그건 받아야 예의지.<end>"

## 630076
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>は３ギアを手に入れた。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28> obtained 3 Gea.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>은 3기어를 손에 넣었다.<end>"

## 630077
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さぁて、もう１杯飲んでくるかな。<end>"
EN: "Well, I think I'll go have another drink.<end>"
KO: "자, 한 잔 더 마시러 갈까.<end>"

## 630078
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "でも…。<end>"
EN: "But...<end>"
KO: "하지만….<end>"

## 630079
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…わかった。<end>"
EN: "All right.<end>"
KO: "…알았어.<end>"

## 630080
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<if><value:$29><equal>%0お兄ちゃん、ありがとうね！<end>お姉ちゃん、ありがとうね！<end>"
EN: "<if><value:$29><equal>%0Thanks, mister!<end>Thanks, miss!<end>"
KO: "<if><value:$29><equal>%0오빠, 고마워!<end>언니, 고마워!<end>"

## 630081
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "よくご無事でしたね。<line-break>安心しました。<line-break>ありがとうございました。<end>"
EN: "I'm so glad you're safe. What a relief. Thank you very much.<end>"
KO: "무사하셔서 다행입니다. 안심했어요. 감사합니다.<end>"

## 630082
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "よ、ひさしぶり。<line-break>お前さんもいいとこあるじゃない。<end>"
EN: "Hey, long time no see. You have your moments, don't you?<end>"
KO: "여어, 오랜만이야. 너도 꽤 괜찮은 데가 있네.<end>"

## 630083
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あんた、大丈夫かい。<end>"
EN: "Are you all right?<end>"
KO: "당신, 괜찮아?<end>"

## 630084
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "助けてくれるの？<end>"
EN: "Will you help?<end>"
KO: "도와주는 거야?<end>"

## 630085
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さすがだぜ、ゼネテスさん！<line-break>ありがとうよ。<end>"
EN: "That's Xenetes for you! Thanks.<end>"
KO: "역시 제네테스 씨야! 고마워.<end>"

## 630086
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "礼なんかいいって。<line-break>それより、いつまでもこんなんじゃ<line-break>タルテュバがつけあがるだけだぜ。<line-break>お前らが戦う意志を見せなきゃ<line-break>なんにも変わりゃしねぇ。<line-break>特にお前みたいな若い男がな。<end>"
EN: "Forget the thanks. If things stay like this, Tartuba will only grow more brazen. Nothing will change unless you show you're willing to fight―especially a young man like you.<end>"
KO: "고맙다는 말은 됐어. 그보다 언제까지고 이 모양이면 타르튜바가 더 기고만장해질 뿐이야. 너희가 싸울 의지를 보이지 않으면 아무것도 안 변해. 특히 너 같은 젊은 남자가 말이지.<end>"

## 630087
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…ゼネテスさん、すまない。<end>"
EN: "Xenetes... I'm sorry.<end>"
KO: "…제네테스 씨, 미안하다.<end>"

## 630088
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "貴族だろうが、国王だろうが、<line-break>人を踏みにじっていいやつなんか<line-break>いやしないんだ。<line-break>そのことだけは覚えておけ。<end>"
EN: "Noble or king, no one has the right to trample over others. If you remember nothing else, remember that.<end>"
KO: "귀족이든 국왕이든 사람을 짓밟아도 되는 자는 없어. 그 사실만은 기억해 둬.<end>"

## 630089
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ま、タルテュバも<line-break>しばらくはおとなしくしてるだろ。<end>"
EN: "Well, Tartuba should stay quiet for a while.<end>"
KO: "뭐, 타르튜바도 당분간은 얌전히 있겠지.<end>"

## 630090
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ありがとうございます。<line-break>本当に助かりました。<end>"
EN: "Thank you. You've been a tremendous help.<end>"
KO: "감사합니다. 정말 큰 도움이 됐습니다.<end>"

## 630091
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ゼネテスと同じくらい<line-break>かっこよかったよ。<end>"
EN: "You were every bit as cool as Xenetes.<end>"
KO: "제네테스만큼 멋있었어.<end>"

## 630092
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いつもゼネテスさんに<line-break>助けてもらえるわけじゃない。<line-break>それはわかってるんだが…。<end>"
EN: "Xenetes won't always be around to help. I know that, but...<end>"
KO: "언제나 제네테스 씨가 도와줄 수 있는 건 아니야. 그건 알고 있지만….<end>"

## 630093
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いやー、助かったよ。<line-break>ゼネテスさんも本当にいいところに<line-break>来てくれたよ。<end>"
EN: "Whew, what a relief. You showed up at just the right time too, Xenetes.<end>"
KO: "이야, 살았네. 제네테스 씨도 정말 좋은 때 와 줬어.<end>"

## 630094
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "今回は、<value:$28>がいたから<line-break>よかったが、いつまでもこんなんじゃ<line-break>タルテュバがつけあがるだけだぜ。<line-break>お前らが戦う意志を見せなきゃ、<line-break>なんにも変わりゃしねぇ。、<line-break>特にお前みたいな若いやつがな。<end>"
EN: "We were lucky <value:$28> was here this time. But if things stay like this, Tartuba will only grow more brazen. Nothing will change unless you show you're willing to fight―especially someone young like you.<end>"
KO: "이번엔 <value:$28>가 있어서 다행이었지만 언제까지고 이 모양이면 타르튜바가 더 기고만장할 뿐이야. 너희가 싸울 의지를 보이지 않으면 아무것도 안 변해. 특히 너 같은 젊은이가 말이지.<end>"

## 630106
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml

## 630107
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ちょうどいいところへ来たな。<line-break>俺についてこい。<end>"
EN: "Perfect timing. Come with me.<end>"
KO: "마침 잘 왔다. 나를 따라와.<end>"

## 630108
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ひとつ聞く。俺がお前の意向を少しでも<line-break>尊重すると心の隅ででも思ったのか？<end>"
EN: "One question. Did even the smallest part of you think I would take your wishes into account?<end>"
KO: "하나 묻지. 내가 네 뜻을 조금이라도 존중할 거라고 마음 한구석에서라도 생각했나?<end>"

## 630109
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そ、そうか…。<line-break>と、とにかく、もう決めたのだ。<line-break>俺についてこい。<end>"
EN: "I-is that so...? W-well, anyway, I've already decided. Follow me.<end>"
KO: "그, 그런가… 어, 어쨌든 이미 정했다. 나를 따라와.<end>"

## 630110
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "何を白々しいことを言っている。<line-break>俺がそんな男でないことは<line-break>お前も知っているだろうが。<end>"
EN: "Don't give me that. You know perfectly well I'm not that kind of man.<end>"
KO: "뻔뻔한 소리 하지 마. 내가 그런 남자가 아니라는 건 너도 알잖아.<end>"

## 630111
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "正解だ。<end>"
EN: "Correct.<end>"
KO: "정답이야.<end>"

## 630112
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ティアナ王女の部屋へ顔を出す。<line-break>正体を見破られないよう気をつけろよ。<end>"
EN: "We're stopping by Princess Tiana's room. Be careful she doesn't discover who you really are.<end>"
KO: "티아나 왕녀의 방에 얼굴을 내밀어. 정체를 들키지 않도록 조심해.<end>"

## 630113
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ようこそおいでくださいました。<line-break>レムオン様。<end>"
EN: "Welcome, Lord Lemghon.<end>"
KO: "어서 오십시오. 레무온 님.<end>"

## 630114
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それに、<value:$28>様。<end>"
EN: "And welcome to you as well, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "그리고 <value:$28> 님.<end>"

## 630115
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お顔の色がさえぬな。<line-break>何か不安なことでも？<end>"
EN: "You look rather pale. Is something troubling you?<end>"
KO: "안색이 좋지 않군. 무슨 걱정이라도 있나?<end>"

## 630116
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "レムオン様はティアナのことを<line-break>よくわかってくださるのですね。<line-break>でも、大したことではありません…。<end>"
EN: "You understand me so well, Lord Lemghon. But it's nothing serious...<end>"
KO: "레무온 님은 티아나를 잘 이해해 주시는군요. 하지만 대단한 일은 아니에요….<end>"

## 630117
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "悩みの種はフィアンセ殿だろう？<line-break>また無礼なことを？<end>"
EN: "That fiance of yours is the cause of your worries, isn't he? Has he been rude to you again?<end>"
KO: "고민거리는 약혼자겠지? 또 무례한 짓이라도 했나?<end>"

## 630118
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ち、違います。無礼などは…。<end>"
EN: "N-no, that's not it. He hasn't been rude...<end>"
KO: "아, 아닙니다. 무례라니….<end>"

## 630119
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ほう、では何をやらかしたのだ？<end>"
EN: "Oh? Then what has he done?<end>"
KO: "호오, 그래서 무슨 사고를 친 거냐?<end>"

## 630120
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…もう、いいのです。<line-break>あんな人のことなど<line-break>考えたくもありません。<line-break>そうだわ。<line-break>お菓子を焼いたのです。<line-break>今、お出ししますわ。<end>"
EN: "...Never mind. I don't even want to think about him. Oh, that's right―I baked something sweet. I'll bring it out now.<end>"
KO: "…이제 됐어요. 그런 사람은 생각하고 싶지도 않아요. 맞다. 과자를 구웠어요. 지금 내올게요.<end>"

## 630121
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "悪いがあまり時間がない。<line-break>今日はこれで失礼する。<end>"
EN: "I'm afraid I don't have much time. I'll take my leave for today.<end>"
KO: "미안하지만 시간이 별로 없어. 오늘은 이만 실례하지.<end>"

## 630122
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "まぁ、残念です。<line-break>また、いらっしゃってくださいね。<end>"
EN: "Oh, what a shame. Please come again.<end>"
KO: "아, 아쉽네요. 또 와 주세요.<end>"

## 630123
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>様も。<end>"
EN: "You too, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>님도.<end>"

## 630124
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "俺には礼はしないのか？<end>"
EN: "Don't I get a proper farewell?<end>"
KO: "나한테는 감사 안 하나?<end>"

## 630125
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "礼儀知らずのお方には<line-break>そんな必要はありませんわ。<end>"
EN: "There is no need to show courtesy to someone who has none.<end>"
KO: "예의 없는 분께는 그럴 필요 없습니다.<end>"

## 630126
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "くすっ。<line-break>それでよいのです。<end>"
EN: "Hee hee. That's more like it.<end>"
KO: "후훗. 그러면 됩니다.<end>"

## 630127
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…あの方もこのくらい、<line-break>素直だったらいいのだけれど…。<end>"
EN: "If only he were this honest...<end>"
KO: "…그분도 이 정도로 솔직하면 좋을 텐데….<end>"

## 630128
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…何か？<end>"
EN: "...What is it?<end>"
KO: "…뭔가?<end>"

## 630129
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "い、いえ…<line-break>あの…。<end>"
EN: "N-no... um...<end>"
KO: "아, 아니… 저….<end>"

## 630130
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…王女には何か考えごとのようだ。<line-break>わたしたちは引き上げるとしよう。<end>"
EN: "It seems the princess has something on her mind. We should take our leave.<end>"
KO: "…왕녀님은 뭔가 생각할 일이 있는 모양이다. 우리는 물러나도록 하지.<end>"

## 630131
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…あ…あの、<line-break>…ごめんなさい。<end>"
EN: "I... um... I'm sorry.<end>"
KO: "…저… 그게, …미안해요.<end>"

## 630132
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いつになく、お美しいな。<line-break>ティアナ王女様。<end>"
EN: "You're looking lovelier than usual, Princess Tiana.<end>"
KO: "평소보다 더 아름다우십니다, 티아나 공주님.<end>"

## 630133
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "レムオン様こそ、一段とりりしく。<line-break>もちろん、<value:$28>様も<line-break>見とれてしまいますわ。<end>"
EN: "You look more dashing than ever yourself, Lord Lemghon. And of course, <value:$28> is quite captivating too.<end>"
KO: "레무온 님이야말로 더 늠름해지셨어요. 물론 <value:$28>님도 넋을 잃을 정도예요.<end>"

## 630134
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フフ、あまり弟をおだてるな。<line-break>本気にしてティアナに熱をあげては、<line-break>フィアンセ殿の剣の餌食にされる。<end>"
EN: "Heh, don't flatter my younger brother too much. If he takes it seriously and falls for you, your fiance will put him to the sword.<end>"
KO: "후후, 내 동생을 너무 띄워 주지 마. 진짜로 믿고 티아나에게 빠지면 약혼자의 검에 베일 테니까.<end>"

## 630135
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あの方は、そんなこと<line-break>なさいませんわ！<end>"
EN: "He would never do such a thing!<end>"
KO: "그분은 그런 짓을하실 분이 아니에요!<end>"

## 630136
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…ほう、めずらしいな。<line-break>あの男の肩を持つとは。<end>"
EN: "Oh? How unusual, hearing you defend that man.<end>"
KO: "…호오, 드문 일이군. 그 남자 편을 들다니.<end>"

## 630137
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ち、違います。　　　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>あの人は…、<line-break>私のことになど関心がないと<line-break>言いたかったのです。<end>"
EN: "N-no. I only meant that he... has no interest in me whatsoever.<end>"
KO: "아, 아니에요. 그 사람은… 제게는 아무 관심도 없다고 말하고 싶었어요.<end>"

## 630138
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…ならよいのだが。　　　　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>…い、いや、雌狐の思惑どおり<line-break>事が進むのがおもしろくないのだ。<line-break>そう、ただ、それだけの意味だ。<end>"
EN: "If that's so, fine. N-no, I simply dislike seeing everything go according to that vixen's plans. That is all I meant.<end>"
KO: "…그렇다면 다행이지만. …아, 아니. 여우 같은 여자의 뜻대로 일이 흘러가는 게 마음에 들지 않을 뿐이다. 그래, 단지 그뿐이다.<end>"

## 630139
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そう…しょせんは、<line-break>お母様が決めた婚約です。<line-break>　<line-break>それは、あの人にとっても<line-break>同じこと…。<end>"
EN: "Yes... In the end, it is only an engagement Mother arranged. It is no different for him...<end>"
KO: "그래… 어차피 어머님이 정하신 약혼이에요. 그건 그 사람에게도 마찬가지겠죠….<end>"

## 630140
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ティアナ…。<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>…どうやら、王女は<line-break>お疲れのようだ。<line-break>俺とノーブル伯はこれで失礼する。<end>"
EN: "Tiana... It seems the princess is tired. The Count of Noble and I will take our leave.<end>"
KO: "티아나…. …아무래도 공주님이 피곤하신 모양이군. 나와 노블 백작은 이만 실례하지.<end>"

## 630141
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いえ、そんなことは…。<end>"
EN: "No, I'm not...<end>"
KO: "아뇨, 그런 건….<end>"

## 630142
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "また来る。<line-break><value:$28>、ゆくぞ。<end>"
EN: "I'll come again. <value:$28>, we're leaving.<end>"
KO: "다시 오겠다. <value:$28>, 가자.<end>"

## 630143
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…ごめんなさい。<end>"
EN: "I'm sorry.<end>"
KO: "…미안해요.<end>"

## 630144
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "エリエナイ公爵様、<line-break>一段とりりしくなっておいですね。<line-break>もちろん、<value:$28>様も。<end>"
EN: "You look more dashing than ever, Duke Elienai. And of course, so does <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "엘리에나이 공작님, 한층 더 늠름해지셨군요. 물론 <value:$28>님도요.<end>"

## 630145
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あまり弟をおだてないでくれ。<line-break>本気にしてティアナに熱をあげては、<line-break>フィアンセ殿の剣の餌食にされる。<end>"
EN: "Don't flatter my younger brother too much. If he takes it seriously and falls for Tiana, her fiance will put him to the sword.<end>"
KO: "내 동생을 너무 띄워 주지 마. 진짜로 믿고 티아나에게 빠지면 약혼자의 검에 베일 테니까.<end>"

## 630146
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あの人の話はやめてください。<line-break>せっかくの楽しい時間が台なしです。<end>"
EN: "Please don't speak of that man. You'll spoil the lovely time we're having.<end>"
KO: "그 사람 이야기는 그만해 주세요. 모처럼 즐거운 시간이 망가집니다.<end>"

## 630147
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "結婚をひかえた女性の<line-break>言葉とは思えぬな。<end>"
EN: "Hardly the words of a woman soon to be married.<end>"
KO: "결혼을 앞둔 여성의 말이라고는 생각되지 않는군.<end>"

## 630148
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "誰が結婚などするものですか！<line-break>貴族としての責務も捨てて、<line-break>酒におぼれる男となんて…！<end>"
EN: "Who says I'm going to marry him? A man who has cast aside his duties as a noble and drowned himself in drink...!<end>"
KO: "누가 결혼 같은 걸 한답니까! 귀족의 책임도 버리고 술에 빠져 사는 남자와는 절대로…!<end>"

## 630149
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フフ…<line-break>わかった、わかった。　　　<line-break>　<line-break>さて、そろそろ失礼するか。<line-break>宮中の噂になってはかなわぬからな。<end>"
EN: "Heh... Very well, very well. Now, perhaps it is time I left. I have no desire to become the subject of court gossip.<end>"
KO: "후후… 알았다, 알았어. 자, 슬슬 실례하지. 궁중의 소문거리가 되면 곤란하니까.<end>"

## 630150
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ふふふ。<line-break>もう、噂になっているかも<line-break>しれませんよ。<line-break>レムオン様、<value:$28>様。<line-break>また、いらっしゃってください。<line-break>お待ちしてます。<end>"
EN: "Hee hee. We may already be the subject of gossip. Lord Lemghon, <value:$28>, please come again. I'll be waiting.<end>"
KO: "후후후. 벌써 소문이 났을지도 모르겠네요. 레무온 님, <value:$28>님. 또 와 주세요. 기다리고 있을게요.<end>"

## 630152
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いいえ。レムオン様を待ちわびて<line-break>食事ものどを通りませんでしたわ。<end>"
EN: "No. I longed so desperately to see you, Lord Lemghon, that I could scarcely eat.<end>"
KO: "아니요. 레무온 님을 기다리느라 식사도 목에 넘어가지 않았어요.<end>"

## 630153
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ほう、とてもそうは見えぬが。<line-break><value:$28>、どう思う？<end>"
EN: "Oh? You certainly don't look it. What do you think, <value:$28>?<end>"
KO: "호오, 전혀 그렇게 보이지 않는데. <value:$28>, 어떻게 생각하나?<end>"

## 630154
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おふたりで<line-break>ティアナをからかうのですね。<line-break>　<line-break>これでも、食後のデザートは<line-break>ひかえておりますのに。<end>"
EN: "So the two of you have joined forces to tease me. And here I have even been denying myself dessert after dinner.<end>"
KO: "두 분이서 티아나를 놀리시는군요. 이래 봬도 식후 디저트는 자제하고 있는데요.<end>"

## 630155
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ほぅ、ウェディングドレスの<line-break>仮縫いでも始まるのか？<end>"
EN: "Oh? Have the fittings for your wedding dress begun?<end>"
KO: "호오, 웨딩드레스 가봉이라도 시작하나?<end>"

## 630156
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "また、そのお話？<line-break>レムオン様でも怒りますよ。<end>"
EN: "That again? Keep it up and even you will make me angry, Lord Lemghon.<end>"
KO: "또 그 이야기인가요? 레무온 님이라도 화내실 거예요.<end>"

## 630157
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それはいい。<line-break>ティアナの怒った顔も<line-break>一度は見てみたいものだ。<line-break>ついでに絵描きを呼んで<line-break>肖像画を描かせよう。<line-break>怒った顔をした記念にな。<end>"
EN: "Good. I should like to see Tiana angry at least once. We could summon a painter and have a portrait made to commemorate the occasion.<end>"
KO: "그거 좋군. 티아나의 화난 얼굴도 한 번 보고 싶어. 겸사겸사 화가를 불러 초상화도 그리게 하자. 화난 얼굴을 기념으로 말이지.<end>"

## 630158
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それでは、そのとなりに<line-break>レムオン様が大笑いした顔を<line-break>描かせましょうか？<end>"
EN: "Then shall we have the painter put a portrait of you laughing heartily beside it, Lord Lemghon?<end>"
KO: "그럼 그 옆에 레무온 님이 크게 웃는 얼굴을 그리게 할까요?<end>"

## 630159
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "やれやれ。王妃ではなく<line-break>王女にやり込められるようでは、<line-break>リューガ家に勝ち目はないな。<end>"
EN: "Good grief. If I can be bested by the princess rather than the queen herself, House Ryuga stands no chance.<end>"
KO: "원 참. 왕비도 아니고 공주에게 당할 정도라면 류가 가문에 승산은 없겠군.<end>"

## 630160
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "まぁ、レムオン様が弱音を…。<line-break>めずらしいこと。<end>"
EN: "My, Lord Lemghon showing weakness... How unusual.<end>"
KO: "어머, 레무온 님께서 약한 소리를…. 드문 일이네요.<end>"

## 630161
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "形勢不利のときは退くに限る。<line-break>そろそろ、退散しようか。<end>"
EN: "When the tide turns against you, retreat is the wisest course. Perhaps it is time we withdrew.<end>"
KO: "형세가 불리할 때는 물러나는 게 상책이지. 슬슬 퇴각할까.<end>"

## 630162
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あら、まだこれからですわ。<end>"
EN: "Oh, but we're only getting started.<end>"
KO: "어머, 아직 이제부터랍니다.<end>"

## 630163
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "残念ながら、政務に追われる身だ。<line-break>今日のところはこれで失礼する。<end>"
EN: "Regrettably, affairs of state keep me occupied. I shall take my leave for today.<end>"
KO: "아쉽지만 정무에 쫓기는 몸이다. 오늘은 이만 실례하지.<end>"

## 630164
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "仕方ありませんね。<line-break>また、いらっしゃってください。<line-break><value:$28>様も。<end>"
EN: "Very well. Please come again, and you too, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "어쩔 수 없군요. 또 와 주세요. <value:$28> 님도요.<end>"

## 630165
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ティアナ様、<line-break>今日は一段と輝いておいでだ。<end>"
EN: "Lady Tiana, you look especially radiant today.<end>"
KO: "티아나 님, 오늘은 한층 더 빛나시는군요.<end>"

## 630166
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ふふふ…、レムオン様ったら。<end>"
EN: "Hee hee... Oh, Lord Lemghon.<end>"
KO: "후후후…, 레무온 님도 참.<end>"

## 630167
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>、お前も少しは<line-break>お世辞の言い方を覚えることだ。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>, you should learn a little about how to pay a compliment.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>, 너도 조금은 빈말하는 법을 배워 둬라.<end>"

## 630168
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "うふふ。<line-break>レムオン様のようなお世辞なら<line-break>おっしゃらない方がよろしくてよ。<line-break>その証拠に、<line-break>レムオン様の浮いた噂なんて<line-break>聞いたことがありませんもの。<end>"
EN: "Hee hee. If the compliments are anything like Lord Lemghon's, you would be better off saying nothing. After all, I have never heard a single romantic rumor about Lord Lemghon.<end>"
KO: "우후후. 레무온 님 같은 아첨이라면 안 하시는 편이 좋겠어요. 그 증거로 레무온 님의 염문 같은 건 들어 본 적이 없으니까요.<end>"

## 630169
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "言っておくが<line-break>お世辞は恋愛の道具ではない。<line-break>策略の道具だ。<end>"
EN: "For the record, flattery is not a tool of romance. It is a tool of strategy.<end>"
KO: "말해 두지만 아첨은 연애의 도구가 아니다. 책략의 도구다.<end>"

## 630170
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それではティアナも、<line-break>レムオン様の策略に<line-break>はまっているのですね。<end>"
EN: "Then I, too, have fallen into your strategic trap, Lord Lemghon.<end>"
KO: "그럼 티아나도 레무온 님의 계략에 빠져 있는 거군요.<end>"

## 630171
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "バカな。<line-break>ティアナを利用できるものなら、<line-break>とっくに利用している。<line-break>お望みならば、人質として<line-break>ここから連れ去ろうか？<end>"
EN: "Nonsense. If Tiana could be put to use, I would have done so long ago. If you wish, shall I carry you away from here as my hostage?<end>"
KO: "말도 안 돼. 티아나를 이용할 수 있었다면 벌써 이용했을 거다. 원한다면 인질로 여기서 데려가 줄까?<end>"

## 630172
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "私を人質に？<line-break>レムオン様が？<line-break>ふふふ…できるはずがありませんわ。<line-break>そんな軽はずみなことをしたら<line-break>どうなるか、レムオン様の頭なら<line-break>すぐに弾き出してしまいますもの。<end>"
EN: "Take me hostage? You, Lord Lemghon? Hee hee... You could never do it. A mind like yours would calculate the consequences of such recklessness at once.<end>"
KO: "저를 인질로요? 레무온 님이? 후후후… 그러실 수 없어요. 그런 경솔한 짓을 하면 어떻게 될지 레무온 님의 머리라면 금방 계산해 내실 테니까요.<end>"

## 630173
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "仰せのとおりだ。<line-break>では、そろそろ失礼する。<end>"
EN: "Just as you say. Well then, I shall take my leave.<end>"
KO: "말씀대로입니다. 그럼 슬슬 실례하겠습니다.<end>"

## 630174
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "また、いらっしゃってください。<line-break>楽しかったですわ。<end>"
EN: "Please come again. I had a lovely time.<end>"
KO: "또 와 주세요. 즐거웠어요.<end>"

## 630175
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お前は、まだ王女と<line-break>話し足りないようだったな？<end>"
EN: "You still hadn't had your fill of talking to the princess, had you?<end>"
KO: "너는 아직 왕녀와 이야기가 덜 끝난 것 같던데?<end>"

## 630176
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フッ、無理をしなくてもよい。<line-break>また、引きずって行ってやる。<line-break>楽しみにしておけ。<end>"
EN: "Heh. No need to force yourself. I'll drag you along again. You can look forward to it.<end>"
KO: "흥, 무리하지 않아도 된다. 또 끌고 가 주마. 기대하고 있어라.<end>"

## 630177
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お顔の色がさえぬな。<line-break>何か不安なことでも？<end>"
EN: "You look rather pale. Is something troubling you?<end>"
KO: "안색이 좋지 않군. 무슨 걱정이라도 있나?<end>"

## 630178
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "レムオン様はティアナのことを<line-break>よくわかってくださるのですね。<line-break>でも、大したことではありません…。<end>"
EN: "You understand me so well, Lord Lemghon. But it's nothing serious...<end>"
KO: "레무온 님은 티아나를 잘 이해해 주시는군요. 하지만 대단한 일은 아니에요….<end>"

## 630179
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "悩みの種はフィアンセ殿だろう？<line-break>また無礼なことを？<end>"
EN: "That fiance of yours is the cause of your worries, isn't he? Has he been rude to you again?<end>"
KO: "고민거리는 약혼자겠지? 또 무례한 짓이라도 했나?<end>"

## 630180
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ち、違います。無礼などは…。<end>"
EN: "N-no, that's not it. He hasn't been rude...<end>"
KO: "아, 아닙니다. 무례라니….<end>"

## 630181
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ほう、では何をやらかしたのだ？<end>"
EN: "Oh? Then what has he done?<end>"
KO: "호오, 그래서 무슨 사고를 친 거냐?<end>"

## 630182
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…もう、いいのです。<line-break>あんな人のことなど<line-break>考えたくもありません。<line-break>そうだわ。<line-break>お菓子を焼いたのです。<line-break>今、お出ししますわ。<end>"
EN: "...Never mind. I don't even want to think about him. Oh, that's right―I baked something sweet. I'll bring it out now.<end>"
KO: "…이제 됐어요. 그런 사람은 생각하고 싶지도 않아요. 맞다. 과자를 구웠어요. 지금 내올게요.<end>"

## 630183
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "悪いがあまり時間がない。<line-break>今日はこれで失礼する。<end>"
EN: "I'm afraid I don't have much time. I'll take my leave for today.<end>"
KO: "미안하지만 시간이 별로 없어. 오늘은 이만 실례하지.<end>"

## 630184
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "まぁ、残念です。<line-break>また、いらっしゃってくださいね。<end>"
EN: "Oh, what a shame. Please come again.<end>"
KO: "아, 아쉽네요. 또 와 주세요.<end>"

## 630185
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>様も。<end>"
EN: "You too, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>님도.<end>"

## 630186
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "俺には礼はしないのか？<end>"
EN: "Don't I get a proper farewell?<end>"
KO: "나한테는 감사 안 하나?<end>"

## 630187
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "礼儀知らずのお方には<line-break>そんな必要はありませんわ。<end>"
EN: "There is no need to show courtesy to someone who has none.<end>"
KO: "예의 없는 분께는 그럴 필요 없습니다.<end>"

## 630188
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "くすっ。<line-break>それでよいのです。<end>"
EN: "Hee hee. That's more like it.<end>"
KO: "후훗. 그러면 됩니다.<end>"

## 630189
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…あの方もこのくらい、<line-break>素直だったらいいのだけれど…。<end>"
EN: "If only he were this honest...<end>"
KO: "…그분도 이 정도로 솔직하면 좋을 텐데….<end>"

## 630190
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…何か？<end>"
EN: "...What is it?<end>"
KO: "…뭔가?<end>"

## 630191
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "い、いえ…<line-break>あの…。<end>"
EN: "N-no... um...<end>"
KO: "아, 아니… 저….<end>"

## 630192
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…王女には何か考えごとのようだ。<line-break>わたしたちは引き上げるとしよう。<end>"
EN: "It seems the princess has something on her mind. We should take our leave.<end>"
KO: "…왕녀님은 뭔가 생각할 일이 있는 모양이다. 우리는 물러나도록 하지.<end>"

## 630193
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…あ…あの、<line-break>…ごめんなさい。<end>"
EN: "I... um... I'm sorry.<end>"
KO: "…저… 그게, …미안해요.<end>"

## 630195
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "相変わらずレムオン様はりりしいこと。<line-break>もちろん、<value:$28>様も、<line-break>見とれてしまいます。<end>"
EN: "As dashing as ever, Lord Lemghon. And of course, <value:$28> is quite captivating too.<end>"
KO: "여전히 레무온 님은 늠름하시네요. 물론 <value:$28>님도 넋을 잃고 보시겠죠.<end>"

## 630196
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ドレスでも着せれば<line-break>少しは見栄えもするのだろうが。<end>"
EN: "A dress might make that one look a little more presentable.<end>"
KO: "드레스라도 입히면 조금은 볼 만해지겠지.<end>"

## 630197
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "でも、そのお姿もすてきです。<line-break>足が長いからお似合いですわ。<end>"
EN: "But that attire looks wonderful too. Those long legs carry it beautifully.<end>"
KO: "하지만 그 모습도 멋져요. 다리가 길어서 잘 어울리세요.<end>"

## 630198
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "どうだか…。<end>"
EN: "I doubt it...<end>"
KO: "글쎄….<end>"

## 630199
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ご自慢でいらっしゃるのね。<line-break><value:$28>様のことが。<end>"
EN: "You're very proud of <value:$28>, aren't you?<end>"
KO: "정말 자랑스러우신가 보군요. <value:$28>님이.<end>"

## 630200
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "バカな。<end>"
EN: "Nonsense.<end>"
KO: "말도 안 돼.<end>"

## 630201
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ふふふ。<line-break>兄妹愛は美しいものですわ。<end>"
EN: "Hee hee. The love between brother and sister is a beautiful thing.<end>"
KO: "후후후. 남매애는 아름다운 법이지요.<end>"

## 630202
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フッ、他人の家族愛を評価なされる前に<line-break>まずは、ご自分がエリス王妃と<line-break>仲直りされてはどうだ？<end>"
EN: "Heh. Before passing judgment on another family's affection, why not make peace with Queen Eris?<end>"
KO: "후, 남의 가족애를 평가하시기 전에 우선 본인이 에리스 왕비와 화해해 보는 게 어떻겠소?<end>"

## 630203
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お母様と？<line-break>…別にケンカなどしておりません。<end>"
EN: "With Mother? We aren't quarreling.<end>"
KO: "어머님과요? …딱히 싸운 건 아닙니다.<end>"

## 630204
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ほう。<line-break>では、これで失礼する。<line-break>政務に追われているのでな。<end>"
EN: "Oh? Then I shall take my leave. Affairs of state demand my attention.<end>"
KO: "호오. 그럼 이만 실례하지. 정무에 쫓기고 있어서 말이야.<end>"

## 630205
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "また、いらっしゃってくださいね。<end>"
EN: "Please come again.<end>"
KO: "또 와 주세요.<end>"

## 630207
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "うれしいですわ。<line-break>レムオン様にお会いできて。<end>"
EN: "I'm so happy to see you, Lord Lemghon.<end>"
KO: "기뻐요. 레무온 님을 뵐 수 있어서.<end>"

## 630208
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "言葉をつつしまれよ。<line-break>フィアンセ殿の耳に入っては事だ。<end>"
EN: "Mind your words. There will be trouble if your fiance hears of this.<end>"
KO: "말을 삼가시오. 약혼자의 귀에 들어가면 큰일이오.<end>"

## 630209
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あの人の話はやめてください。<line-break>せっかくの楽しい時間が台なしです。<end>"
EN: "Please don't speak of that man. You'll spoil the lovely time we're having.<end>"
KO: "그 사람 이야기는 그만해 주세요. 모처럼 즐거운 시간이 망가집니다.<end>"

## 630210
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "結婚をひかえた女性の<line-break>言葉とは思えぬな。<end>"
EN: "Hardly the words of a woman soon to be married.<end>"
KO: "결혼을 앞둔 여성의 말이라고는 생각되지 않는군.<end>"

## 630211
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "誰が結婚などするものですか！<end>"
EN: "Who says I'm going to marry him?<end>"
KO: "누가 결혼 같은 걸 한답니까!<end>"

## 630212
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ムキになるな。からかっただけだ。<line-break>だが、そろそろ失礼しよう。<line-break>宮中の噂になってかなわぬからな。<end>"
EN: "Don't get so worked up. I was only teasing. Still, it is time I left. I have no desire to become the subject of court gossip.<end>"
KO: "발끈하지 마라. 놀렸을 뿐이다. 하지만 슬슬 실례하지. 궁중 소문거리가 되면 곤란하니까.<end>"

## 630213
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ふふふ。大丈夫です。<line-break>おきれいな妹君がご一緒ですもの。<end>"
EN: "Hee hee. There is no need to worry. Your beautiful sister is with you.<end>"
KO: "후후후. 괜찮습니다. 아름다운 여동생분이 함께 계시니까요.<end>"

## 630214
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "きれいな妹君…か。<end>"
EN: "A beautiful younger sister... is that what you think?<end>"
KO: "예쁜 여동생이라…<end>"

## 630215
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "また、いらっしゃってください。<line-break>お待ちしてます。<end>"

KO: "또 와 주세요. 기다리고 있을게요.<end>"

## 630217
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いいえ。レムオン様を待ちわびて<line-break>食事ものどを通りませんでしたわ。<end>"
EN: "No. I longed so desperately to see you, Lord Lemghon, that I could scarcely eat.<end>"
KO: "아니요. 레무온 님을 기다리느라 식사도 목에 넘어가지 않았어요.<end>"

## 630218
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そういう冗談は、妹の前では<line-break>ひかえてもらいたい。<line-break>　<line-break>こいつは単純なのだ。<line-break>なんでも信じ込んでしまう。<end>"
EN: "I would rather you did not make such jokes in front of my sister. This one is terribly naive and believes everything.<end>"
KO: "그런 농담은 여동생 앞에서는 삼가 주길 바란다. 이 녀석은 단순해서 뭐든 곧이곧대로 믿어 버려.<end>"

## 630219
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ふふふ。存じませんでしたわ。<line-break><value:$28>様の前では、<line-break>いいお兄様でいらっしゃるのですね？<end>"
EN: "Hee hee. I never knew. You are such a good brother when <value:$28> is present, aren't you?<end>"
KO: "후후후. 몰랐어요. <value:$28>님 앞에서는 좋은 오라버니이신가 보네요?<end>"

## 630220
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "俺のことより、フィアンセ殿とは<line-break>うまくいっているか？<end>"
EN: "Enough about me. Are things going well with your fiance?<end>"
KO: "내 얘기보다 약혼자와는 잘 지내고 있나?<end>"

## 630221
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フィアンセだなんて、やめてください。<line-break>あの人のことを考えただけで、<line-break>気分が悪くなります。<line-break>レムオン様、意地悪ですわ。<line-break><value:$28>様には<line-break>おやさしいのに。<end>"
EN: "Please don't call him my fiance. Just thinking of that man makes me ill. You're so mean, Lord Lemghon, when you're so kind to <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "약혼자라니, 그만하세요. 그 사람 생각만 해도 기분이 나빠져요. 레무온 님, 심술궂으세요. <value:$28> 님에게는 다정하시면서.<end>"

## 630222
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "やさしい？　俺が？<line-break>ふっ、バカな。　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>そろそろ失礼する。<line-break>政務に追われる身ゆえ。<end>"
EN: "Kind? Me? Heh, nonsense. It is time I left. Affairs of state keep me occupied.<end>"

## 630223
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "もうお帰りですの？<line-break>残念ですわ。<end>"
EN: "You're leaving already? What a shame.<end>"
KO: "벌써 돌아가시나요? 아쉽네요.<end>"

## 630224
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おふたりが並んでいらっしゃると、<line-break>とても兄妹には見えませんね。<line-break>　<line-break>まるで友達…<line-break>いえ、恋人同士のようだわ。<end>"
EN: "When I see the two of you together, you hardly look like brother and sister. You seem more like friends... No, more like lovers.<end>"
KO: "두 분이 나란히 계시면 정말 남매로는 안 보이네요. 마치 친구… 아니, 연인 같아요.<end>"

## 630225
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フッ、何を言い出すかと思えば。<line-break>宮中のご婦人方が喜びそうな話だ。<end>"
EN: "Heh. And here I wondered what you were about to say. The ladies at court would delight in such a tale.<end>"
KO: "훗, 무슨 말을 꺼내나 했더니. 궁중 부인들이 좋아할 만한 이야기군.<end>"

## 630227
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "くだらん。誰が実の妹と…。<end>"
EN: "Absurd. Who would do such a thing with his own sister...<end>"
KO: "시시하군. 누가 친여동생과….<end>"

## 630228
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "政権を取った暁には、<line-break>くだらぬ噂を取り締まる法律でも<line-break>作るとするか。<end>"
EN: "Once I take power, perhaps I shall pass a law against such ridiculous rumors.<end>"
KO: "정권을 잡게 되면 쓸데없는 소문을 단속하는 법이라도 만들까.<end>"

## 630230
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そう。夫婦そろって仲よくな。<end>"
EN: "Yes. Happily together as husband and wife.<end>"
KO: "그래. 부부가 함께 사이좋게 지내라.<end>"

## 630231
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

KO: "누가 그런 남자랑! 그런 짓을 하면 진심으로 저주할 거예요!<end>"

## 630232
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 630233

## 630234

## 630235

## 630236

## 630237
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 630238

## 630239

## 630240

## 630241
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 630242

## 630253

## 630254

jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 630255
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そういうことを言うと、<line-break>また、王宮のヒマ人どもの<line-break>噂の種になるぞ、ティアナ。　　　　<line-break>そんな噂が、フィアンセ殿に知れては<line-break>大事だ。奴の剣にかかって<line-break>死ぬのだけは遠慮したい。<end>"

KO: "그런 말을 하면 또 왕궁의 한가한 녀석들한테 소문거리가 된다, 티아나. 그런 소문이 약혼자 귀에 들어가면 큰일이지. 그 녀석 검에 맞아 죽는 것만은 사양하고 싶어.<end>"

## 630256
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml

KO: "그 사람은 그런 짓을하실 분이 아니에요!<end>"

## 630257
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 630258
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 630259
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 630260

jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 630261

KO: "에리에나이 공작님이야말로 늠름해지셨어요. 넋을 잃을 것 같아요.<end>"

## 630268
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あの人の話なら、やめてください。<line-break>せっかくの楽しい時間が台なしです。<end>"
EN: "If you're going to speak of that man, please stop. You'll spoil the lovely time we're having.<end>"
KO: "그 사람 이야기라면 그만해 주세요. 모처럼 즐거운 시간이 망가집니다.<end>"

## 630269
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それが、結婚をひかえた女性の<line-break>お言葉とはね。<end>"
EN: "And those are the words of a woman soon to be married?<end>"
KO: "그게 결혼을 앞둔 여성의 말씀인가요.<end>"

## 630270
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "誰が結婚などするものですか！<end>"
EN: "Who says I'm going to marry him?<end>"
KO: "누가 결혼 같은 걸 한답니까!<end>"

## 630271
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ムキになるな。<line-break>からかっただけだ。<line-break>　<line-break>さて、そろそろ失礼するか。<line-break>宮中の噂になってはかなわぬからな。<end>"
EN: "Don't get so worked up. I was only teasing. Now, perhaps it is time I left. I have no desire to become the subject of court gossip.<end>"
KO: "발끈하지 마. 놀린 것뿐이야. 자, 슬슬 실례하지. 궁중의 소문거리가 되면 곤란하니까.<end>"

## 630272
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ふふふ。大丈夫ですわ。<line-break>きれいな妹君がご一緒ですもの。<end>"
EN: "Hee hee. There is no need to worry. Your beautiful sister is with you.<end>"
KO: "후후후. 괜찮아요. 예쁜 여동생분이 함께 계시니까요.<end>"

## 630273
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec063-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "また、いらっしゃってください。<line-break>お待ちしてます。<end>"
